150. neytal
[pakaṟkuṟi vantukaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkum talimakaṉit tōḻi talimakaḷi iṭattuyttu vantu,
ceṟippaṟivuṟīi varivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 9-14. tār ārmārpa - māli poruntiya mārpaṉē,nī taṇanta ñāṉṟu - nī
pirintakāli, kaṭum celalvāḷ cuṟā vaḻaṅkum - virinta celaviṉiyum vāḷpōṉṟa
kompiṉiyumuṭiya cuṟā mīṉ iyaṅkum,vaḷimēy perumtuṟi - caṅkukaḷ mēyum
periyatuṟiyiyuṭiya, kaṉitta neytal kaṇpōl māmalar -itaḻ ceṟinta neytalatu kaṇpōlum
periya malar,māli maṇi itaḻ kūmpa - mālip pōtil aḻakiyitaḻkaḷ kuviya; kāli -
viṭiyalil, naṉittaceruntip pōtuvāy aviḻa - arumpiyaceruntiyiṉ pōtukaḷ itaḻa
viriya, kaḷ nāṟukāviyoṭu - tēṉ nāṟukiṉṟa kuvaḷiyuṭaṉ, taṇ eṉamalarum - taṭpam uṟa
malarukiṉṟa, kaḻiyum kāṉalumkāṇtoṟum - kaḻiyiyum cōliyiṉiyum kāṇuntōṟum, pala
pulantu -palavum eṇṇi veṟuttu;vārārkol eṉap paruvarum nam talivar vārārōeṉat talivi
varuntuvaḷ, ataṉmēlum;
1-9. yāy -aṉṉiyāṉavaḷ, piṉṉuviṭa neṟitta kūntalum -piṉṉumpaṭi (vaḷarntu) neḷinta
kūntaliyum,poṉ eṉa ākattu arumpiya cuṇaṅkum - poṉ pōla mārpilē tōṉṟiya
tēmaliyum, vampu viṭak kaṇ uruttu eḻutarummuliyum - kaccu aṟak kaṇṇuṭaṉ uruppeṟṟu
eḻuntamuliyiṉiyum, nōkki - pārttu, peritu elliṉieṉa - peritum aḻakuṭiyi
eṉṟu, pal māṇ kūṟi - palamuṟiyum kūṟi, perum tōḷ aṭiya muyaṅki - periyatōḷi
muḻuvatum muyaṅki, nīṭu niṉintu -neṭituniṉintu, aruṅkaṭippaṭuttaṉaḷ - (taliviyi)
ariya kāvalil vittaṉaḷ.
(muṭipu) tārār mārpa! talivi, nī taṇanta ñāṉṟu kaḻiyuṅkāṉaluṅ kāṇṭoṟum vārārkol
leṉapparuvarum; (ataṉ mēluma) yāy kūntalum cuṇaṅkummuliyum nōkki, elliṉi eṉak
kūṟi, muyaṅkiaruṅ kaṭippaṭuttaṉaḷ.
(vi - ri.) uruttu - vekuṇṭu eṉṟumām. vāṭcuṟā vaḻaṅkumkaḻiyeṉavum,
peruntuṟiyiyuṭiya kaḻiyeṉavumiyiyum. ceruntiyum neytaluṅ kāviyumkāliyil
malarum eṉka. kaḻiyuṅkāṉalumiyaṟkippuṇarccik kālattut talivaṉ cūḷuṟṟiṭamākaliṉ
avaṟṟik kāṇṭoṟum veṟuttāḷeṉṟalumām.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'kaḷavalarāyiṉum'eṉṉum (cevili kūṟṟu nikaḻumāṟu kūṟum)
cūttirattu,aḷavu mikat tōṉṟiṉum eṉṉum tuṟikkaṇ, icceyyuḷ tōḻi cevili
kūṟiyatik koṇṭu kūṟiyatueṉṟu kūṟiṉar; nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
2'iṉpattiveṟuttal' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu 'ētam āytal'eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiṟku,
kūṭṭattiṟku varumiṭiyūṟu uṇṭeṉṟu palavum ārāytal. atu, notumalarvariyak karutuvar
kol leṉavum, pirintōrmaṟantu iṉi vārārkol leṉavum tōṉṟum uḷḷanikaḻcci eṉṟu
urittu, atu, 'vārār kolleṉap paruvarum, tārār mārpa nī taṇanta ñāṉṟē'eṉa varum
eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, pērā.

151. pāli
[talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇvēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 5-15. tōḻi - vāḻi-, kāl viripu uṟuvaḷi eṟitoṟum- iṭamelām
virintu mikka kāṟṟu vīcuntoṟum,kalaṅkiya - kalaṅkiyatum, poṟivarik kalimāṉtaliyil -
puḷḷikaḷiyum varikaḷiyumuṭiyakalimāṉiṉ taliyiṉ, mutaṉmutal kavartta -aṭiyiṭattiṟ
kappuviṭṭa; kōṭu kavaṭṭa kuṟuṅkāluḻiñcil - kompu pōṉṟa kavaṭṭiṉiyuṭiya
kuṟiyakāliṉiyuṭiya vākimarattiṉ, ciṉi viḷinta -kiḷiyil viḷintatum ākiya,
tāṟuneṟṟam -neṟṟiṉ kuli, āṭumakaḷ arikōl paṟiyiṉ - āṭumkūttiyaratu kōlāl
aritteḻum oliyuṇṭākkum paṟipōla, aiyeṉa olikkum - viyappuṇṭākolikkum,
patukkittu āya cetukki nīḻal -patukki yiṉiyuṭiyatāya kuṟinta niḻaliyuṭiya,kaḷḷimuḷ
ariporunti – kaḷḷiyiṉ muṭporuntiya aṭiyil taṅki, cellunarkkuuṟuvatu kūṟum -
vaḻic celvārkku uṇṭākumnikaḻccikaḷik kūṟum, ciṟu cennāviṉ - ciṟiyacevviya
naraviṉatu, maṇi ōrttaṉṉa - maṇiyolikēṭṭāṟ pōlum, teṇ kural - teḷintakuralikkoṇṭa,
kaṇivāyp palliya kāṭuiṟantōr - cōtiṭam kūṟum vāyiyuṭiyapallikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭik
kaṭantu ceṉṟa namtalivar;
1-5. tam nayantuuṟivōrt tāṅki - tammi virumpiyiruppōrip purantu, tām nayantiṉ
amarkēḷiroṭu ēm uṟakkeḻīi - tām virumpiyiṉimi poruntiya naṭṭāroṭu iṉpam
mikiyintu, nakutal āṟṟār nalkūrntōr eṉa -makiḻntiruttali māṭṭārāvar vaṟumi
yuṟṟōreṉṟu kūṟi, mikuporuḷ niṉiyum neñcamoṭu -mikka poruḷi īṭṭa niṉiyum
neñcattāl, maṉaruḷ piṟituāpa - nampāl mikavum aruḷuṭiyavarallar āvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! kāṭiṟantōr, tāṅki, keḻīi.nakutal āṟṟār eṉap poruḷ
niṉiyumneñcamoṭu aruḷ piṟitāya: eṉceyvām.
(vi - ri.) tamnayan tuṟivōr - tam kuṭikkaṇ iḷiyārumpaṇiyāḷarum pāṇar
mutalāyiṉārumām. tām nayanteṉṉum peyareccattu akaram tokkatu. kēḷir -naṭṭār.
neñcamoṭu - neñcattāl. poruḷiyēkātalippavar aruḷik kiviṭuvar eṉṉumkaruttiṉi,
1'poruḷēkātalar kātal, aruḷē kātalar eṉṟi nīyē'eṉṟataṉāṉ aṟika. kāl viripu
eṉpuḻik kāliṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu. kalaṅkiya neṟṟameṉavum,ciṉiviḷinta neṟṟam eṉavum
taṉittaṉi iyiyum.mutal iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu aṭi, piṉṉatu iṭam eṉṉumporuḷaṉa:
kōṭalaṅkavaṭṭa; allum ammumcāriyikaḷ. neṟṟam: am, aci. pakutipporuḷvikuti
eṉṟalumām. cennāviṉ kural eṉavummaṇiyōrttaṉṉa kural eṉavum iyiyum. uṟuvatu
kūṟumciṟu cennāviṉ eṉṟamiyāl, kaṇivāy eṉṟatupallikku iyaṟki yaṭi.

152. kuṟiñci
[iravukkuṟivantu nīṅkuntalimakaṉtaṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) neñcē.
1-3. neñcu naṭuṅkuarumpaṭar tīra vantu - nam neñcamnaṭuṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa
piṟitoṉṟāltīrtaṟkariya tuṉpamāṉatu tīra vantu kūṭi, kuṉṟu uḻinaṇṇiya cīṟūr āṅkaṇ celī
iya peyarvōḷ -kuṉṟiṭattup poruntiya taṉatu ciṟiya ūrkkuccella mīḷvōḷāya nam
taliviyiṉ, vaṇar curiaimpāl - vaḷintu curuṇṭa kūntalkaḷ;
4-14. nuṇ kōlakavunarp puranta pērici - nuṇṇiya kōliyuṭiyapāṇarip puranta periya
pukaḻiyum, ciṉam keḻu tāṉi- ciṉam mikka paṭiyiṉiyumuṭiya, tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ- tittaṉ
veḷiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, iraṅkum nīrpparappiṉ kāṉal am peruntuṟi -
olikkumnīrpparappiṉiyuṭiya kāṉalam peruntuṟi eṉṉumpaṭṭiṉattē, taṉam tarunaṉkalam
citiyattākkum -poṉṉik koṇṭuvarum nalla marakkalamcitiyumāṟu tākkukiṉṟa, ciṟu veḷ
iṟaviṉ kuppiaṉṉa - ciṟiya veḷḷiya iṟāmīṉiṉ tokutipōṉṟa, uṟupaki tarūum moymūcu
piṇṭaṉ - mikkapakiyit tarum valimikka piṇṭaṉ eṉpāṉatu, muṉimuraṇ uṭiyak kaṭanta
veṉvēl - pōrceyyummāṟupāṭu citiya veṉṟa veṟṟi vēliyum, icinalīkik kaḷiṟu vīcu
vaṇmakiḻ - pukaḻ mēviya nallīkiyiṉiyum kaḷiṟukaḷi vaḻaṅkum vaṇmiyāṉākiyakaḷippiṉiyum
uṭiya, pārattut talivaṉ āramnaṉṉaṉ - pāram eṉṉum ūrkkut talivaṉākiyārampūṇṭa naṉṉaṉ
eṉpāṉatu, ēḻil neṭuvarippāḻiccilampil - ēḻil eṉṉum nīṇṭamaliyiṉ pāḻi yeṉṉum
pakkamaliyiṭattuḷḷa,kaḷimayil kalāvattu aṉṉa - kaḷikkum mayiliṉtōkiyi ottaṉa:
14-24. tōḷē - ivaḷtōḷkaḷ, valvil iḷiyar perumakaṉ naḷḷi -valiya villiyuṭiya
vīrarkaṭkut talivaṉāṉa naḷḷi eṉpāṉatu, cōli aṭukkattu -cōlikaḷ mikka pakka maliyiṭattē
curumpu uṇavirinta kaṭavuḷ kāntaḷ uḷḷum - vaṇṭu uṇṇavirinta kaṭavuḷ cūṭutaṟkuriya
kāntaṭ pūvuḷḷum,iṟumpūtu kañaliya āymalar pala uṭaṉ nāṟi, viyappumikka aḻakiya malar
palavum oruṅku nāṟuvatu pōlanāṟivalliṉum vallārāyiṉum ceṉṟōrkku -paricil karuti
vantārkku avar kalviyilvallunarāyiṉum allārāyiṉum, cāl aviḻ -miṭāc cōṟṟi,
neṭuṅkuḻi niṟiya vīcum -maṇṭiyiṉ neṭiya kuḻi niṟiyumpaṭiyāka aḷikkum,māal yāṉi
āay kāṉattu - periya yāṉikaḷiyuṭiyāy eṉpāṉatu kāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya, taliyāṟṟu
niliiyacēy uyar piṟaṅkal - taliyāṟu eṉṉumiṭattunilipeṟṟa mika uyarnta maliyiṭattuḷḷa,
vēyamikaṇ iṭipurii - mūṅkiliṟ poruntiyakaṇukkaḷiṉ naṭu viṭatti ottu, cēya
āyiṉum naṭuṅkutuyar tarum - cēymikkaṇ uḷḷaṉavāyiṉum nāmnaṭuṅkattakka tuyariṉit
tarum.
(muṭipu) neñcē! cīṟūrāṅkaṭ celīiya peyarvōḷ aimpāl naṉṉaṉ pāḻic cilampiṉ
mayiṟkalāvattaṉṉa; tōḷ naḷḷi aṭukkattu āymalarnāṟi, āay kāṉattup piṟaṅkal
vēyamikkaṇṇiṭi purii, naṭuṅkutuyar tarum
tittaṉveḷiyaṉ peruntuṟi naṉkalam citiyat tākkumiṟaviṉ kuppiyaṉṉa uṟupaki tarūum
piṇṭaṉatu muraṇuṭiyakkaṭanta naṉṉaṉ eṉka.
(vi - ri.) naṭuṅkutuyar tarumē eṉpataṉi muṉṉuṅ kūṭṭi aimpālkalāvattaṉṉavāy
naṭuṅkutuyar tarum eṉṟuurittuk koḷka. cāl - miṭā. cālaviḻ niṟiyavīcum eṉavum,
neṭuṅkuḻi māal yāṉiyeṉavuṅkūṭṭiyurittalumām; kuḻi - īṇṭu yāṉiyiakappaṭuttum
payampu. yāṉiyiyuṭiya āay eṉka;āay kāṉam eṉṟalumām. eṉṉa? 1'...aṇṭiraṉ,kuṉṟam
pāṭiṉa kollō, kaḷiṟumika vuṭiya ikkaviṉpeṟu kāṭē' eṉpavākaliṉ.

153. pāli
[makaṭ pōkkiyacevilittāy coṟṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 2-4. aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷāya eṉ makaḷ,amtīm kiḷavi āyamoṭu keḻīi -
aḻakiya iṉiyacoṟkaḷiyuṭiya āyattāruṭaṉ porunti,pantuvaḻip paṭarkuvaḷ āyiṉum - pantāṭu
miṭattucciṟitu ceṉṟu varuvāḷāyiṉum, naṉi nontu vempummaṉ - mikavum nontu
peritum varuntuvāḷ;
13-9. ōṅku variaṭukkattu - uyarnta malic cāraliluḷḷa, tēmpāyntu ārkkum teri
iṇark kōṅkiṉ -vaṇṭukaḷ pāyntu āravārikkum viḷakkamuṟṟakottukkaḷiyuṭiya kōṅkiṉatu,
uyarnta ceṉṉi -uyarnta ucciyil, elli - iraviṉkaṇ, mīṉoṭupolinta vāṉil tōṉṟi -
mīṉoṭu viḷaṅkumvāṉeṉat tōṉṟip (piṉ) , kāl uṟak kaḻaṉṟakaṇ kamaḻ putu malar - kāṟṟu
aṭittaliṉ kaḻaṉṟuviḻum iṭaṉellām kamaḻum putiya malar, kiviṭucuṭaril tōṉṟum -
kiyiṉāl viṭappeṟumcuṭarppoṟi pōlat tōṉṟum , mi paṭu māmalivilaṅkiya curaṉ - mēkam
poruntiya periya malikuṟukkiṭṭa curaneṟiyil:
4-13. vēṉil teṟukatir uliiya - vēṉiṟkālattuk kāykiṉṟañāyiṟṟiṉ katir keṭutta,
vem kāṭṭu -veppam mikka kāṭṭilē, uṟu vaḷi - virintuaṭikkum mikka kāṟṟu, oli
kaḻikkaṇ uṟuputīṇṭaliṉ - taḻitta mūṅkiliṉ kaṇukkaḷipporuntit tākkaliṉ, eṭutta -
eḻuppappaṭṭa, poṅkū -viḷaṅkukiṉṟa, poṟi pitirpu eḻu var eri -poṟiyic citaṟi
eḻukiṉṟa mikka neruppiṉāl,pitu aṟu cimiyam payam nīṅku - pacumiyaṟṟucciyiyuṭiya
payaṉ oḻintavum, nal aṭikkuaminta alla - taṉ nalla aṭikaṭku ēṟṟaṉaallavumākiya, ār
iṭi - ariya vaḻikaḷi, iṉi -ippoḻutu, melliya - meṉmittaṉmiyuṭiya avaḷ,vaṉkaṇāḷaṉ
mārpu uṟa vaḷii - koṭumiyuṭiya talivaṉtaṉ mārpiṉālē muṟṟa vaḷittu, iṉ
colpiṇippa - iṉiya collālē kaṭṭiviṭa, nampi -ataṉi virumpi, nam kaṇ uṟutaru
viḻumam uḷḷāḷ -nammiṭattē uṟum tuṉpattiṉi niṉiyāḷāki,oyyeṉa vallunaḷ kollō -
viriyac cellavallamiyuṭiyaḷ āvaḷō;
1. nōtakum uḷḷam- eṉ uḷḷam ataṉi niṉintu varuntukiṉṟatu ; nōku- yāṉum
nōkiṉṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) aḷiyaḷpantuvaḻip paṭarkuvaḷ āyiṉum naṉinontuvempummaṉ; iṉi melliyaḷ, mali
viḷaṅkiyacuraṉil, vēṉil veṅkāṭṭup payam nīṅkuāriṭiyil oyyeṉac cella vallunaḷ
kollō!uḷḷam nōtakum; yāṉnōku.
(vi - ri.) nōkō - ō: acinili, oḻiyiciyākak koṇṭuurittalumām. pantuvaḻi -
pantuviḷiyāṭumiṭam.vempummaṉ; maṉ mikutip poruṭṭu; kaḻivumām.oyyeṉac cella eṉa
viritturikka. kiviṭu cuṭar-paraṇiṉmītuḷḷa kāṉavar kiyiṉiṉṟum viṭuttapantam.

154. mulli
[viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉtērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 13. valava - pākaṉē!
1-10. paṭumaḻipoḻinta payam miku puṟaviṉ - mikka maḻicorintamiyāl payaṉ mikka
mullinilattē,neṭunīr avala pakuvāyt tēri - āḻamākiyanīriṉiyuṭiya paḷḷaṅkaḷiluḷḷa
piḷantavāyiṉiyuṭiya tērikaḷ, neṭu neṟi – nīṇṭa vaḻiyelām, ciṟu palliyattiṉ kaṟaṅka -
ciṟiyapalavākiya vācciyaṅkaḷippōla olikkavum,kuṟumputal piṭaviṉ neṭuṅkāl alari -
kuṟiyaputarākiya piṭaviṉ nīṇṭa kāmpiṉiyuṭiyamalarkaḷ, cemnila maruṅkiṉ nuṇ ayir
varippa -cemmaṇṇākiya nilattiṭattu nuṇṇiya maṇalilutirntu kōlam ceyyavum, vemciṉa
araviṉ piaṇantaṉṉa - koṭiya ciṉamuṭiya pāmpiṉ paṭam mēlēnivantāl pōṉṟa, taṇ kamaḻ kōṭal
tātupiṇiaviḻa - taṇṇeṉa maṇakkiṉṟa kāntaṭ pūviṉ tātukaṭṭaviḻntu viriyavum,
tirimaruppu iraliteḷaṟal paruki - muṟukkiya kompiṉiyuṭiyāṇmāṉ teḷinta nīrik
kuṭittu, kāmar tuṇiyoṭuēmuṟa vatiya - viruppam vāynta piṇiyuṭaṉiṉpam mikat
taṅkavum, kāṭu kaviṉpeṟṟa taṇpatapperuvaḻi - kāṭu aḻakupeṟṟa taṇṇiya cevvivāynta
periya vaḻiyilē;
11-3. ōṭuparimeliyāk koycuval puravi - ōṭum vēkam kuṟiyātakoyyappaṭṭa piṭari
mayiriṉiyuṭiya kutiriyiṉ,tāḷ tāḻ tārmaṇi tayaṅkupu iyampa - tāḷiṉ aḷavutāḻnta
māliyiṭattu maṇi viḷaṅki olikka, tērūrmati - tēri ōṭṭuvāyāka;
13-5. cīrmikupu -cīrmikku, nam vayiṉ purinta koḷki -nammiṭattu virumpiya
koḷkiyiṉiyuṭiya, ammāariviyi - aḻakiya māmi niṟattiyuṭiya namtaliviyi, virintu
tuṉṉukam - virintu ceṉṟuaṭivōm.
(muṭipu) valava! tēri kaṟaṅka, alari varippak kāntaṭpū tātupiṇi aviḻa irali ēmuṟak
kāṭu kaviṉpeṟṟa peruvaḻi,puravit tār maṇi iyampu tēr ūrmati, ariviyivirintu
tuṉṉukam.

(vi - ri) tēriyiṉ oliyum ciṟu palliyattiṉ oliyum orutaṉmiyavātal, 2'tēri
yoliyiṉmāṉaccīramittuc cillari' 'kaṟaṅkum ciṟupal iyattōṭu' eṉappiṉ varuvataṉālum
aṟiyappaṭum. 'araviṉ piaṇantaṉṉa taṇkamaḻ kōṭal tātu piṇi aviḻa'eṉṉum ikkaruttu,
1'araviṉaṇaṅkuṭi aruntalik pivirippavi pōl.....paḵṟuṭup peṭutta alaṅkukuli
kāntaḷ'eṉa muṉṉarum vantuḷḷami kāṇka. ariviyittuṉṉutaṟ poruṭṭut tēr virintu
ūrmati eṉṟu urittalumām. tōṭār tār maṇi eṉpatupāṭamāyiṉ, tokuti koṇṭa tārmaṇi
eṉka. tār- kutiriyiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭum māli.

155. pāli
[talimakaṉ piriviṉ kaṇvēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 6. tōḻi-,
1-4. puṉi yiḻi -aḻakiya aṇiyuṭiyāḷē!, eṉṟum aṟaṉ kaṭip paṭāavāḻkkiyum -
eññāṉṟum pāvaneṟiyiṟcellāta vāḻkkiyum, piṟaṉkaṭic celā accelvamum - piṟaṉ
maṉivāyiliṟ ceṉṟu nillāta mēmpāṭum āya, iraṇṭum - ivviraṇṭum, poruḷiṉ
ākumeṉṟu - poruḷāṉē yākum eṉṟu kūṟi, nam iruḷ ērimpāl nīviyōr - namatu iruṇṭa
aḻakiyakūntalit taṭaviya talivar;
6-12. pal vayiṉpayam niri cērnta pāṇāṭṭāṅkaṇ - palaviṭattum pāṟpacuk kūṭṭam
cērntuḷapāṇāṭeṉṉum avviṭattē, neṭu viḷik kōvalartōṇṭiya kūval - nīṇṭa cīḻkki
yoliyiyuṭiyakōvalar tōṇṭiya kiṇaṟṟiṉiṉṟum mukanta, koṭuvāyp pattal nīr vārntu uku
ciṟukuḻi -vaḷinta vāyiṉiyuṭiya pattaliṉiṉṟu nīr vaṭintucellum ciṟiya kuḻi, kāy
varuttamoṭu -nīriṉṟik kāyntataṉālāya varuttattuṭaṉ,cērvu iṭam peṟātu - nīr vēṭki
taṇittaṟkuccērattakka vēṟiṭam peṟāmal, peruṅkaḷiṟumititta aṭiyakattu - periya kaḷiṟu
mitittēkiyaaṭic cuvaṭṭil, irumpuli otuṅkuvaṉa kaḻinta - periyapulikaḷ aṭivittu
naṭantu ceṉṟa, cetumpal īrvaḻi- cēṟṟunilamāya īramuṭiya vaḻikaḷ;
13-6. ceyir tīrnāviṉ vayiriyar - kuṟṟamaṟṟa nāviṉi yuṭiyakūttar, piṉṟi acitta -
tōḷiṉ piṉṉēkaṭṭiya, maṇ ār muḻaviṉ kaṇakattu – mārccaṉi aminta mattaḷattiṉ
kaṇṇiṭattē, viral ūṉṟuvaṭuvil tōṉṟum - viralāl eṟinta vaṭukkaḷpōlat tōṉṟum, maral
vāṭu maruṅkiṉ maliiṟantōr - maralkaḷ vāṭiya iṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiyamaliyik kaṭantu ceṉṟār;
5-6. nōy nāmuḻakkuvam āyiṉum - avaratu pirivāl nāmnōyuḻappōm āyiṉum, tām tam
ceyviṉimuṭikka - avar tamatu poruḷīṭṭu viṉiyiṉi muṭittuvaruvārāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻkkiyum celvamum iraṇṭumporuḷiṉākum eṉṟu kūṟi nam aimpāl
nīviyōr, maral vāṭu maruṅkiṉmali yiṟantōr; nāmnōyuḻakkuvam āyiṉum, tām tam
ceyviṉimuṭikka.
(vi - ri.) aṟaṉkaṭi - pāvam; aṟattiṉ nīkkappaṭṭamiyiṉaṟaṉkaṭiyāyiṟṟu eṉpatu
1parimēḻakaruri. pāvamāvatu irantōrkku illi eṉṟal.aṟaṉkaṭip paṭā vāḻkki, irantārkku
ītalum,piṟaṉkaṭip paṭāac celvam - piṟarpāṟ ceṉṟuiravāmiyum ām eṉka. eṉṟum
eṉpataṉiiraṇṭiṭattum kūṭṭuka. celvam - vīṟu, mēmpāṭu,iruḷ ēr - iruḷiyotta
eṉṟumām. aimpālnīviyōr eṉṟatu, pirivu karutit taliyaḷiceytōr eṉṟavāṟu. pāṇāṭu -
pāṇaṉ nāṭeṉka; 'pāṇaṉnaṉṉāṭṭumpar' (11) , 'vaṭāatu, nalvēṟ pāṇaṉnaṉṉāṭṭuḷḷati'
(325) eṉap piṟāṇṭu varutalkāṇka. pāṇaṉ nāṭṭil āṉirikaḷ tiraṇṭiruntaṉa-veṉṟum,
avaṟṟiṟku nīrūṭṭaṟkuk kōvalartām tōṇṭiyakūvaliṉiṉṟum pattalāl nīr iṟittup pala
ciṟukuḻikaḷil nirappuvar eṉṟum, vēṉilvemmiyāl kūval nīr vaṟṟiṉamiyiṉ
nīriṟikkappeṟātu akkuḻikaḷkāyntiruntaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉkaṇ nīruṇṇavanta kaḷiṟukaḷ
nīrillāmiyālāya varuttattōṭuavaṟṟi mitittu naṭantu ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, piṉāṇṭuc ceṉṟa
pulikaḷ ak kaḷiṟukaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭṭilē kāl vittu naṭantu ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟum,ak kaḷiṟṟiṉ
aṭiccuvaṭṭiṟ patinta puliyiṉkāl viraṟ cuvaṭu, muḻaviṉ kaṇṇilē viralūṉṟiyavaṭuppōlat
tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟum koḷka. kaḷiṟukuḻiyil aṭi vittu ēkiyatum, puli, akkaḷiṟṟaṭiyil
mitittuc ceṉṟatum avvuḻicciṟitu īram iruntamiyāl eṉka. cetumpu-cēṟu.
alpakutipporuḷ vikuti. nām nōyuḻattalāltalivar karutiya viṉi muṭiyātu oḻiyuṅkol
eṉṉumkaruttiṉāl, tām tam ceyviṉi muṭikkaveṉōmpaṭi kūṟiṉāḷeṉka.

(u - ṟi.) yāṉi yaṭiyakattum puli yaṭivittuc ceṉṟa cuvaṭu -muḻaviṉ kaṇṇilē
viralūṉṟiya vaṭuppōlattōṉṟum vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟār eṉpatu, talivar poruḷīṭṭutaṟkaṇ eytum
tuṉpattiyēiṉpamākak karutic ceṉṟār eṉṟapaṭi.

156. marutam
[tali makaḷiiṭattuyttuvanta tōḻi talimakaṉi varivu kaṭāyatu]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. muracu uṭic celvar puraviccūṭṭu -muracaṅkaḷiyuṭiya celvaratu
kutiriyatu taliyiṉucciyiṭattu, mūṭṭuṟu kavari tūkki aṉṉa -iṇittut titta kavariyi
nimirttuvittālotta, ceḻum cey nelliṉ cēyaripuṉiṟṟuk katir - ceḻumi vāynta
vayaliluḷḷanelliṉ civanta arikayiuṭiya iḷaṅkatiri, mutuātiṉṟal añci - mutiya pacu
tiṉvatik kaṇṭuañci, kāvalar - vayaṟkāvalar, karumpu arutti -karumpiṉi uṇpittu,
pākal āykoṭipakaṉṟiyoṭu parīi - pākaliṉ ciṟanta koṭiyippakaṉṟiyiṉ koṭiyuṭaṉ aṟuttu
(avaṟṟāl), kāñciyiṉ akattu yākkum -kāñcimarattiṭattuk kaṭṭivikkum, tīm puṉalūra -
nīrvaḷam poruntiya ūriyuṭiya talivaṉē!
12. peruma - perumāṉē!
13-7. yāy - emaṉṉi, nilittuṟik kaṭavuṭku - tuṟiyiṉiṭattunilipeṟṟa
teyvattiṟku, kiyuṟiyāka - kiyuṟipporuḷāka, kaḷḷum kaṇṇiyum -
kaḷḷiṉiyummāliyiyum, nilikkōṭṭu nālcevi veḷḷikkiṭāay occi - nimirnta
kompiṉiyumtoṅkum kātiṉiyumuṭiya veḷḷāṭṭuk kiṭāyuṭpaṭac celuttiyum,
taṇimaruṅku aṟiyāḷ aḻa -taṉ makaḷukku uṟṟa nōy taṇiyum upāyamaṟiyāḷāy aḻa, maṇimaruḷ
mēṉi - ittaliviyiṉnīlamaṇi pōṉṟa mēṉi, poṉ niṟam koḷal -pacaliyāl poṉ niṟattiṉi
yaṭital
7-12. kuvaḷi uṇkaṇivaḷum yāṉum - nīlappūppōlum miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya ittaliviyum
yāṉum, tiṟavitu āka -cevvitāka, kaḻunīr āmpal muḻuneṟip pintaḻi -kaḻunīr āmpal
ivaṟṟiṉ itaḻ oṭiyāta pūkkaḷuṭaṉkūṭiya paciya taḻiyuṭi, ākattu alippa -
ākattiṉkaṇalittiṭa, kāyā ñāyiṟṟu - ñāyiṟu kāytalillātakālippoḻutil, poytal
āṭi - viḷiyāṭi,polika eṉa vantu - (piṉ) nī vāḻvāyāka eṉakkūṟi vantu, niṉnākap
piḻitta tavaṟō - niṉṉuṭaṉpiḻitta kuṟṟattālāya toṉṟō.
(muṭipu) tīm puṉalūra! peruma! yāy, nilittuṟikkaṭavuṭkukkaḷḷum kaṇṇiyum kiṭāyum
uṭpaṭōccit taṇimaruṅku aṟiyāḷ aḻa, ivaḷ mēṉipoṉṉiṟaṅ koḷal, ivaḷum yāṉum
poytalāṭinī polikeṉa vantu niṉ nakāp piḻittatavaṟō!
(vi - ri.) mūtā - mutiya erumiyumām. muḻuneṟi - itaḻoṭikkappaṭāta muḻup pū.
puṉiṟṟākkatirmutal tiṉṟal añci eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku,īṉṟaṇimiyuṭiya pacu nelliṉ
katiriyuṭiyamutaliṉit tiṉṟali añci eṉka. poytal āṭip polikeṉa vantu - nīvir poytal
āṭip polikeṉat tāy viṭukka vantu eṉṟurittalumām.nilikkōṭu - aṟātu niṟkum
kompu eṉṟalumām.
(u - ṟi) viḷinta piṉ peritu payaṉpaṭum iḷaṅkatiri ātiṉṟali añcik karumparuttik
kaṭṭip payaṉkoḷvārpōla, piṉṉē perumpayaṉ tarumkaḷaviṉi aḻittup payaṉtarātu ceyyum
alarkūṟuvār vāyaṭaṅki yoḻiya varintu koḷvāyākeṉṟavāṟu.

158. kuṟiñci
[talimakaṉciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi cevilittāykkuccolluvāḷāyt talimakaṉ kēṭpac

(co - ḷ.) 7. aṉṉi-, vāḻi-,
1-7. urum uraṟu karuviyaperumaḻi talii - iṭi muḻaṅkum tokutiyavāya mikkamaḻi

(vi - ri) miṭi - paraṇ. aṇaṅkumār: mār aci; avvaṇaṅkutuñcunarik kaṉaviṉ maruṭṭalum

[piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭatalimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 12. kātal am tōḻi - kātaliyuṭiya tōḻiyē!
1-4. teṇkaḻiviḷinta veṇkal uppiṉ - teḷintakaḻiyiṉkaṇ viḷinta veḷḷiya kal
uppiṉatu,koḷḷi cāṟṟiya - viliyik kūṟi viṟṟa,umaṇ-uppu vāṇikar, koṭuṅaka oḻuki -

iṟantaṉar eṉa - namkātalar ceṉṟaṉareṉṟu, naṉi avalam koḷḷal -mikavum tuṉpuṟātē;

kuṟumpoṟik kuṇāatu -kuṟumpoṟi eṉṉum malikkuk kiḻakkiṉkaṇuḷḷatum, vilkeḻu
taṭakki velpōr vāṉavaṉ-viṟporuntiya periya kiyiṉiyuṭiya pōrvellum cēraṉatu,
miñiṟu mūcukavuḷa ciṟu kaṇ yāṉi -vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum (matam vaḻiyum) kaṉṉattiṉiyuṭiya
ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ,toṭi uṭi taṭa maruppu oṭiya nūṟi - pūṇ pūṇṭa
periyakōṭṭiṉi oṭiyumpaṭi aḻittu, koṭumuṭi kākkum -koṭumuṭi eṉpāṉ kāttu
varukiṉṟa, kurūukkaṇneṭumatil - viḷaṅkiya iṭattiyuṭiya neṭiya matilcūḻnta, cēṇ
viḷaṅku ciṟappiṉ - neṭuntūramceṉṟu viḷaṅkum ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya,
āmūreytiṉum-āmuriyē aṭivatāyiṉum, niṉ pūṇ tāṅkuākam poruntutal maṟantu -
niṉatu pūṇ cumantamārpiṉip poruntutali maṟantu, āṇṭu amintuuṟiyunar allar - aṅkē
maṉam poruntittaṅkiviṭuvā rallar.
(muṭipu) kātalam tōḻi! umaṇ uyirttiṟanta oḻikalaṭuppiṉ koṭuvil lāṭavar
ūṉpuḻukkayarumkavaliyik kātalar iṟantaṉareṉa avalaṅkoḷḷal;(avar) āmūr eytiṉum niṉ ākam

160. neytal
[tōḻi varivu malintucolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 3-8. niṟic cūl yāmi - niṟivāya cūluṟṟayāmi,aṭumpu koṭi citiya
vāṅki - aṭumpiṉik koṭicitiya iḻuttu, koṭum kaḻi kuppi veṇmaṇalpakkam cērtti -
vaḷinta kaḻiyiṭattu veḷḷiyamaṇal mēṭṭiṉ pakkattē cērttu (ataṉ kaṇa) ,maṟittu īṉṟu
putitta - maṟiya īṉṟu putitta,kōṭṭu vaṭṭu uruvaṉ pulavu nāṟu muṭṭi -
yāṉikkompiṉāṟ
ceyta vaṭṭiṉ vaṭivamuṭiya pulālnāṟum muṭṭiyi, pakuvāyk kaṇavaṉ - piḷantavāyiṉiyuṭiya
-ṇ yāmi, pārppu iṭaṉ -kum aḷavi -ataṉiṭattiṉiṉṟu kuñcu veḷippaṭum aḷavu,
ompum- pātukāttirukkum, kāṉal am cērppaṉ -cōliyiyuṭiya kaṭaṟkarit talivaṉatu;
9-15. tiṇtēr -valiya tērāṉatu, ē toḻil naviṉṟa eḻilnaṭipuravi -ampiṉ vēkampōlac
cellutalip paḻakiya aḻakiyanaṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kutirikaḷ, muḷuṟil ciṟattalañci -
tāṟṟiṉāl kuttappeṟiṉ vēkam aḷavukaṭattali añci, vaḷpiṟkāṭṭi -
kaṭivāḷattiṉālkuṟippikka, mella varavu uṭimiyiṉ - mellattāvic cellutal
koṇṭamiyiṉ, ceḻunīr taṇkaḻi nīntaliṉ - ceḻumi vāynta nīriṉiyuṭiyakuḷirnta kaḻiyiṉik
kaṭakkuṅkāl, āḻinutimukam kuṟinta poti mukiḻ neytal -attēr uruḷiyiṉ kūriya
muṉiyāl aṟukkappeṟṟapotinta arumpukaḷiyuṭiya neytal, pāmpu uyartaliyil cāmpuvaṉa
nivappa - pāmpiṉ mēlētūkkiya taliyippōla vāṭi mēleḻa, iravantaṉṟu -itukāṟum
irākkālaṅkaḷil vantu koṇṭiruntatu;
16-9. pāypariciṟantu - (iṉṟu attēr) pāyum kutirivēkattāṟ ciṟappuṟṟu,
karavātu - maṟiyāmal, oleṉa olikkum iḷiyaroṭu - olleṉa āravāramceyyum ēval
iḷiyaroṭu, valvāy aravac ciṟuūr kāṇa - valiya vāyiṉālē alarākiya oliyicceyyum
ciṟiya ūrp peṇṭir kāṇa, pakalvantaṉṟu - pakalilē vantatu; (ataṉāl) ;
1-2. eṉ niṟi ilneñcam naṭuṅkiṉṟu - eṉatu niṟiyillāta neñcamnaṭuṅkiyatu,
aḷittu - atu iraṅkattakkatu, oṭuṅkuīr ōti - oṭuṅkiya kariyakūntaliyuṭiya
tōḻiyē!niṉakkum aṟṟō - niṉakkum avvāṟu neñcamnaṭuṅkiyatō?
(muṭipu) kāṉalañ cērppaṉ tēr itukāṟum iraviṉkaṇvantaṉṟu; ippoḻutu pakal vantaṉṟu,
eṉ neñcamnaṭuṅkiṉṟu; aḷittu ! oṭuṅku īr ōti! niṉakkumaṟṟō?
(vi - ri.) yāmiyāṉatu muṭṭiyi maṟiviṭattil īṉṟuputittup pōkumeṉavum, āṇ
yāmiyāṉatu ammuṭṭiyik kuñcu veḷippaṭum aḷavu pātukākkumeṉavum koḷka. ivvuṇmi,
1'tāyiṉmuṭṭi pōla uṭkiṭantu, cāyi ṉallatu piṟitevaṉuṭittō, yāmippārppiṉ aṉṉa,
kāmamkātalar kiyaṟaviṭiṉē.' 2'tīmperumpoyki yāmiyiḷam pārppu, tāymuka
nōkkivaḷarntici ṉāṅku' eṉṉuñ ceyyuṭkaḷāṉum uṇarka.

muḷtāṟu. kāṭṭi - kāṭṭa eṉattirikka. muṉpellām iravantaṉṟu eṉavum ippoḻutu pakal
vantaṉṟu eṉavum viritturikka.ippoḻutu karavātu iḷiyaroṭu cīṟūr kāṇappariciṟantu pakal
vantaṉṟu eṉṟamiyāl,muṉpellām karavilē iḷiyariṉṟic cīṟūrkāṇāmalum, pariciṟavāmalum
iravil vantateṉka.ippoḻutu tēr iṅṅaṉam vantatu eṉṟatu varivuvēṇṭi vanta teṉṟapaṭi. eṉ
neñcam naṭuṅkiṉṟu;oṭuṅkīrōti! niṉakkum aṟṟō eṉṟatu taliviyinōkki, varivu malintu
nakiyāṭic colliyatu.
(u - ṟi.) 'niṟiccūl yāmiyik kāmam niṟintataliviyākavum, atu aṭumpaṅ koṭiyi
yaṟuttu ataṉiveṇmaṇaṟ pakkattē cērttatu, talivi taṉ tāyartaṉṉiyarpāluḷḷa aṟput
taḷiyiyaṟuttuavvaṉpit talivaṉ pakkalilē cērttutaṉmiyākavum, atu tāṉ īṉṟa muṭṭiyi
maṟittupputittatu, talivi tāṉ oḻukiya kaḷavoḻukkattippuṟattārkkup pulaṉākāmal
maṟittuvittataṉmiyākavum, ammuṭṭi pulavu nāṟiyatu, ompiyatu, akkaḷavoḻukkattit
talimakaṉ variyavantumāṭcimip paṭuttum tuṇiyum iṭiyīṭiṉṟikkuṟivaḻi yoḻukit
talimakaḷi ōmpiṉa taṉmiyākavum uḷḷuṟiyuvamam koḷka' eṉpar, irācakōpāla aiyaṅkār.

161. pāli
[pirivuṇarttiyatōḻi, talimakaḷatuvēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu muṉṉamēuṇarntāḷ namperumāṭṭiyeṉṟu
talimakaṉiccelavu vilakkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 9-14. (taliva !) curumpu tōṭṭu āḻpu uṇa - vaṇṭukaḷmalaritaḻuṭpukkut
tēṉiyuṇṇa, olivarum irum palkūntal - taḻitta kariya palavāya kūntaliyum, am māmēṉi -
aḻakiya māmi niṟamuṭiya mēṉiyiyum, āy iḻi- āynta aṇikaḷiyu muṭiya, kuṟumakaḷ -
iḷiyaḷāyataliviyiṉ, cuṇaṅku cūḻ ākattu - titali paṭarntamārpiṉkaṇ, aṇaṅku eṉa
urutta - vīṟṟutteyvameṉa urukkoṇṭa, nal varal iḷamuli naṉiya -nalla vaḷarcciyiyuṭiya
iḷiya mulikaḷ naṉiya, palitaḻ uṇkaṇ - pala itaḻiyuṭiya nīlap pūppōṉṟamiyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ, paṉi
parantaṉa - nīrparantoḻukiṉa;
1-9. vaṇar curivaṭiyāp pitti vaṉkaṇ āṭavar-vaḷintucuruṇṭakōtappeṟāta mayiriṉiyuṭiya
koṭiya maṟavar,aṭi amipakaḻi āra vāṅki - kuti yaminta ampiṉimuḻutum
iḻuttuviṭuttu, vampalarc cekutta añcuvarukavali - vaḻic cellum putiyarik koṉṟa
accamtōṉṟum kavartta neṟiyil, paṭumuṭi naciiyavāḻkkic cem cevi eruvic
cēval,poruntummuṭi naṇumpulāli virumpiyuṇṇumvāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya civanta
ceviyiyuṭiyaparuntiṉ cēval, īṇṭu kiḷi payirum - neruṅkiyataṉ cuṟṟattiṉi
aḻikkum, veruvaru kāṉam -accam tōṉṟum kāṉatti, poruḷ purintu -poruḷi
virumpi, nīnti irappa eṇṇiṉireṉpatu - kaṭantu cella eṇṇiṉir eṉpati tāṉciṟappak
kēṭṭaṉaḷ kol - avaḷ muṉṉarēnaṉkukēṭṭu ḷāḷō? (āyiṉa) ;
1. viṉivayiṟpiritalyāvatu - nīr eṇṇiyāṅku poruḷīṭṭumviṉiyiṉ kaṇ avaḷip pirintu
cellutalyāṅṅaṉam iyalvatākum? iyalātukāṇ.
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷ ākattu muli naṉiya kaṇ paṉi parantaṉa, veruvarukāṉam poruḷ purintu
iṟappa eṇṇiṉireṉpatu, tāṉ ciṟappak kēṭṭaṉaḷ kollō?(āyiṉ) viṉivayiṟ piritalyāvatu?
(vi - ri.) cēval muṭiyi uṇṭaṟkuk kiḷiyip payirum eṉka.aṇaṅku - vīṟṟut teyvam.
atumuliyiṭattirukkum meṉpatu.
1'āmaṇaṅkukuṭiyirun tañcuṇaṅku parantaṉavē' eṉpataṉāṟpeṟappaṭum. kaṇ paṉi parantaṉa eṉpatu,
iṭattiṉikaḻ poruḷiṉ toḻil iṭattiṉmēlniṉṟatu.

162. kuṟiñci
[iravukkuṟikkaṇtalimakaḷik kaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkiya talimakaṉ taṉneñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) neñcē-,
1-6. koḷak kuṟipaṭā- koḷḷak koḷḷak kuṟipaṭātatum, kōṭu vaḷarkuṭṭattu - caṅkukaḷ
perukum āḻattiṉiyuṭimiyāl,aḷappu aritākiya - aḷattaṟku ariyatākiyatum, kuviiru
tōṉṟal - tiraṇṭa kariya tōṟṟattiṉiyuṭiyatum ākiya, kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa - kaṭalikkaṇṭālotta,
māka vicumpiṉ - vāṉil,aḻaṟkoṭi aṉṉa miṉṉu vacipu nuṭaṅka - tīyiṉkoṭiyi yotta
miṉṉal mēkattip piḷantukoṇṭu acintu cella, kaṭitu iṭi urumoṭu -
kaṭumiyākiṭikkum iṭiyuṭaṉ, kataḻ uṟi citaṟi - virintanīric citaṟi, viḷivu iṭaṉ
aṟiyā - muṭiviṭamaṟiyāvāṟu, vāṉ umiḻ naṭu nāḷ - mēkampeytalic ceyyum naṭu
iravil;
7-9. aruṅ kaṭikkāvalar ikaḻ patam nōkki - ariya kāttaṟṟoḻiliyuṭiya kāvalar
nekiḻntiruntacevviyi nōkki, paṉi mayaṅku aci vaḷi alippa -kuḷir poruntiya
acintu varum vāṭikkāṟṟuvarutta, tanti neṭunakar oru ciṟi niṉṟaṉeṉ āka -taṉ
tantiyiṉ nīṇṭa māḷikiyiṉ oru puṟattēniṉṟu koṇṭiruntēṉāka;
16- 25. vāymoḻi -vāymic colliṉiyum, nal ici tarūumiravalarkku uḷḷiya naci
piḻippu aṟiyā - nallakīrttiyiṉi aṭivikkum iravalarkku avareṇṇiya viruppam piḻittal
aṟiyappaṭātīkiyiṉiyum uṭiya, kaḻal toṭi atikaṉ - kaḻalumvīra vaḷiyum aṇinta atikaṉ
eṉpāṉatu, kōḷaṟavuaṟiyāp payam keḻu palaviṉ - kāyttalilliyātali aṟiyāta payaṉuṭiya
palāmarattiṉōṭu,vēṅki cērnta veṟpakam poliya -vēṅkimaramum poruntiyuḷḷa maliyiṭam
polivuṟa,vil keḻutāṉi pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ - villiyuṭiyacēṉiyiyuṭiya pacum pūṇ
pāṇṭiyaṉatu, kaḷiṟu aṇi velkoṭi kaṭuppa - kaḷiṟṟiṉ mītu eṭutta vellumkoṭiyi
yoppa, kāṇvara oḷiṟuvaṉa iḻi tarumuyarntu tōṉṟu aruvi - aḻakupeṟa
miḷirvaṉavāyiḻiyum uyarntu kāṇappeṟum aruvikaḷiyuṭiya,nēr koḷ neṭuvarik kavāṉ -
nērāṉa neṭiyamaliyiṉ cāraliluḷḷa, cūr aramakaḷiril peṟaṟkuariyōḷ - accam tarum
teyvappeṇkaḷippōlap peṟutaṟku ariyaḷākiya nam talivi;
10-6. aṟal eṉaavirvarum kūntal - aṟalpōl viḷaṅkumkūntaliṉiyum, malar eṉa vāḷ
mukattu alamarum āyitaḻ maḻikkaṇ - nīlamalareṉa oḷiporuntiyamukattil cuḻalum aḻakiya
itaḻiṉiyuṭiya kuḷirntakaṇṇiṉiyum, māvīḻ muki nirittaṉṉa veṇ pal -vaṇṭukaḷ virumpum
mulli arumpiṉi variciyāka niṟuttiṉālottaveṇ palliṉiyum,naki māṇṭu ilaṅku nalam
keḻu tuvarvāy -puṉṉakiyāl māṇpuṟṟu viḷaṅkum naṉmi vāyntapavaḷam pōṉṟa vāyiṉiyum
uṭiyaḷāy, kōl amiviḻuttoṭi viḷaṅka vīci - aḻaku vāynta ciṟantavaḷiyal viḷaṅkak kiyi
vīci, kāl uṟu taḷiriṉnaṭuṅki - kāṟṟu vīcappeṟṟa taḷireṉa nuṭaṅkivantu, nōy acā vīṭa
āṉātu muyaṅkiṉaḷ - namatunōyākiya varuttam nīṅka amiyātu muyaṅkiṉaḷ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! vāṉ umiḻ naṭunāḷ tanti neṭunakar oruciṟi niṉṟaṉeṉāka; cūrara
makaḷiriṟ peṟaṟkariyōḷ vīci naṭuṅki vantu nōyacā vīṭamuyaṅkiṉaḷ, vicumpiṉ miṉṉu
vacipu nuṭaṅka,vāṉam urumoṭu uṟicitaṟip peyalumiḻ naṭu nāḷeṉavum, veṟpakam poliya
velkoṭi kaṭuppuyarntu tōṉṟu aruvi neṭuvarik kavāṉiluḷḷa cūraramakaḷir eṉavum koḷka.
(vi - ri.) kōṭu - kariyumām. māka vicumpu - irupeyaroṭṭu.karumiyum parappum
paṟṟik kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉeṉṟār. viḷi viṭaṉ taṅku miṭamumām. aruṅkaṭieṉṟataṉāṟ kāvaliṉ
arumi kūṟappaṭṭatu. vaḷiacippa oru ciṟi niṉṟataṉāṟ kāvaliṉ arumikūṟappaṭṭatu. vaḷi
acippa oru ciṟi niṉṟaṉeṉeṉṟataṉāl, talivipāluḷḷa vēṭki mikutiyāltalivaṉ
iravukkuṟikkaṇ niṉṟa arumikuṟikkappaṭṭatu. kōlam - kōl eṉak kuṟintuniṉṟatu; kōl -
tiraṭciyumām. naṭuṅkiya -accattāl naṭuṅki eṉṟalumām. piḻippariyīkiyiṉiyumuṭiya
eṉa viritturikka. kōḷaṟavu aṟiyāeṉṟatu, eññāṉṟum kāyttaliyuṭiya eṉṟapaṭi.pacumpūṭ
pāṇṭiyaṉ, peyar. neṭuvarikkavāṉ -nīṇṭa mūṅkiliyuṭiya pakka maliyumām.

163. pāli
[piriviṉkaṇvaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ āṟṟāmimītūrac colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-6. viravu malar kuḻiya - palavakiyāya malarkaḷumkuḻintiṭa, viṇ atirpu
taliiya - vāṉiṉ kaṇmuḻaṅkik kūṭiya, taṇmaḻi poḻinta tāḻipeyal kaṭināḷ - kuḷirnta
mēkam corintupōṉakuṟinta peyalikkoṇṭa kūtiriṉ kaṭi nāḷil,tuṉi uḷamkūra - maṉattē
veṟuppu mika, emiyamākacceṉṟōr uḷḷi - yām tamiyēmākap pirintuceṉṟa talivari
eṇṇi, cilvaḷi nekiḻa – eñciya cilavāya vaḷikaḷum kaḻaṉṟu pōka, perunaci
uḷḷamoṭuvarutici nōkki - periya ārvam koṇṭuḷḷattōṭu avar mīṇṭuvarum ticiyi
nōkki(varunti) , viḷiyum evvamoṭu aḷiyaḷ eṉṉātu -iṟattaṟkētuvāya tuṉpattōṭu
kūṭiyiruttaliṉaḷikkattakkāḷ ivaḷ eṉṟu niṉiyātu;
7-10. kaḷiṟuuyirttaṉṉa kaṇ aḻi tuvali - yāṉi (nīripparuka) uyirppatu pōlum iṭam
maṟiya vīcumpaṉittuḷiyāl, muḷari kariyum muṉpaṉippālnāḷ - tāmarimalar karintiṭum
muṉpaṉikkālattup pātiyiravil, kuṉṟu nekiḻppu aṉṉakuḷirkoḷ vāṭi - maliyiyum
naṭuṅkac ceyvatupōṉṟa kuḷirikkoṇṭa vāṭikkāṟṟē, eṉakkēvantaṉi pōṟi - nī eṉpālē
naliya vantaṉi pōlkiṉṟāy;
10-4. puṉal kālayiriṭu kuppiyiṉ - nīr ōṭukāliṭattuḷḷanuṇmaṇalālāya mēṭu
karintiṭalpōla, neñcunekiḻntu aviḻa - neñcam karintu iḷaka,koṭiyōr ceṉṟa tēettu -
koṭiyarāya attalivar ceṉṟa ticiyil, maṭiyātu iṉiyi ākicceṉmati - ayarātu iṉṉa
taṉmiyiṉi ākiccelvāyāka; (celliṉ) , viṉi vituppu uṟunaruḷḷalum uṇṭu -
poruḷīṭṭum toḻiliṟperuvēṭki yuṭiyār eṉṉi niṉittu varutalumuṇṭu.
(muṭipu) tāḻpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ; ceṉṟōr uḷḷievvamoṭu (iruttaliṉ) aḷiyaḷ
eṉṉātu,kuḷirkoḷ vāṭiyē! eṉakkē vantaṉi pōṟi;koṭiyōr ceṉṟa tēettuc ceṉmati;
viṉivituppuṟunar uḷḷalumuṇṭu.
(vi - ri.) viḷiyum evvamoṭu eṉpataṟkut tuṉpattāliṟantu paṭuvaḷ eṉṟurittalumām.
viḷiyāevvamoṭu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ keṭāta tuṉpattiṉoṭu (iruttaliṉ) eṉṟurikka.
eṉṉātu vantaṉi eṉak kūṭṭuka.

164. mulli
[pācaṟikkaṇ iruntatalimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ) neñcē!
11-4. pakivar matilmukam murukkiya toṭi citi maruppiṉ – pakivaratu matiṟkataviṉic
cititta pūṇcitintakōṭṭiṉiyum, kantu ocikkum kāl yāṉi -kaṭṭut taṟiyiṉi oṭikkum
kāliṉiyumuṭiyayāṉikaḷiyuṭiya, vem ciṉa vēntaṉviṉiṭippeṟiṉ - koṭiya
ciṉattiṉiyuṭiya namvēntaṉ ip pōriṉi muṭikkap peṟiṉ;
1-7. ñāyiṟu katirkiyāka vāṅki - ñāyiṟu taṉ katirē kiyākīrattiṉik kavarntu, pitu
aṟa teṟutaliṉ -pacumiyaṟak kāytaliṉ, payam karantu māṟi - nīrilliyāki oḻiya,
viṭuvāyp paṭṭa - piḷattalporuntiya, viyaṉ kaṇ mānilam - mikka iṭam vāynta periya
puviyilē, kāṭukaviṉ etira - kāṭu (paṇṭi) aḻakiṉi eyta, kaṉipeyal poḻitaliṉ -
mikka maḻipoḻintamiyāl, poṟi vari iṉavaṇṭu ārppa -poṟikaḷiyum varikaḷiyum uṭiya
kūṭṭamāya vaṇṭukaḷāravāram ceyya, naṟuvī mulliyoṭu tōṉṟi paluṭaṉ tōṉṟa - naṟiya
mullippūvuṭaṉ ceṅkāntaḷpūvum vēṟu pala pūkkaḷum oruṅku malara, kāṉam vī kamaḻveṟi
ēṉṟaṉṟu - kāṭu malar nāṟum nāṟṟamporuntiyatu;
8-11. avarnilievaṉkol eṉa mayaṅki - nam talivar nilieṉṉāyiṟṟō eṉa mayaṅki, iku paṉi
uṟikkumkaṇṇoṭu - tāḻum nīriṉic cintāniṟkum kaṇkaḷuṭaṉ, āṅku iṉipu iṉṉātu
uṟivi -maṉiyiṉkaṇ varunti iṉṉāmiyuṭaṉ vativāḷāyatalivi, tol nalam peṟūum nalkālam
itukāṇṭiciṉ - (nammik kaṇṭu) paḻiya nalattiṉiaṭiyum nalla kālam ituvākum
kāṇpāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! veñciṉa vēntaṉ viṉiviṭap peṟiṉ,mānilam kāṭu kaviṉ etirap peyal
poḻitaliṉkāṉam veṟi ēṉṟaṉṟu; avar nili yātō eṉamayaṅki iṉipu āṅku iṉṉātu uṟivi
toṉṉalampeṟūum naṟkālam itukāṇ.
(vi - ri.) payam - vaḷamumām. viṭuvāy - piḷappu. nalam peṟūumnaṟkālam itu eṉa
māṟuka. veñciṉa vēntaṉviṉiviṭap peṟiṉ eṉṟamiyāl, vēntaṉveṟṟi yeytiyum ciṉa
mikutiyāṟ cantiṟkuoruppaṭātuḷaṉ eṉpatu pōtarum.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'ēṉōrmaruṅkiṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, (icceyyuḷ) piritaṟ pakutiyākiya
pācaṟip pulampal eṉiṉumnilam paṟṟi mulliyāyiṟṟu eṉṟurittaṉar, iḷampūraṉār.

165. pāli
[makaṭpōkkiya tāyatunilimi kaṇṭār colliyatu.]
kayantali maṭappiṭi payampiṟ paṭṭeṉak

(co - ḷ.) 1-4. kayantali maṭappiṭi payampiṟ paṭṭeṉa -melliya taliyiyuṭiya iḷiya
peṇyāṉi kuḻiyilakappaṭṭatāka, kaḷiṟu viḷippaṭutta kampali verīi- kaḷiṟṟuyāṉi kūppīṭu
ceyta āravārattiṟkuañci, oyyeṉa eḻunta cevvāyk kuḻavi -virinteḻunta civanta
vāyiṉatāya ataṉ kaṉṟu, tātueru maṟukiṉ mūtūr āṅkaṇ - tātākiya
eruviṉiyuṭiyaterukkaḷiyuṭiya paḻiya ūrāya avviṭattu, erumi nalāṉ peṟu muli
māntum - erumiyāya nallamāṭṭiṉiṉṟum peṟukiṉṟa. mulippāli uṇṇum iṭamāya, nāṭu
pala iṟanta naṉṉa rāṭṭikku - pala nāṭukaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟa naṉmiyi yuṭiyāḷ poruṭṭu ;
7. āyamum aṇi iḻantu aḻuṅkiṉṟu - āyamum poliviḻantu varuntukiṉṟatu;
7-13. tāyum - tāyum, iṟīiyar eṉ uyir eṉa - eṉuyir keṭuvatāka eṉṟu nontu kūṟi,
tarumaṇal kiṭanta pāvi - koṇarntu parappiya maṇalil kiṭanta makaḷatu pāviyi eṭuttu,
kaṇṇum nutalum nīvi - ataṉ kaṇṇiyum neṟṟiyiyum taṭavi, taṭavu nili nocci vari niḻal
taṇ eṉa acii - vaḷinta niliyiṉiyuṭiya nocciyiṉ vari variyākavuḷḷa niḻalil taṭpamuṟak
kiṭatti, tāḻik kuvaḷi vāṭumalar cūṭṭi - tāḻikkaṇṇē yuḷḷa kuvaḷiyiṉ vāṭiya malaric
cūṭṭi, eṉ arumakaḷē - eṉ ariya makaḷē, iṉ tōḷ tārāy eṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻum - niṉatu
iṉiya tōḷit taruvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟit taḻuvi aḻāniṟpaḷ.
(muṭipu) nāṭu pala iṟanta naṉṉarāṭṭikku āyamum apaṅkiṉṟu; tāyum, iṟīiyar eṉ uyir
eṉakkūṟi, pāviyik kaṇṇum nutalum nīvi, niḻal acii, malar cūṭṭi, eṉ arumakaḷē
iṉtōḷ tārāy eṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻum.
(vi - ri.) kaṟpukkaṭaṉ pūṇṭu kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟāḷākaliṉ - 'naṉṉarāṭṭi' eṉṟār. āyamum
aṇiyiḻan taḻuṅkiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāl tāyatu nilimiyōṭu āyattiṉ nilimiyiyum kaṇṭār
colliṉar eṉṟu koḷka. āyamum tāyum eṉṉum ummikaḷ eccapporuḷaṉa. īṉṟōṭṭārāy eṉpatu
pāṭamāyiṉ, tāy pāviyi nōkki, niṉṉi īṉṟavaḷākiyeṉ makaḷik kāṭṭuvāyāka eṉṟu
kūṟiṉāḷeṉakkoḷḷal vēṇṭum. taṇṇeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ eṉṟuiyittalumām. talivi yiruntu nīr
vākkappeṟāmiyāl, tāḻik kuvaḷiyiṉ malarvāṭuvatāyiṟṟu.

(mē - ḷ.) 1'taṉṉumavaṉum avaḷum cuṭṭi' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu(icceyyuḷ) 'tāy
niliyum āyattu niliyumkaṇṭōr kūṟiyavāṟuṇarka' eṉṟum 2'pālkeḻukiḷavi nālvarkku
murittē' eṉṉuñcūttirattu, 'tarumaṇaṟ kiṭanta pāviyeṉ, arumakaḷēyeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ

166. marutam
[parattiyoṭu puṉalāṭiya talimakaṉ talimakaḷiṭip pukku yāṉ-āṭiṟṟilaṉ eṉṟu cūḷuṟṟāṉ
eṉpatu kēṭṭa 3parattitaṉ pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 11. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-4. nalmaram kuḻīiya- nalla maraṅkaḷ cūḻnta (kaḷ viṟkum illiluḷḷa) , pal nāḷ aritta
naṉimutir cāṭi - palanāḷumvaṭikkappeṟṟa kaḷ niṟinta cāṭiyi, kōoyuṭippiṉ -

167. pāli

(co - ḷ.) 7.neñcē-,
1-5. vayaṅku maṇiporuta - paḷiṅku maṇiyiṉi yotta, vakiamivaṉappiṉ - kūṟupāṭaminta
aḻakiṉiyuṭiya, pacukāḻ alkul māayōḷoṭu - paciya carattālāyamēkaliyi aṇinta

periya kiyiṉiyuṭiyayāṉiyiṉ, verin ōṅku ciṟupuṟamuriña -mutukiṉiṉ ṟuyarntu

cuvariluḷḷa viṭṭamaram vīḻantatāka, maṇippuṟāt tuṟanta maramcōrmāṭattu - maṇippuṟā
viṭṭoḻinta maramcōrnta māṭattiṉiyum, eḻutu aṇi kaṭavuḷpōkaliṉ - eḻutappeṟṟa aḻakiya
kaṭavuḷ puṟattēpōyviṭṭamiyiṉ, pul eṉṟu - poli vaṟṟu. oḻukupali maṟanta - iṭiyaṟātu
nikaḻum pali maṟakkappeṟṟa,meḻu kāp puṉ tiṇi - meḻukappaṭāta pulliyatiṇṇiyil, pāl
nāy tuṉṉiya paṟikkaṇ ciṟuil - īṉṟaṇimiyuṭiya nāy taṅkiya paṟintiṭattiyuṭiya
ciṟṟiliyum, avviṭattē kuyilkāḻ citiya maṇṭi - iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kim maraṅkaḷcitiyumāṟu

iṟappiṉiyumuṭiya ampalattiṉ kaṇ ;

yuṭiyēmāy, nāḷi - nāḷip pōḻtil,cēkkuvam kol - taṅki iruppēmō

168. kuṟiñci
[iravukkuṟi vantatalimakaḷi iravukkuṟi vilakki varivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 3. vāṉ tōy veṟpa - vāṉi aḷāviya maliyiyuṭiyatalivaṉē!
1-2. yāmam nummoṭukaḻippi - irāppoḻuti num muṭaṉ kūṭik kaḻittu,nōy mika -
piṉpu iravukkuṟiyiṉ ētam añcittuṉpam mikutalāl, paṉivār kaṇṇēm vikutum -nīr
oḻukuṅ kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyēmāy varuntiyirukkiṉṟēm ākaliṉ;
3-14. pallāṉkuṉṟil paṭuniḻal cērnta - pallāṉ kuṉṟueṉṉum maliyiṟ poruntum
niḻaliṉkaṭcernta, nal āṉ parappiṉ kuḻumūr āṅkaṇ -nalla āṉiriyiṉ parappiṉik
koṇṭakuḻumūriṭattē, koṭik kaṭaṉ ēṉṟa kōṭāneñciṉ - īkiyāya kaṭaṉi
ēṟṟukkoṇṭakōṭṭamillāta neñciṉiyuṭiya, utiyaṉaṭṭilpōla - utiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu aṭukkaḷi
pōla,oli eḻuntu aruvi ārkkum peruvaric cilampiṉ- olimikku aruvikaḷ āravārittu
iḻiyum periyavariyiṉatu pakka maliyil, kaḷiṟu īṉṟaṇi irupiṭitaḻīi - kaḷiṟu īṉṟa
aṇimiyuṭiya periya piṭiyiṉittaḻuvikkoṇṭu, taṉ tūṅku naṭikkuḻavi tuyil
puṟamkāppa - taṉatu acinta naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kuḻaviuṟaṅkukiṉṟa iṭattik kāttirukka,
kaṭuṅkaṇvāḷ vari vayappuli - taṟukaṇmiyuṭiya vāḷ pōṉṟavariyiṉiyuṭiya valiya puli,
oṭuṅku aḷi pulampa pōki- oṭuṅkiya muḻi taṉittiṭap puṟam pōy, kal muḻiuraṟa -
maliyiṉ kukiyiṭattil muḻaṅka, kāṉavarmaṭinta kaṅkul - vēṭṭuvarum tuyilum
naḷḷiravil,māṉ atarc ciṟu neṟi - vilaṅkukaḷ cellumvaḻiyākiya ciṟuneṟiyil, nī
varutal - nī varutali,iṉi āṉṟal vēṇṭum - iṉi oḻital vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) veṟpa! yāmam nummoṭu kaḻippi, paṉivārkaṇṇēm vikutum ākaliṉ, cilampil,
kaḷiṟukāppa, puli uraṟa kāṉavar maṭinta kaṅkulil ciṟuneṟikkaṇ nī varutali iṉi āṉṟal
vēṇṭum.
(vi - ri.) irāp poḻuti nummuṭaṉ kaḻikkum nāḷellāmappoḻutu iṉpamāyiṉum nī
varum vaḻiyiṉētatti niṉintu peritum tuṉpuṟukiṉṟōm.ākaliṉ, iṉi nī avvāṟu

169. pāli
[talimakaṉ,iṭiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) neñcē!
1-8. maram talikarintu nilam payam vāṭa - maraṅkaḷ uccikariyavum nilam vaḷam kuṉṟavum,
alaṅku katirvēynta aḻal tikaḻ naṉantali - acikiṉṟañāyiṟṟiṉ katir kuḻappeṟṟa vemmi
viḷaṅkumakaṉṟa pāli nilattē, puli tolittu uṇṭa perumkaḷiṟṟu oḻi ūṉ - puliyāṟ
koṉṟutiṉṉappeṟṟa periya yāṉiyiṉatu eñcikkiṭantūṉil, kalikeḻu maṟavar kāḻkōttu
oḻintati -āravāram poruntiya āṟalippōr kōliṟkōttuk (koṇṭēkiyatu pōka)
eñciyati,olitiri kaṭalviḷi amiḻtiṉ kaṇam cāl umaṇar- olikkum ali poruntiya kaṭalil
viḷiyumuppiṉik koṇaram kūṭṭam mikka uppuvāṇikar, ñelikōl ciṟu tī māṭṭi -
tīkkaṭikōlālākkiya ciṟu tīyil vatakki, cuṉi koḷtī nīr cōṟṟu uli kūṭṭum
-cuṉiyiṟkoṇṭa iṉiya nīrālāya cōṟṟiṉuliyil kūṭṭi ākkum, curampala kaṭanta nam vayiṉ-
pala cura neṟikaḷiyum kaṭantu vantuḷa nammiṭattu,paṭarntu - taṉ uḷḷam paṭartalāl;
8-14. tiruntu iḻi -tiruntiya aṇikaḷiyuṭiya nam talivi, celkatirmaḻukiya
pulampukoḷ māli - maṟiyum katir maṅkiyataṉimi koṇṭa māliyil, naṉi pacali
pāyntamēṉiyaḷ - pacali mikavum paranta mēṉiyaḷāki, neṭituniṉintu - nīḷa niṉintu,
mel viral cērttiyanutalaḷ - melliya viralkaḷic cērttaneṟṟiyiṉaḷāy, kayal umiḻ nīril
kaṇ paṉimalki vāra - kayalmīṉ umiḻum nīrpōlakkaṇkaḷil nīr niṟintu oḻuka, perum
tōḷnekiḻnta cellaloṭu - periya tōḷmelintatālāya tuṉpattuṭaṉ, varuntum -varuntuvaḷ,
aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! curampala kaṭanta namvayiṉ taṉ uḷḷampaṭar taliṉ, tiruntiḻi, māliyilē
neṭituniṉintu pacali pāynta mēṉiyaḷāy, viralcērtti nutalaḷāy, kaṇpaṉi vārac
cellaloṭuvaruntum; aḷiyaḷ.
(vi - ri.) karintu - kariya eṉat tirikka. payam - ēṉivaḷaṅkaḷ. kāḻ - iruppuc
calāki. kaṭalviḷiamiḻtu - uppu. kaṇam cāl umaṇar eṉṟamiyiṉumaṇar kūṭṭamākavē varuvar
eṉpatu peṟṟām.ciṉikoḷ tī nīr eṉṟamiyāl curattil nīriṉmi peṟappaṭum. paṭarntu -
paṭātalāl.

170. neytal
[talimakaḷ, kāmamikkakaḻipaṭarkiḷaviyāṟ coṟṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 8. alava - naṇṭē!
4-7. iru kaḻi malarnta- kariya nīrōṭiyil malarnta, kaṇ pōlneytal kamaḻ itaḻ nāṟṟam -
kaṇṇippōlumneytaṟ pūviṉ kamaḻum itaḻ nāṟṟattiṉi,amiḻtu eṉa nacii - amiḻtam pōlum
eṉavirumpic ceṉṟu, taṇ tātu ūtiya - kuḷirntatātiṉi uṇṭa, vaṇṭu iṉam kaḷi
ciṟantu - vaṇṭiṉkūṭṭam kaḷippumikku, paṟiiya taḷarum tuṟivaṉi -paṟattaṟku iyalātu
cōrum tuṟiyiyuṭiya talivaṉukku ;
1-2. kāṉalum kaḻaṟātu -kaṭaṟkaric cōliyum ceṉṟu tūtu kūṟātu, kaḻiyumkūṟātu -
uppaṅkaḻiyum iyampātu, tēṉ imirnaṟumalarp puṉṉiyum moḻiyātu - vaṇṭukaḷolikkum
naṟiya malariṉiyuṭiya puṉṉiyummoḻiyātu;
3. oru niṉ allatupiṟitu yātumilaṉ - niṉṉiyē yaṉṟi vēṟuettuṇiyum illēṉ;
9-14. kitiam paṭuciṉi - tāḻi marattiṉ tāḻnta kiḷiyil, kāmarpeṭiyoṭu evvamoṭu
acāam - viruppam mikkapeṭiyuṭaṉ varuttamuṭaṉ taḷarntirukkum, kaṭalciṟu kākki -
ciṟiya kaṭaṟkākki, kōṭṭu mīṉvaḻaṅkum vēṭṭam maṭi parappiṉ - cuṟāmīṉiyaṅkum
vēṭṭiyāṭutal nīṅkiya iṭattiluḷḷa,veḷ iṟā kaṉavum - veḷḷiya iṟāmīṉip paṟṟiyuṇpatākak
kaṉavu kāṇum, naḷ eṉ yāmattu - iruḷceṟinta naṭuiravil vantu, palkāl niṉ uṟuviḻumam
kaḷintōḷ - pala nāḷum niṉatu mikkatuyariṉi nīkkiyōḷ, taṉ uṟu viḻumam nīntumōeṉavē -
niṉ pirivāl eytiya taṉatu mikkatuyariṉik kaṭakka vallaḷō eṉṟu ;
7-8. nīyē collalvēṇṭum - at talivaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu nīyē kūṟutalvēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) alava! tuṟivaṉukkuk kāṉaluṅkaḻaṟātu ; kaḻiyuṅ kūṟātu ;puṉṉiyum
moḻiyātu ; oru niṉṉallatu piṟituyātum ilaṉ; naḷḷeṉ yāmattu vantu pal
nāḷumniṉṉuṟu viḻumam kaḷintōḷ, taṉṉuṟu viḻumamnīntumō eṉa nīyē collal vēṇṭum.
(vi - ri.) kāṉal, kaḻi, puṉṉi eṉpaṉa cāṉṟākat talivareṉṉik kalantu ceṉṟārāyiṉum,
avi yellāmtalivaṉip pirintu eytum varuttatti uṇaravallaṉaaṉmiyāṉum, orukāl
uṇariṉum ceṉṟukūṟamāṭṭātaṉa ākalāṉum nīyē tuṟivaṉukkuccollalvēṇṭum eṉat talivi
alavaṉi nōkkikkūṟiṉāḷeṉka. kaṇpōl neytal eṉṟatu, 1'poruḷēuvamam ceytaṉar
moḻiyiṉum' eṉpataṉālamintatu. tuṟivaṉi - tuṟivaṉukku. kākki peṭiyoṭuveḷ iṟāk
kaṉavum yāmam eṉka. kōṭṭu mīṉvaḻaṅkum parappu eṉavum, vēṭṭamaṭi parappu eṉavumiyiyum.
niṉṉuṟu viḻumam kaḷintōḷeṉṟamiyāl nī avaḷatu viḻumattikkaḷiyātiruttal naṉṟi kōṟalām
eṉpatupaṭaniṉṟatu. iṉi, paṟiiya taḷarum eṉpataṉimuṟṟākkik kāṉal mutaliyaṉa pōlātu vaṇṭu
ceṉṟukūṟutaṟku uriyatēṉum, atu tēṉiyuṇṭa kaḷippālcōrntu kiṭakkum ātaliṉ nīyē
collalvēṇṭum eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām. kāmam mikkakaḻipaṭar kiḷaviyāvatu talivaṉip pirintu
varuntumtalivi, taṉatu vēṭki mikutiyāl, kēḷātaṉa vaṟṟikkēṭpaṉavāka viḷittu,
talivaṉiṭattuc ceṉṟu eṉtuṉpatti moḻiyīrō eṉṟu kūṟutalām. nīntumōeṉṟataṉāl viḻumam
kaṭal eṉṟu kuṟittavāṟām.
(u - ṟi.) tātiṉi uṇṭa vaṇṭiṉam kaḷiciṟantu paṟakkamāṭṭātutaḷarum tuṟivaṉ eṉṟatu, tāṉ
peṟṟa vēṟuiṉpattil mayaṅkit taliviyi maṟantirukkumtalivaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
kākki peṭiyoṭuyāmattu iṟāk kaṉavum eṉṟatu, talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭiyiruntu muṉpu
peṟṟa iṉpatti niṉintu varuntukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
(mē - ḷ.) 2'nōyumiṉpamum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷ, 'collāmarapiṉa
colluvaṉavāka aḻuki paṟṟik kūṟiyatu, iviuyar tiṇiyumāyiṉa' eṉṟār.
nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
3'iṉpattiveṟuttal' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu 'niṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅkaḷintōḷ, taṉṉuṟu viḻumam
nīntumō eṉavē'eṉpatu tuṉpattup pulampal eṉṉum meyppāṭuākumeṉṟum, 4'muṭṭuvayiṟ

171. pāli
[talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.]

172. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi talimakaḷi iṭattuuyttuvantu talimakaṉi varivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. vāraṇam uraṟum nīrtikaḻ cilampil - yāṉimuḻaṅkum taṉmi vāynta pakka
maliyil,piracamoṭu viri iya vayaṅku veḷ aruvi - tēṉuṭaṉkalantu varumviḷakkamuṟṟa
veḷḷiya aruvi, iṉiciimiḻ iyam kaṭuppa - iṉṉoliyuṭaṉ muḻaṅkummattaḷatti yoppa, im
eṉa kalmuki viṭar akamcilampa vīḻum - immeṉum oliyuṭaṉ maliyiṉkukikaḷum
piḷappiṭaṅkaḷum olikka viḻum, kāmputalimaṇanta ōṅku malic cāral - mūṅkilneruṅkiya
uyarnta maliccāralil uḷḷa ;
5-14. irumpuvaṭittaṉṉa karuṅkik kāṉavaṉ - irumpiṉivārttuc ceytāṟ pōṉṟavaliya
kiyiṉiyuṭiyavēṭṭuvaṉ, viri malar marāam porunti -virinta malariṉiyuṭiya
veṇkaṭampiṉic cārntuniṉṟu, kōl terintu - ampiṉi āyntukoṇṭuvarinutal yāṉi aru
niṟattu aḻutti - variporuntiya neṟṟiṉiyuṭiya kaḷiṟṟiṉ ariyamārpiṟ celutti, ikal
aṭu muṉpiṉ veṇkōṭukoṇṭu - pakiyiṉik kollum valiyiṉiyuṭiyaataṉ veḷḷiya
kompiṉik koṇṭuvantu, taṉpulvēy kurampi pulara ūṉṟi - taṉatu ūkam pullālvēynta
kuṭiciyil pulāl nāṟṟam vīca ūṉṟutalceytu, muṉṟil nīṭiya muḻavu uṟaḻ palaviṉ
piḻimakiḻ uvakiyaṉ - ak kuṭici muṉṟilil uyarntamuḻavu pōlum palāk kaṉiyiṉiṉṟu
piḻanteṭuttamatuviṉi uṇṭa kaḷippiṉaṉāki, kiḷiyoṭu kaliciṟantu - cuṟṟattuṭaṉ
āravārammikku, cāntañekiḻiyiṉ uṉpuḻukku ayarum- cantaṉaviṟakālāyatīyil ūṉuṭaṉ kūṭiya
cōṟṟi aṭṭuṇṇum iṭamākiya,kuṉṟanāṭa - kuṉṟam poruntiya nāṭṭiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
14-18. nī aṉpiliākutal aṟiyēṉ yāṉ - nī aṉpilāy ātali yāṉmuṉpu aṟintilēṉ,
aḵtu aṟintaṉeṉ āyiṉ - ataṉi yāṉ aṟintuḷēṉāyiṉ, aṇiiḻi āy itaḻ uṇkaṇ
kuṟumakaḷ - aḻakiyaaṇikalaṉiyum aḻakiya itaḻiṉiyuṭiya miyuṇṭakaṇkaḷiyum uṭiya iḷiyaḷāya
eṉ taliviyiṉ, maṇiēr māṇ nalam citiya - nīlamaṇi pōlum ciṟantaaḻakukeṭa, poṉ nēr
pacali pāiṉṟu maṉ - poṉṉiotta pacali peritum parattal illiyākum.
(muṭipu) kuṉṟa nāṭa! nī aṉpili yākutal aṟiyēṉ : aḵtuaṟintaṉeṉ āyiṉ kuṟumakaḷ
māṇalam citiya pacalimaṉpā iṉṟu. aruvi immeṉa vīḻum cāralilkāṉavaṉ yāṉikkōṭu koṇṭu
kurampi ūṉṟiccānta ñekiḻiyiṉ ūṉpuḻukku ayarum kuṉṟameṉka.
(vi - ri.) nīr - cuṉi nīrumām. mukiyākiya viyareṉṟalumām. aruvi vīḻum maliccāral
eṉavum,kāmpu talimaṇanta malic cāral eṉavum taṉittaṉikūṭṭuka. yāṉiyik koṉṟu ataṉ
kōṭu koḷḷumvaliya ki yeṉpatu tōṉṟa irumpu vaṭittaṉṉa karuṅkieṉappaṭṭatu. marāam
poruntutal, yāṉi taṉiruppiyaṟintu iriyātavāṟu patuṅki niṟṟal.iravukkuṟiyiṉ ētam
añcit talivivaruntutaliyum, ūril alar eḻutaliyum karutivirintu varintu koḷḷātu
kaḷavoḻukkattilēyētāḻntiruttalpaṟṟit talivaṉi 'aṉpili'eṉṟum, nī ivvāṟu oḻukuti
eṉpataṉi muṉṉaraṟintiruppēṉāyiṉ, yāṉ kūṭṭattiṟkuuṭaṉpaṭṭirē ṉākaliṉ, varivu
nīṭṭittalkāraṇamākat talivi nalaṉcitiyap pacali paṭartalilliyākum eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ
eṉka.
(u - ṟi.) piracamoṭu viriiya aruvi viṭarakam cilampa vīḻumeṉṟatu, taliviyuṭaṉ kūṭiya
talivaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkamūreṅkum alaruṇṭāka nikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu.yāṉi veṇkōṭu koṇṭu
kāṉavaṉ kurampi pularūṉṟi uvakiyaṉ kaliciṟantu cānta ñekiḻiyiṉūṉpuḻuk kayarum
eṉṟatu, talivaṉ ariyaḷāya taliviyieḷiyaḷ eṉa matittuk kaḷavoḻukkamākiyiṉpattilē
tarukkiyiruttal eṉṟavāṟu.

173. pāli

(co - ḷ.) 8, tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-4. aṟamtalippiriyātu oḻukalum - aṟaneṟiyiṉiṉṟumnīṅkātu ilvāḻkki naṭattalum,

añci, uḻimāṉ ampiṇi iṉaṉ irintu ōṭa- āṇmāṉum peṇmāṉum ākiya iṉaṅkaḷ
keṭṭuōṭavum, kāṭu kaviṉ aḻiya - kāṭukaḷ aḻaku keṭavum,kōṭi urii - kōṭi paravi,
niṉṟu tiṉa viḷintaam paṇi neṭuvēy - nilipeṟṟu nīriṉiuṟiñcutalāl vaṟṟiya aḻakiya

tōṟṟamuṭiyaṉavāki, paḻaṅkuḻi tāam - (aṅkuḷḷārkaḻaṅkāṭiya) paḻiya kuḻiyilē viḻum
(iṭamākiya), iṉkaḷi naṟaviṉ iyaltēr naṉṉaṉ - iṉiyakaḷippittarum kaḷḷiṉiyum
iyaṟṟappaṭṭatēriṉiyumuṭiya naṉṉaṉatu, viṇporu neṭuvarikkavāṉ - vāṉi aḷāviya nīṇṭa
mūṅkiliyuṭiya pakkamaliyiṉiyum, poṉpaṭu maruṅkiṉ mali iṟantōr- poṉ poruntiya
iṭaṅkaḷiyumuṭiya maliyiṉikkaṭantu ceṉṟōrāya nam talivar;
4-8. naṟunutal mi īrōti - naṟiya neṟṟiyiyum kariya periya kūntaliyumuṭiyāḷē,
arumpaṭar uḻattal - ariya tuṉpattālvaruntutali, cilnāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉṟu
-cilakālam poṟuttuk kōṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟi,niṉ nal māṇ elvaḷi tiruttiṉarāyiṉ -
niṉatunalla māṇpuṟṟa oḷi poruntiya vaḷiyiṉittiruttit taliyaḷi ceytaṉarātalāl, varuvar
-virivil vantuṟuvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! ceyviṉi purinta neñciṉarāy,maliyiṟantōrākiya nam talivar,
arumpaṭaruḻattal ciṉṉāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉelvaḷi tiruttiṉarātaliṉ, (virivil)
varuvar.
(vi - ri.) naṟunutal, ōti eṉpavaṟṟiṟku, nutali nīvutalumōtiyik kōtutalum ceytaṉar
eṉak kuṟippiṟporuḷ koḷḷalumām; 1'valimuṉpiṉ' eṉṟa pālikkaliyil, 'tāḻ
katuppuaṇikuvar kātalar 'oḷḷiḻi tiruttuvar kātalar,oṇṇutal nīvuvar kātalar' eṉa varutal
kāṇka.tiruttiṉarāyiṉ - tiruttiṉarātaliṉ eṉka.ciṉṉāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟi
iṅṅaṉamtaliyaḷi ceytu akaṉṟārātaliṉ, avar niṉṉiniṉintu virivil vārā

174. mulli
[pācaṟikkaṇ talimakaṉtaṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. iru peru vēntar māṟu koḷ viyaṉkaḷattu -pēraracar iruvar māṟupāṭu
koṇṭu porum periyapōrkkaḷattē, oru paṭi koṇṭu varupaṭipeyarkkum - tamatu
oppaṟṟapaṭikkalattikkoṇṭu etir varum paṭikaḷippiṟakkiṭac ceyyum, celvam
uṭiyōrkkuviṟal niṉṟaṉṟu eṉa - veṟṟiyākiya celvamuṭiyōrkku ipperumi nilipeṟṟatu
eṉṟu kūṟi,pūkkōḷ ēya taṇṇumi vilakki - taṇṇumimuḻakkuṭaṉ porunta aracaṉ
aḷikkumpūkkōḷiṉiyum ēlātu tavirttu, celvēm ātalaṟiyāḷ - nām taṉpāl virintu
cellutaliaṟiyāḷāy;
10-14. vēṅki ūḻ uṟunaṟuvī kaṭuppak kēḻ koḷa - vēṅkiyiṉmalartaluṟṟa naṟiya malari oppa
naṉṉiṟam koḷḷa,ākattu arumpiya mācu aṟu cuṇaṅkiṉaḷ - mēṉiyiṟṟōṉṟiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa
tēmaliyuṭiyaḷāy, nal maṇalviyaliṭi naṭanta - nalla maṇaliyuṭiya akaṉṟaviṭattēnaṭanta cilmel
otukkiṉ māayōḷ - cilavāyamelliya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya māmi niṟattiṉiyuṭiya namtalivi ;
5-10. nēr kālmulli mutukoṭi kuḻippa - nirampiya kālkaḷiṟpaṭarnta mutiya
mullikkoṭi taḷirkka, kālivāṉattuk kaṭuṅkural koṇmū - kīḻtticiyilvāṉatteḻunta
kaṭiya kuraliyuṭiya mēkam, nīrcorintu muḻaṅkutoṟum - nīrip peytu muḻaṅkuntōṟum,
nappulantu kiyaṟṟu oṭuṅki - nammiveṟuttuc ceyalaṟṟu oṭuṅki, paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa
pacalimēṉiyaḷ - tuṉpiṉi eytiya pacali pāyntamēṉiyaḷāy, yāṅku ākuvaḷ kāl -
enniliyiṉiaṭivāḷō?
(muṭipu) neñcē! nām pūkkōḷ ēya taṇṇumi vilakkiccelvēmātali aṟiyāḷāya māyōḷ,
koṇmū nīrcorintu muḻaṅku toṟum kiyaṟṟu oṭuṅkinammip pulantu pacali mēṉiyaḷāki
yāṅkākuvaḷkollō!
(vi - ri.) varupaṭi peyarkkum celvam eṉṟamiyālcelvameṉpatu veṟṟiyi uṇarttiṟṟu.
viṟal -mēṉmi. māṟṟāri veṉṟa veṟṟivīrarkkuaracaṉ poṟpū aḷittuc ciṟappuc
ceyvaṉ;appūviṉip peṟutal pūkkōḷākum. taṇṇumi ēyapūkkōḷ eṉa māṟuka. pakiyi veṉṟu
aracaṉaḷikkum ciṟappiṉip peṟutaṟkum tāḻttunillātu nām celvēmāyiṉum kuṟitta
paruvamvantamiyiṉ talivi eṅṅaṉam ākuvaḷō eṉṟutalivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc collic
celavuvituppuṟukiṉṟāṉ eṉka. kāli vāṉam - ñāyiṟutōṉṟum kīḻttici vāṉam eṉka. tāṉ
avaḷippiriyum ñāṉṟu maṇaṟparappilē avaḷ kuṟukaaṭiyiṭṭu naṭanta meṉṉaṭi avaṉ
neñcamviṭṭakaṉṟila tākaliṉ, 'naṉmaṇal viyaliṭinaṭanta, ciṉmel lotukkiṉ māayōḷ'eṉṟāṉ.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'ēvaṉmarapiṉ' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷmīḷvāṉ taṉ neñciṟku urittatu
eṉṟum,itaṉuḷ pūkkōḷēya taṇṇumi vilakkic celvēmeṉṟaliṉ, aracaṉāṟ ciṟappup peṟṟa
-talivaṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟum, 2'oṉṟāttamariṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ākit tōṉṟumpāṅkōr
pāṅkiṉum eṉṉun tuṟikku, tamakkuākkam ciṟanta naṭpuṭiyōrākit tōṉṟumnaṭpuṭiyōrkku
uṟṟuḻi yutavac cēṟaṟkaṇṇumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, ic ceyyuḷ ataṟkuutāraṇamākum eṉṟum
kūṟiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

175. pāli
[piriviṉkaṇvaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ
tōḻikkuccoṟṟatū umām.]

(co - ḷ.) 9. tōḻi-,
1-8. vīṅku viḷimpuurīiya - tamatu taṭitta tōḷiṉ viḷimpiṉi uraciya,vici ami nōṉ
cili - vēkam aminta valiyavillil vittu, vāṅku toṭi piḻiyā vaṉkaṇāṭavar - iḻuttu
viṭum ampu kuṟi tapputal illātataṟukaṇmiyiyuṭiya maṟavar, viṭutoṟum
viḷikkumvevvāy vāḷi - eyyuntōṟum olittuccellum koṭiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya ampu,
āṟu celvampalar uyir celap peyarppiṉ -vaḻiccellum putiyaratu uyirkeṭappōkkutalāl,
pāṟu kiḷi payirntu paṭumuṭi kavarum -paruntu taṉ kiḷiyiṉi aḻittu mikka
pulāliuṇṇum, vem curam iṟanta kātalar - koṭiya curaneṟiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam kātalar,
neñcu uṇara -tamatu neñcu uṇara, ariya vañciṉam colliyum -kaṭiya cūḷ urittum, palmāṇ
terivaḷi muṉkipaṟṟiyum - paṉmuṟi ārāynta vaḷiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉatumuṉ kiyiṉip paṟṟit
taliyaḷi ceytum ;
10-18. taṇ peyaleḻili - taṇṇiya maḻiyip peyyum mēkam, kāliyal neṭu tēr kivaṇ
ceḻiyaṉ - kāṟṟuppōliyalum neṭiya tēriṉiyum kivaṇmiyiṉiyumuṭiyapāṇṭiyaṉ
neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, ālaṅkāṉattu amarkaṭantu uyartta - taliyālaṅkāṉattup pōriveṉṟu uyartta,
vēliṉum pal ūḻ miṉṉi -vēṟpaṭikaḷik kāṭṭil paṉmuṟi miṉṉi, muracu eṉamāiru
vicumpil kaṭiiṭi payiṟṟi - kariyaperiya vāṉiṉkaṇ (avaṉatu veṉṟu eṟi) muracam
eṉakkaṭiya iṭiyiṉip palakālum tōṟṟu vittu, nērkatir niritta nēmi am celvaṉ -
nirampiyakatirkaḷiṉ oḻuṅkiṉik koṇṭa āḻiyiyuṭiyatirumāliṉ, pōr aṭaṅku akalam
poruntiyatārpōl - pakivar pōr oḻitaṟkuk kāraṇamāyamārpiṉiṭattē taṅkiya mālipōla,
urukeḻutiruvil tēettuk kulii - paṉṉiṟam vāyntaaḻakiya villi avvicumpiṉiṭattē
vaḷittu, maṇpayam pūppap pāay - nilam payaṉittarap paravi,tāḻnta pōḻtu - iṟaṅkip
peyyuṅ kālattu;
8-9. viṉi muṭittuvarutum eṉṟaṉar aṉṟē - tāmcellum viṉiyi muṭittukkoṇṭu
mīṇṭum varuvēm eṉṟāraṉṟō?(aṅṅaṉam vantilarē ; eṉ ceyval!)
(muṭipu) veñcuramiṟanta kātalar, vañciṉam colliyum, muṉkipaṟṟiyum ; taṇpeyal eḻili
tāḻnta pōḻtuvarutum eṉṟaṉarē? (aṅṅaṉam vantilarē; eṉceyval!)
(vi - ri.) viḷimpu - tōliṉ mēṟpuṟam, peyarppiṉ -peyarttalāl. avar kūṟiya
vañciṉattiṟku avarneñcē cāṉṟāka amiyum eṉpāḷ, 'neñcuṇara'eṉṟāḷ;
1'neñcattukkuṟukiyakariyilli eṉap piṟacāṉṟōr kūṟutalum kāṇka.muṉki paṟṟi yeṉpataṉi
vēṟu pirittu vañciṉamcolli eṉpataṉōṭu kūṭṭi yurittalumām. muṉkipaṟṟi vañciṉam
kūṟum vaḻakkuṇmi. 2'nēriṟimuṉki paṟṟic, cūrara makaḷiro ṭuṟṟa cūḷē'eṉpataṉāl
aṟiyappaṭum. kariya vāṉukku nēmiyañcelvaṉatu akalamum, avvāṉiṟṟōṉṟiya
villukkuavvakalattiṟ poruntiya tārum uvamam. tēem -iṭam, vāṉiṉ iṭam kārpparuvat
toṭakkattuvarutum eṉṟavar vārāmiyāl, yāṉ eṉceyvēṉeṉat tōḻikkut talimakaḷ kūṟiṉāḷ.

176. marutam
[tōḻi, talimakaṉi vāyiṉmaṟuttatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-12. kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ - kaṭalikkaṇṭāṟpōṉṟa iṭamakaṉṟa
nīrpparappil,nilampaka vīḻnta vērmutir kiḻaṅkiṉ- nilampiḷakkumāṟu iṟaṅkiya vēril
mutirntakiḻaṅkiṉiyum, kaḻi kaṇṭaṉṉa tūmpu uṭi tiraḷkāl - mūṅkilik kaṇṭālotta
tuḷiyuṭiya tiraṇṭataṇṭiṉiyum, kaḷiṟṟuc cevi aṉṉa pācaṭimaruṅkil - kaḷiṟṟiṉ
cevipōṉṟa paciyiliyiṭattē, kaḻu nivantaṉṉa koḻumuki iṭiiṭi - kaḻuuyarntu tōṉṟali
yotta ceḻittamoṭṭukkaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē, muṟuval mukattiltayaṅkap pūtta paṉmalar -
puṉcirippiṉiyuṭiyamukam pōla viḷaṅkap pūtta pala malarkaḷiyumuṭiya,tāmari puḷ imiḻ
paḻaṉattu - tāmariyiṉiyuṭiyapuṭkaḷ olikkum vayalil uḷḷa, vēppu naṉi aṉṉaneṭukaṇ nīr
ñeṇṭu - vēmpiṉ arumpiṉiyotta nīṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya nīr ñeṇṭāṉatu(parattamikkup
pirintu pōyatu.) iri tēr veṇkuruku añci - iriyiṉi āyntu pārttiruntaveḷḷiya
nāriyiṉik kaṇṭu añci, ayalatu olittapakaṉṟi iru cēṟṟu aḷḷal - ayaliluḷḷa
taḻittacivatiyiṉiyuṭiya kariya cēṟṟup piḻampil,titaliyiṉ varippa ōṭi - tēmalpōla
variyuṇṭākōṭicceṉṟu, virintu taṉ īr mali maṇ aḷicceṟiyum ūra - virintu taṉatu
īram mikka maṇaḷiyuṭ patuṅkum ūraṉē!
13-8. maṉinaku maraṉivar vayali koḻuṅkoṭi - maṉiyiṉ kaṇ viḷaṅkummarattiṟ paṭarum
vayaliyākiya koḻuviya koṭiyiṉi,arimalar āmpaloṭu ārtaḻi tii - viḷaṅkummalarkaḷiyuṭiya
āmpaloṭu cērttuk kaṭṭiyataḻiyuṭiyi uṭuttu, viḻuvu āṭu makaḷiroṭu
taḻūuaṇipolintu - viḻāviṉ kaṇ āṭum makaḷiroṭu taḻuviāṭum aṇiyāṟ polivuṟṟu,
malar ēr uṇkaṇmāṇ iḻi kuṟuntoṭi muṉki tuṭakkiya - malariyotta miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyum
māṇpuṟṟaaṇiyiṉiyum uṭiya niṉ paratti taṉatu kuṟiyavaḷiyaṇinta muṉkiyiṉāl paṇitta,
neṭu toṭarviṭuttatu - neṭiya piṇippiṉi viṭuttuc ceṉṟaaḷaviṟkē, uṭaṉṟaṉaḷ -
vekuṇṭaṉaḷāy;
21-6. taṉ mukattu eḻutueḻil citiya - taṉ mukattiṉatu eḻutuvataṟku ētuvākiyaaḻaku
keṭa, aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki - aḻutu ēkkamuṟṟu, vaṭittueṉa urutta titti - poṉṉi urukki
vārttāloppa urukkoṇṭa tēmaliyum, pal ūḻnoṭitteṉa civanta melviral -
paṉmuṟimuṟittuk koḷḷaliṉ civanta melliyaviraliṉiyum, tirukupu kūrnuṉi maḻukiya
eyiṟṟaḷ -tirukik kaṭittalāṉ kūriya muṉi maḻuṅkiyeyiṟṟiyumuṭiyaḷāy, ūr muḻutum
nuvalum niṉkāṇiya ceṉmē - ūr eṅkum alar kūṟappaṭumniṉṉik kāṇṭaṟkut tēṭic
celvāḷ (ākaliṉ);
18-21. niṉ kātali -niṉ kātaliyākiya avaḷ, empōl pul uḷikkuṭumip putalvaṉ
payantu - emmippōlap pulliyuḷi pōlum kuṭumiyiyuṭiya putalvaṉippeṟṟu, neluṭi
neṭunakar niṉ iṉṟu uṟiya - nelluṭiya nīṇṭamaṉiyil niṉṉip pirintu taṅka
eṉṉakaṭattaḷō - eṉṉa kaṭappāṭu uṭiyavaḷō?
(muṭipu) ūra! māṇiḻi taṉ muṉki toṭakkiya neṭuntoṭarviṭuttatu uṭaṉṟaṉaḷāy aḻutaṉaḷ
ēṅki, ūr muḻutumnuvalum niṉṉikkāṇiya ceṉmē ākaliṉ niṉkātaliyāya avaḷ empōṟ
putalvaṟ payantuneṭunakar niṉiṉṟu uṟiya eṉṉa kaṭattaḷō?
(vi - ri.) kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa eṉpataṉālum, tāmariyiṉ kiḻaṅku,taṇṭu, ili, muki, malar eṉṟa
ivaṟṟiṟkuk kūṟiyuvamikaḷālum marutanilattiṉ koḻumiyum nīrmikutiyum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
neṭuṅkaṇ -neṭitāy nivantu tōṉṟum kaṇ. vayalik koṭimaṉiyiṉkaṇ vittu
vaḷarkkappaṭutalāl, maṉinakuvayali eṉappaṭṭatu. uṇ kaṇṇiṉiyummāṇiḻiyiṉiyumuṭiya niṉ
kātaliyeṉakkūṭṭiyurittalu mām. pōlum : oppil pōli.maṟṟu, viṉimāṟṟu. eḻutu
eḻil - ōviyar pārttueḻututaṟku ētuvākiya aḻaku. viralkaḷip palamuṟinoṭittalum,
eyiṟṟiṉit tirukutalumciṉattiṉāl nikaḻntaṉa. avaḷkippiṇippiṉic ciṟitu viṭutta
aḷaviṟkēuṭaṉṟu niṉṉit tēṭit tirivāḷ eṉṟa miyiṉ'avaḷatu poṟumiyiṉmiyiyum. yām
maṉiyiṉkaṇniṉṉi yiṉṟiyē uṟikkiṉṟōm eṉṟataṉāl,tamatu poṟumiyiyum talivi
pulappaṭuttiṉāḷ eṉka.nelluṭi neṭunakar eṉa viruntōmpaṟkuiṉṟiyamiyāta neluluṭimi
kūṟiya ataṉāṉē, yāmputalvaṟ payantum viruntōmpiyumniṉakkumirumikkum ētuvākiya
maṉiyaṟattiṉ vaḻuvātoḻukukiṉṟēm eṉṟu talivi kuṟittāḷām,putalvaṟ payantu eṉṟatu,
mutumiyi uḷḷip parattiyiṟ pirintāṉ eṉappulappaṭuttapaṭiyumām. ūr muḻutum nuvalum
eṉṟamiyāl, piṟar kūṟum paḻi karuti ippoḻutuīṇṭu varukiṉṟāyaṉṟi aṉpiṉāl
varukiṉṟāyalli eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi vāyil maṟuttāḷām. ceṉmē - cellum;
īṟṟumiciyukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭatu.
talivikkum taṉakkum uḷḷa orumippāṭṭiṉāl tōḻi,empōl eṉat taliviyik kūṟiṉāḷ.
itaṉittalivi kūṟṟākavē koḷvatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkum pērāciriyarkkum karuttātal
tolkāppiya uriyāl aṟiyappaṭum.
(u - ṟi.) puṉimiḻ paḻaṉattu ñeṇṭu kurukañci iruñcēṟṟaḷḷal titaliyiṉ varippa ōṭi
maṇṇaḷicceṟiyum eṉṟatu, parattiyar cērikkaṇ avarnalattiṉi nukarntirunta talivaṉ alar
eḻutaliañci apparattiyar ceyta kuṟi, taṉ mārpilē kiṭappattaṉ maṉikkaṇ virintu
varalāyiṉaṉ eṉṟapaṭi.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'avaṉaṟivāṟṟa aṟiyu mākaliṉ' eṉṉum cūttirattu, 'pukaṉṟa
uḷḷamoṭu....īrattu maruṅkiṉum' eṉṉumtuṟikkaṇ, ic ceyyuḷi utāraṇamākak
kāṭṭi,maṟuppāḷpōla nayantāḷāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
2'iḷivēyiḻavē...aḻuki nāṉkē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattueḻuteḻil citiya aḻutaṉaḷ
ēṅki...niṟkāṇiyaceṉmē' eṉpatu, taṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya iḷivaralporuḷāka avalaccuvi piṟantatu
eṉṟum, 3'teyvamañcal' eṉṉum cūttirattu, 'vēmpu naṉiyaṉṉa... ceṟiyum ūra'
eṉpatu,talimakaṉ vāyil vēṇṭac ceṉṟāṉip piṟarkūṟum paḻikku vantāy eṉṟamiyiṉ, iḵtu
uḷḷatuvarttal (eṉum meyppāṭu) āyiṟṟu eṉṟumkūṟiṉar, pērā.

177. pāli
[piriviṭivēṟupaṭṭatalimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 6-12. tōḻi-, kāṇpu iṉ kaḻi amalcilam piṉ vaḻitalivāṭa - kāṇṭaṟku iṉiya
mūṅkil niṟintapakkamaliyiluḷḷa curapuṉṉiyiṉ ucci vāṭumpaṭi,katir katam kaṟṟa -
ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirciṉattip payiṉṟa, ē kal neṟiyiṭi – perukiya kaṟkaḷ poruntiya
curaneṟiyil,piṅkoṭip pākal ceṅkaṉi nacii – paciya pākaṟkoṭiyiṉ civanta paḻattiṉi
virumpi,kāṉamaññi kamam cūl mā peṭi - kāṭṭiluḷḷamayiliṉ niṟinta karuviṉik koṇṭa
kariya peṭi,ayiri yāṟṟu aṭikari vayiriṉ naralum,ayiriyāṟṟiṉatu aṭikariyiṉkaṇ
ūtukompeṉolikkum-kāṭu iṟantu akaṉṟōr nīṭiṉar āyiṉum- kāṭṭiṉit tāṇṭic ceṉṟa
talivar (kuṟittaparuvaṅ kaṭantu) tāḻttu vārātiruppiṉum;
3-6. ciṟukaṇ iru piṭitaṭaki māṉa - ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya periyapeṇyāṉiyiṉ periya
kiyiṉi oppa, oruṅkupiṇittu iyaṉṟa ney aruntu veṟikoḷ aimpāl- cērakkaṭṭi viṭṭa
ney pūcappeṟumnaṟunāṟṟamuṭiya kūntaṟkaṇṇē, tēm kamaḻveṟimalar peymmār - tēṉ maṇakkum
naṟiyamalarip peytaliyum;
13-20. veṇvēliliniṟam peyara ōcci - veṉṟi vēli ilimuṉiniṟam māṟic
cenniṟamaṭiyac celutti,māṟṟōr mali maruḷ yāṉi maṇṭu amaroḻitta -pakivaruṭiya
malipōlumyāṉikaḷi mikkapōriṉkaṇ aḻitta, kaḻalkāl paṇṇaṉ -vīrakkaḻal taritta kāliṉiyuṭiya
paṇṇaṉatu, kāvirivaṭavayiṉ - kāviriyiṉ vaṭapāluḷḷa, niḻal kayam taḻīiyaneṭukāl māviṉ -
kuḷirnta kuḷattiṉi yaṭuttuḷḷaneṭiya aṭimarattiṉiyuṭiya māmarattiṉ, taḷir ērākam
takipeṟa mukinta- taḷiri yotta ākattilaḻakupeṟa arumpiya, aṇaṅku uṭi niṉ vaṉamuli
tāya -varuttum iyalpuṭiya niṉ aḻakiya muliyiṟ paranta,cuṇaṅkiṭi toyyili varittal
niṉintē -cuṇaṅkiṉiṭiyē toyyil eḻututaliyum niṉintu;
vallē varuvar -virintu vantaruḷvar ātaliṉ;
1-3. tolnalamcitiyac cāay - paḻiya aḻaku citintiṭa varunti,alkalum - nāṭōṟum,
iṉṉum vārār - iṉṉumvantilar, iṉi evaṉ ceyku eṉa - iṉi eṉṉaceyvēṉ eṉṟu, perum
pulampu uṟutal ōmpumati -mikka varuttam aṭitalip parikarippāyāka:
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāṭiṟantakaṉṟōr nīṭiṉar āyiṉum,niṉatu aimpāl veṟimalar peytaliyum,
niṉvaṉamulit tāaya cuṇaṅkiṭit toyyileḻututaliyum niṉintu vallēvaruvar: ātaliṉ,
nalamcitiyac cāay, alkalum perum pulampu uṟutalōmpumati.
(vi - ri.) taṭakki māṉap piṇittiyaṉṟa aimpāl eṉka,arunti eṉpatu aruntu eṉat
tirintateṉakkoṇṭu, arunti veṟikoḷ eṉṟalumām. katamkaṟṟa - vemmi muṟukiya eṉṟapaṭi.
e kal neṟi: ē -perukka mātali, 1'ēpeṟṟākum' eṉpataṉ uriyāl aṟika; ēkal neṟi
-uyarcciyiyuṭiya neṟiyeṉṟumām. 1'ēkalveṟpaṉ' eṉpatu kāṇka. peṭi vayiriṉ
naraluṅkāṭu eṉka. ayiriyāṟu tamiḻnāṭṭiṉvaṭakkaṇṇuḷḷatōr yāṟu eṉpatu
2'vaṭukarperumakaṉ, pērici yerumi naṉṉāṭṭuḷḷati,ayiriyā ṟiṟantaṉar āyiṉum'
eṉpataṉālaṟiyappaṭum. pōlum: oppil pōli, malimaruḷ:maruḷ, uvamaccol. kāviri
vaṭavayiṉ ciṟukuṭieṉṉum ūriluḷḷa māviṉ taḷir eṉaviritturikka. paṇṇaṉatu ūr
ciṟukuṭi eṉpatu, 3'taṉakkeṉavāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ, paṇṇaṉ ciṟukuṭippaṭappi'
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.

178. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi varivu malintucolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 16-, tōḻi-,
1-13. vayirattu aṉṉavi ēntu maruppiṉ - vayiratti otta kūriyamēṉōkkiya kōṭṭiṉiyum,
vetirvēr aṉṉaparūu mayirppaṉṟi - mūṅkil vēriṉi ottaparutta mayiriṉiyumuṭiya
paṉṟiyāṉatu, paṟikkaṇaṉṉa niṟic cuṉi paruki - paṟiyiṉ kaṇṇiṉi ottaniṟinta cuṉi
nīrik kuṭittu, nīlattu aṉṉa akalilic cēmpiṉ - nīlamaṇiyi yottaniṟattiṉiyuṭiya akaṉṟa
iliyiṉiyuṭiya cēmpiṉ,piṇṭam aṉṉa koḻu kiḻaṅku mānti - piṇṭittuvittāṟpōṉṟa
vaḷaviya kiḻaṅkiṉi niṟiyattiṉṟu, piṭi maṭintaṉṉa kalmici - peṇyāṉikiṭantālotta
kalliṉmītu, ūḻ iḻipu -varuttamiṉṟi muṟiyāka iṟaṅkivantu, yāṟucērntaṉṉa ūṟu nīr paṭā
ar – yāṟṟiṉi aṭuttiruppatu pōṉṟa nīr ūṟum iṭattiluḷḷaciṟutūṟākiya, pimputal
nāḷiciṉi kuruku iruntaṉṉa -paciya putariluḷḷa ceṟinta kiḷikaḷil veṇṇāri
yiruntālotta, vaṇpiṇi aviḻntaveṇ kūtāḷattu alaṅku kuli alari tīṇṭi -
veḷḷiyakūtaḷañ ceṭiyiṉ aciyum kottiluḷḷavaḷamporuntiya muki virinta
malariṉipporunti, tātu uka - ataṉ pūmpoṭi mēlēutirtalāl, poṉ uri kaṭṭaḷi kaṭuppak
kāṇvara- poṉ urittu māṟṟu aṟiyum kaṭṭaḷikkalliṉi oppa aḻakupeṟat tōṉṟi, kiḷi
amalciṟutiṉi viḷikural mēyntu - kiḷittal mikkaciṟiya tiṉiyiṉ viḷinta katiriṉi
mēyntu, kaṇ iṉitu paṭukkum nalmali nāṭaṉoṭu - iṉitu kaṇṇuṟaṅkum iṭamākiya nalla
malināṭṭiyuṭiyāṉoṭu;
14-6. uṇarntaṉipuṇarnta nīyum - ūṭip piṉ avvūṭali nīṅkippuṇarntiṭum nīyum, niṉ
tōḷ paṇikkaviṉaḻiyātu - niṉatu tōḷiṉ mūṅkil pōlum aḻakuoḻiyātu, eṉṟum tuṇi
puṇarntu - niṉ talivaṉuṭaṉeṉṟum cērntu vāḻntu piṉ, taval ilulakattu uṟiiyar -
eṉṟuṅkeṭāta maṟumi yulakattut taṅkivāḻ vīrāka;
17-20. elliyumiravum eṉṉātu - pakalum iravum eṉṟillātu, kaleṉa koṇṭal vāṉ maḻi
poḻinta vikaṟi -kalleṉum oliyuṭaṉ mēkam periya maḻiyiccorinta viṭiyaṟ kālattē,
taṇpaṉi aṟciram -kuḷirnta paṉiyiyuṭiya aṟcirak kālam,tamiyōrkku aritu eṉa -
taṉittiru pōrkkuttāṅkaṟkari tākumeṉavuṇarntu. kaṉaviṉum pirivuaṟiyalaṉ -
kaṉaviṉkaṇṇum piritali aṟiyāṉ;
20-2. ataṉ tali -ataṉmēlum, taṉ paṇpiṉāṉ - taṉatunaṟkuṇattiṉāl, muṉ tāṉkaṇṭa
ñāṉṟiṉum -mutaṉ mutal tāṉ kaṇṭa nāḷiṉum, piṉ perituaḷikkum - piṉ enta nāḷiṉum
mikavum aruḷceyvāṉāvaṉ,
(muṭipu) tōḻi! paṉṟi kaṇ iṉitu paṭukkum naṉmalināṭaṉoṭu, eṉṟum puṇarntu
uṟiiyarō; aṟciramtamiyōrkku ariteṉak kaṉaviṉum pirivuaṟiyalaṉ; ataṉtali taṉ
paṇpiṉālē, muṉ kaṇṭañāṉṟiṉum piṉ peritu aḷikkum.
(vi - ri.) vayirattaṉṉa maruppu, vi maruppu, ēntu maruppu, eṉattaṉittaṉi iyiyum.
kuḻintu vaṭṭamāyiruttalpaṟṟic cuṉikkup paṟikkaṇ uvami kūṟappaṭṭatu.paṭāar - ciṟu
tūṟu ; paṭukar eṉṟumām. paṭukar -nīrnili. karuniṟamuṭiya paṉṟiyiṉ uṭampilkūtaḷattiṉ
poṉṉiṟap pūmpoṭi paṭintiruttal,poṉṉiṉ māṟṟaṟiyum kariya kaṭṭaḷik kalliṟpoṉ
urittāṟpōlum eṉa uvamamum poruḷumottu vilaṅkutal kāṇka.
talivaṉmāṭṭuttavaṟilliyāyiṉum avaṉpāl iruppatākēṟaṭṭukkoṇṭu, kaḷavuk kālattum ciṟu
pāṉmiūṭutalum, piṉṉar avvūṭal nīṅkik kūṭutalumtalivimāṭṭuṇmiyiṉ, 'uṇarntaṉi
puṇarnta'eṉṟāḷ. uṇartal - ūṭal nīṅkutal. iṉi,iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutaliya
kūṭṭaṅkaḷāl,talivaṉuṭiya paṇpiṉi iṉituṇarnta nīyumeṉṟurittalumām. nīyum eṉṟa
ummiyāltalivaṉum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. taval il ulakam -keṭutalillāta iṉpattiyuṭiya
maṟumiyulakam.ivvulakilē taṇavāk kātaluṭaṉ kūṭi illaṟam iṉitunaṭātti, naṉmakap peṟṟu
vāḻntōr,maṟumiyilum piriviṉṟi iṉpam tuyppareṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaḷātaliṉ, eṉṟum
tavaliṉulakattu uṟiiyar eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka; 1'immimāṟi maṟumi yāyiṉum, nīyā kiyareṉ
kaṇavaṉi, yāṉākiyarniṉ ṉeñcunēr pavaḷē' 2'immiyulakat ticiyoṭum viḷaṅki, maṟumi
yulakamummaṟuviṉ ṟeytupa, ceṟunarum viḻiyum ceyīrtīrkāṭcic, ciṟuvarp payanta cemma
lōreṉa'eṉpaṉa kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl, talivaṉ variya varutali yuṇarntu tōḻi varivumalintu
kūṟiṉāḷ eṉpatupeṟṟām. aṅṅaṉam talivaṉ varital orutali eṉavaṟpuṟuttutaṟkuk kaṉaviṉum
pirivu aṟiyalaṉ eṉṟum, muṉtāṉ kaṇṭa ñāṉṟiṉum piṉperitaḷikkum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
iyaṟkippuṇarcci toṭaṅki orukālik korukāltalivimāṭṭu aṉpu perukit
taliyaḷiceytuvarutalik kaṇṭuḷāḷ. ākaliṉ, taṉ paṇpiṉāṉē aḷikkum eṉṟaṉaḷ.
(u - ṟi.) paṉṟi cuṉi parukik kiḻaṅku māntit tiṉiviḷikural mēyntu iṉitu
kaṇpaṭukkum nāṭaṉeṉṟatu, talivaṉ tāṉ karutiya poruḷelām peṟṟut taliviyi maṇantu
iṉpantuyttu iṉitu vāḻvāṉ eṉṟapaṭiyām.

179. pāli
[pirivuṇarttiyatalimakaṟkut tōḻi celavu aḻuṅkac colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-10. taliva-, viṇtōy cimiya viṟal varik kavāṉ- vāṉi aḷāviya
ucciyiṉiyuṭiya perumi taṅkiyamaliyiṉatu pakkamalikkaṇṇē, veṇtēr ōṭum
kāykaṭamaruṅkil - pēyt tēr ōṭāniṟkum kāyntakaṟkāṭṭiṉ pakkattē, tuṉi eri paranta
tuṉarumviyaṉ kāṭṭuc ciṟukaṇ yāṉi - virintaneruppup paraviya kiṭṭutaṟkariya
periyakāṭṭiluḷḷa ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya yāṉi, neṭu kinīṭṭi vāṉ vāy tiṟantum -
appēyttēriṉpāl taṉ neṭiya kiyiṉi nīṭṭiyumperiya vāyiṉit tiṟantum - vaṇpuṉal
peṟāatukāṉ pulantu kaḻiyum kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ - vaḷaviyanīriṉip peṟāmal kāṭṭi
veṟuttuk kaḻintupōm iṭam akaṉṟa pālinilattiṉkaṇ, viṭuvāyc ceṅkaṇik koṭuvil
āṭavar - viṭutalvāynta civanta ampiṉiyuṭiya vaḷinta villik koṇṭa maṟavaratu nalnili
poṟittakal nili atara - nalla veṟṟi niliyi eḻutiyanaṭukaṟkaḷ nilikoṇṭa
vaḻikaḷiyuṭiyavāya, arampukoḷ pūcal kaḷiyunar kāṇāc curamcela virumpiṉir āyiṉ -
kuṟumparkaḷ ceyyum pūcali nīkkunarik kāṇāta curaneṟiyik kaṭantu cellavirumpiṉīr
āyiṉ;
10-4. iṉ nakimuruntueṉat tiraṇṭa muḷeyiṟṟut tuvar vāy -iṉiya nakiyiṉiyum
mayiliṟakiṉ aṭiyeṉat tiraṇṭamuṭpōlum kūriya paṟkaḷiyum civanta vāyiṉiyum,kuvaḷi nāḷ
malar puriyum uṇkaṇ - kuvaḷiyiṉ putiyamalari yokkum miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyum uṭiya,
imatiēr vāḷ nutal pulampa - inta matiyiṉi yottoḷi poruntiya
neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiyāḷvarunta, patipeyarntu uṟital numakku ollumō - ippatiyinīṅkippōyt
taṅkutal umakkupporuntuvatāmō?
(muṭipu) taliva!curamcela virumpiṉirāyiṉ, vāḷnutal pulampappatipeyarntu uṟital
numakku ollumō?
veṇtēr ōṭumviyaṉ kāṭṭil, yāṉi, ki nīṭṭiyum vāytiṟantum puṉal peṟātu pulantu
kaḻivatumkoṭuvillāṭavar naṭukal nilipeṟṟaatarkaḷiyuṭiyatum arampukoḷ pūcal
kaḷiyunarillātatum ākiya curam eṉka.
(vi - ri.) koṭuvil āṭavar, karanti maṟavar eṉka, arampu -kuṟumpar, kāṭṭut talivar,
'iṉṉaki...vāṇutalpulampa' eṉṟatu, taliviyiṉ iṉiya nakiyum kūriyapallumtuvar vāyum
uṇkaṇṇum vāṇutalum āya ivi,nummuṉ tōṉṟi nummi aḻuṅkuvippaṉa vākaliṉ,nīr
pirintuṟital ollāteṉṟāḷ eṉṟapaṭi.

180. neytal
[irantu piṉṉiṉṟatalimakaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi talimakaḷikkuṟinayappak kūṟiyatu.
talimakaṉciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻikkuccolliyatūumām.]

(co - ḷ) 1-9. tōḻi-, taki mika kōti āyamoṭu kuvavu maṇal ēṟi -aḻaku mika
māliyiyuṭiya āyattāruṭaṉ (nāṉ) tiraṇṭamaṇal mēṭṭil ēṟi, vī tati kāṉal vaṇṭal uyara
-malar neruṅkiya cōliyil viḷiyāṭalceyyāniṟka, oruvaṉ - oru talivaṉ, kataḻparitiṇtēr
kaṭii vantu - virinta kutiriyiyuṭiyavaliya tēriṉic celutti vantu, taṇ kayattu
amaṉṟoḷpū kuvaḷi - taṇṇiya kuḷattē niṟinta oḷḷiyapūkkaḷiyuṭiya kuvaḷiyiṉ arumpu
alittu iyaṟṟiyacurumpu ār kaṇṇi - arumpiṉi virit tukkaṭṭiyavaṇṭukaḷ moykkum
kaṇṇiyiṉi, piṉṉuppuṟamtāḻak koṉṉē cūṭṭi - eṉatu piṉṉalik koṇṭamutukiṉpāl
tāḻntiṭa yāṉ vēṇṭātē taliyiṟcūṭṭi, nalvaral iḷamuli nōkki - nallavaḷarcciyiyuṭiya
iḷiya muliyip pārttu,neṭituniṉintu nillātu peyarntaṉaṉ - ētō nīḷaniṉintu piṉ
tāḻkkātu pōyiṉaṉ;
9-15. ataṟkē -avvaḷaviṟkē, i aḻuṅkal ūr - inta āravāramikkūr, pulavu nāṟu
irukaḻituḻii paluṭaṉ puḷiṟi koṇṭa- pulāl vīcum periya kaḻiyiṉit tuḻāvippalavakip
puṭkaḷum oruṅkē taṅkiyirukkum, muḷuṭi neṭu tōṭṭut tāḻi maṇantu - muḷḷuṭiya
nīṇṭitaḻiyuṭiya tāḻiyic cārntu, ñāḻaloṭu keḻīi -pulinakak koṉṟiyoṭu porunti,
paṭappi niṉṟamuṭattāḷ puṉṉip poṉ nēr nuṇtātu nōkki- tōṭṭattil niṟkum vaḷinta
tāḷiṉiyuṭiyapuṉṉiyatu poṉṉi yotta nuṇṇiya tātiṉippārttu, eṉṉum nōkkum -
eṉṉiyum nōkkāniṟkum;
1. naki naṉi uṭittu -itu mikavum nakiyiṉit taruva toṉṟākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nāṉ āyamoṭu vaṇṭal ayara, oruvaṉ tērkaṭii vantu, kaṇṇiyiṉip
piṉṉuppuṟam tāḻakkoṉṉē cūṭṭi iḷamuli nōkki, neṭitu niṉintupeyarntaṉaṉ; ataṟkē
inta aḻuṅkalūr puṉṉittātu nōkki eṉṉum nōkkum; itu nakinaṉiyuṭittu.
(vi - ri.) yāṉ virumpātirukkac ceytē cūṭṭiṉāṉeṉpāḷ 'koṉṉē cūṭṭi' yeṉṟāḷ.
piṉavaṉōṭu taṉakku yātoru toṭarpum iṉṟeṉpāḷnillātu peyarntaṉaṉ eṉṟāḷ.
ivvūrpuṉṉiyiṉ tātu nōkki eṉṉiyum nōkkumeṉṟatu, eṉ. mēṉi puṉṉiyiṉ
tātupōlum pacalipaṭarnta teṉak karutukiṉṟatu eṉṟapaṭi; itu kuṟippunuṭpamām.
tōḻiyāṉavaḷ talivi kuṟi nayappakkaruti, iṅṅaṉam paṭittu moḻiyāl kūṟuvē, ikkāṉaliṉkaṇ
iṅṅaṉam vantu celvāṉ talivaṉēyāvāṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaḷāyk kuṟi nērvāḷāmeṉka.
iraṇṭāvatu tuṟiyiṉ karuttu taliviyāṉavaḷtalivaṉ variyātu pirintiruttalāl, taṉ
mēṉipacali pūttaliyum ataṉāl ūriṉkaṇ alareḻutaliyum talimakaṉ kēṭpat
tōḻikkuccolliṉāḷām eṉpatu.

181. pāli
[iṭiccurattu oḻiyakkarutiya neñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 3. neñcē-,
22-6. pakāar paṇṭamnāṟum vaṇṭu aṭar aimpāl - viṟpāratu naṟumaṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ nāṟukiṉṟa
vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum aimpakutiyākiya kūntaliṉiyum, paṇittaki taṭiiyakāṇpu iṉmeltōḷ -
mūṅkiliṉtakutiyiyuṭiyatāy vaḷinta kāṇṭaṟku iṉiyamelliya tōḷiṉiyumuṭiya, aṇaṅkucāl
ariviirunta - aḻaku mikka nam taliviyirunta, maṇam kamaḻmaṟukiṉ maṇal perum kuṉṟu -
maṇam nāṟumterukkaḷiyuṭiya maṇaliyuṭiya periya kuṉṟam;
3-7. āay eyiṉaṉ - āyeyiṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, muruku uṟaḻ muṉpoṭu - murukaṉiotta
valimiyoṭu niṉṟu, oṉṉār ōmpu araṇkaṭanta vīṅku perum tāṉi - pakivar
pātukākkumaraṇkaḷi veṉṟu kaṭanta mikka periya cēṉikaḷiyuṭiya, aṭupōr miñili
ceruviṟku -pōr aṭutalvalla miñili eṉpāṉ ceruviṉ kaṇ,kaḷam civappap porutu -
kurutiyāl kaḷamcivappuṟap porutu, piṉ uṭii vīḻnteṉa -tōṟṟu vīḻntaṉaṉāka ;
7-10. ñāyiṟṟu oḷkatiruruppam putiya - ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷḷiya katirkaḷiṉ veppam (avaṉ
uṭaliṟpaṭātu)maṟiya, ōrāṅku-orupeṟṟiyē, vampap puḷḷiṉ kampalipperuntōṭu - putiya
puṭkaḷiṉ oli poruntiyaperiya kūṭṭam, vicumpiṭitūra āṭi - vicum piṭammaṟiya
vaṭṭamiṭṭup piṉpu, uṭaṉ mocintu -oruṅkukūṭi;
11-9. pūviri akal tuṟi -pūkkaḷ virinta akaṉṟa tuṟi yiṉiyuṭiya, kāvirippēr
yāṟṟu - kāviriyāya periya āṟṟiṉ,
kaṉivicik kaṭunīr - mikkaviciyuṭaṉ kaṭukivarum nīrāṉatu, ayir koṇṭu īṇṭi-
nuṇmaṇalikkoṇṭu tiraḷumpaṭi, ekkar iṭṭa,kuppi veṇmaṇal - mēṭākkiya
veḷḷiyamaṇaṟkuviyaliyum, yāṇar vippiṉ - putuvaruvāyiyumuṭiya ūrkaḷiyuṭiya, vaḷam
keḻu vēntar -celvam mikka cōḻavēntarāṟ purakkappaṭum,ñālam nāṟum nalamkeḻu nalici -
ulakamellāmparavum naṉmi poruntiya naṟpukaḻiyuṭiya, nālmaṟimutunūl mukkaṇ celvaṉ -
nāṉkuvētaṅkaḷāya paḻiya nūli aruḷiya mukkaṇṇiyuṭiyaparamaṉatu, ālamuṟṟam - ālamuṟṟam
eṉṉumiṭattu, kaviṉpeṟatiiya - aḻakupeṟa iyaṟṟappeṟṟa, poykicūḻnta poḻil - poykiyic
cūḻntuḷapoḻiliṉkaṇṇē, maṉi makaḷir - ciṟṟiliḻittuviḷiyāṭum ciṟumikaḷatu, kicey
pāvit tuṟikkaṇ- aḻakuṟac peyyappeṟṟa pāvikaḷiyuṭiya tuṟiyiṉkaṇṇē, iṟukkum - vantu
taṅkuvatum ;
20-2. makara neṟṟivāṉtōy purici - makarak koṭiyiṉiucciyiṟkoṇṭa vāṉittōyum
matiliyum,cikaram tōṉṟāc cēṇ uyar nalil - muṭiaṟiyappaṭātavāṟu cēṇiṉkaṇ uyarnta
nallamāṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiyatum ākiya, pukāarnal nāṭṭatuvē -kāvirip pūm paṭṭiṉattiyuṭiya
nallacōḻanāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇatākum;
1-3. tuṉ arumkāṉamum tuṇital āṟṟāy - cellutaṟku ariyakāṭṭik kaṭakkavum tuṇivuṟal
ceyyāyāy, piṉniṉṟupeyarac cūḻntaṉi āyiṉ - piṉṉē niṉṟupeyarntu mīṇṭiṭak
karutiṉiyāyiṉ, eṉniliurimō - eṉatu inniliyiṉi āṇṭuc ceṉṟu avaṭkuurippāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! arivi irunta maṇaṟperuṅ kuṉṟu pukāarnaṉṉāṭṭatu; nī kāṉam tuṇital
āṟṟāy; piṉniṉṟu peyarac cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ, eṉniliāṇṭuc ceṉṟu, avaṭku urippāyāka.
(vi - ri.) kāṉamum - kāṭṭiṉik kaṭakkavum; ummi eccapporuṭṭu; aci niliyumām.
oṉṉār, ōmparaṇkaṭanta vīṅkuperuntāṉi eṉpataṉāl miñiliyiṉtāṉip perukkamum,
murukuṟaḻ muṉ poṭu porutueṉpataṉāl eyiṉaṉatupōr valiyum peṟappaṭṭaṉa,kaḷaña civappap
porutu eṉṟamiyāl, pakivartāṉiyiyellām koṉṟu piṉ vīḻntamipeṟṟām. kaḷañ civappa
vīḻntaṉaṉ eṉakkoṇṭu, pakivaṉatu vēlāl tākkuṇṭu vīḻntāṉeṉṟurittalumām. eyiṉaṉ
puḷḷiṉpātukāvalaṉākaliṉ, ivaṉ vīḻnta pōḻtupaṟavikaḷellām ivaṉmēl ñāyiṟṟiṉkatirpaṭātu
maṟittaṉa eṉpatu, 1'puḷḷiṟkuēma mākiya perum peyar, veḷḷattāṉi atikaṟ (eyiṉaṟ)
koṉṟu'eṉpataṉālum,1'veḷiyaṉvēṇmāṉ āay eyiṉaṉ...miñiliyoṭu, naṇpakaluṟṟa ceruvil
puṇkūrn, toḷvāḷ mayaṅkamarvīḻnteṉap, puḷḷoruṅku aṅkaṇ vicumpiṉviḷaṅku ñāyiṟu,
oṇkatir teṟāmic ciṟakarilkōli, niḻal ceytu uḻaṟal' eṉpataṉālumaṟiyappaṭum. 'akavunar
vēṇṭiṉ veṇkōṭṭu,aṇṇal yāṉi īyum vaṇmakiḻ, veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉāay eyiṉaṉ' (208)
eṉṟu ivaṉ kūṟap peṟutaliṉīṇṭu 'vaṇmi eyiṉaṉ' eṉṟu kāṇappaṭum pāṭamumporutta
muṭittē.

eyiṉaṉvīḻnteṉa, uruppam putiya, puḷḷiṉ peruntōṭuāṭi, mocintu, tuṟikkaṇ iṟukkum
pukār naṉṉāṭueṉka, mukkaṭcelvaṉatu ālamuṟṟattiṭattē tiiyapoyki cūḻnta poḻiliṉ
makaḷir kiceypāvikaḷiyuṭiya tuṟiyeṉṟu iyiyum. vaḷaṅkeḻuvēntarāl purakkappaṭum
pukār naṉṉāṭu eṉka; ālamuṟṟam eṉalumām. īṇṭi, īṇṭaveṉat tirikka.iṟivaṉ pukaḻē
meyyāya pukaḻ ākalāṉum, atuvēulakeṅkum parattaṟ kuriyatākalāṉum 'ñālamnāṟum
nalaṅkeḻu nallici mukkaṭ celvaṉ'eṉṟār. nāṉmaṟi mukkaṇ celvaṉatuvāymoḻiyām eṉpatu,
2'naṉṟāyntanīṇimir caṭi, mutumutalvaṉ vāypōkā, toṉṟupurinta īriraṇṭiṉ, āṟuṇarnta
oru mutu nūl'eṉṟataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. ālamuṟṟam - civapirāṉeḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷatōr
iṭamākum. iṟukkum pukāreṉavum puriciyiṉiyum nal illiṉiyumuṭiya pukāreṉavum
taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. pakarvār eṉpatu pakāareṉat tirintu niṉṟatu. paṇṭam - pūvum
cāntummutalāyiṉa. aṇaṅkucāl arivi - varuttutaliyuṭiyaarivi. eṉṟātal, teyvam
pōlum arivieṉṟātal kūṟutalumām. kuṉṟam naṉṉāṭṭatu ; cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ āṇṭuc ceṉṟu
eṉ niliuriyeṉa viritturikka.

182. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi irā varuvāṉippakal vara eṉṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-8. pū kaṇ vēṅki poṉ iṇar milintu - aḻakiyiṭattiṉiyuṭiya vēṅki
marattiṉ poṉpōlumpūṅkottukkaḷic cūṭi, vāṅku ami nōṉcilieruttattu irīi - vaḷinta
mūṅkilālāya valiyavillit tōḷil irutti, tīm paḻappala viṉcuḷiviḷi tēṟal - iṉiya
palāppaḻattiṉcuḷiyiṉiṉṟum viḷinta tēṉi, vīḷi ampiṉiḷiyaroṭu mānti - cīḻkki
yoliyuṭaṉcellum ampiṉiyuṭiya ēvaliḷiyaruṭaṉ niṟiyakkuṭittu, ōṭṭu iyal piḻiyā
vayanāy piṟpaṭa -vilaṅkukaḷit turattip paṟṟum iyalputapputalillāta valiya nāy piṉṉēvara,
vēṭṭampōkiya kuṟavaṉ - vēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟa kuṟavaṉ,kāṭṭa kuḷavit taṇputal kurutiyoṭu
tuyalvara -kāṭṭiluḷḷa malliki yāya taṇṇiya putarutirattoṭu acintiṭa, muḻavu mā
toliccum kuṉṟanāṭa - muḷḷam paṉṟiyik koṉṟu vīḻttumkuṉṟukaḷi yuṭiya nāṭṭiṉ
talivaṉē!
9-12. uravu maḻi aravueṟi urumōṭu oṉṟi - kālvīḻttu - valiya mēkamaraviṉi eṟintu
kollum iṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭik kāliṟakkaṅ koṇṭu, poḻinta pāl nāḷ kaṅkul -nīriṉic corinta
ari nāḷākiya iravil, taṉiyivanta āṟu niṉintu - nī taṉiyiyāki vanta vaḻiyētattiṉi
niṉintu, alkalum paṉiyoṭu kaluḻumivaḷ kaṇṇē - ivaḷ kaṇ eppoḻutum nīr
koṇṭuaḻāniṟkum;
12-8. ataṉāl-,atirkural mutukali kaṟimuṟi muṉii - atirumkuraliṉiyuṭiya mutiya
mucukkali miḷakiṉ taḷiri uṇṭu
veṟuttu, uyar cimi neṭu kōṭṭuukaḷa-uyarnta ucciyiṉi yuṭiya nīṇṭacikaraṅkaḷilē
tāvutalāl, ukka-utirnta, kamaḻitaḻ alari tāay - maṇam nāṟum itaḻiṉiyuṭiyapūkkaḷ
parantu, vēlaṉ veṟi ayar viyaṉ kaḷamkaṭukkum - vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭum periya
kaḷattiṉiokkum, peruvari naṇṇiya cāralāṉ-periya maliyiaṭuttuḷḷa cāraṟ kaṇṇē, kaṭum
pakal varutalvēṇṭum - kaṭiya pakaṟ kaṇṇē nī varutal vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) nāṭa! pāṉāṭ kaṅkul taṉiyi vantavāṟu niṉintu,ivaḷkaṇ alkalum paṉiyoṭu
kaluḻum ; ataṉālperuvari naṇṇiya cāraṟkaṇṇē nī kaṭumpakal varutalvēṇṭum.
(vi - ri.) putariṭattē patuṅkiyiruntamuḷavumāvik kollutaliṉ putal
kurutiyoṭutuyalvarutalāyiṟṟu. iṭiyoli kēṭṭu aravuuṭkumeṉṉum vaḻakkuppaṟṟi
araveṟiyurumeṉappaṭṭatu. urumiṉ kaṭumi kūṟiyapaṭiyumām.urumōṭoṉṟi eṉṟataṉāl
āṟṟiṉatu ētamkuṟittāḷ. tuṇiyoṭum varutaṟkariya ētappāṭuṭiyavaḻiyil naḷḷiruḷil
taṉimiyāyt taṉporuṭṭuvarukiṉṟāy eṉpatuṇarntu, ivaḷ āṟṟāmiperituṭiya ḷākiṉṟāḷ
eṉpāḷ, 'taṉiyi vantāṟu niṉintalkalum paṉiyoṭu kaluḻu mivaḷ kaṇṇē'eṉṟāḷ. kaṭum
pakal - naṇpakal eṉṟapaṭi. teyya ;aci.
(u - ṟi.) kuṟavaṉ muḷavumāvik kolla ataṟku iṭamākiya putalkurutiyoṭu acintu
tōṉṟiyateṉpatu, talivaṉtaliviyoṭu kaḷavoḻukkattil oḻuka,avvoḻukkam taliviyiṉ mēṉi
vēṟupāṭṭāltāy mutaliyōrukkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟām.
