183. pāli
[talimakaṉ kuṟitta paruvavaravu kaṇṭu talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. tōḻi-, kuvaḷi uṇkaṇ kaluḻavum - eṉatu kuvaḷimalarpōṉṟa miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ
aḻavum, tiruntu iḻititali alkul avvari vāṭavum - tiruntiyaaṇiyiyum
tēmaliyumuṭiya alkuliṉ aḻakiya varivāṭṭamuṟavum, aruḷār nattuṟantu - aruḷceyyārāy
nammip pirintu, attamāraḻuvam ceṉṟu - aruneṟikaḷiyuṭiya paranta pāliyikkaṭantu
ceṉṟu, cēṇiṭiyār āyiṉum -cēymikkaṇ uḷḷār āyiṉum, naṉṟum nīṭalareṉṟi - mikavum
tāḻkkātu varuvar eṉkiṉṟāy;
5-10. iruṅkiḷikkoṇmū - periya kūṭṭamāya mēkaṅkaḷ, kuvavu tiri -vaḷinta
alikaḷiyum, paṉittuṟi - kuḷirnta tuṟiyiṉiyumuṭiya, peruṅkaṭal iṟantu - periya
kaṭalilēmikkuc ceṉṟu, nīr paruki aruntu koḷḷiya -nīriṉip parukiyuṇṭamikutiyi
yuṭiyaṉavāy, kuṭakkuērpu - mēṟkē eḻuntu, vayavu piṭi iṉattiṉ - cūluṟṟapeṇyāṉik
kūṭṭampōla, vayiṉ vayiṉ tōṉṟi- iṭantōṟum tōṉṟi, orucēra pāṭu āṉṟu -olimikku,
oruṅku uṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi - peytaṟkuneruṅka, kār kāli vantaṉṟu - kār kālamkāliyē
vantatu eṉiṉum;
10-15. māli - mālippoḻutil, kuḷirkoḷ piṭaviṉ kūr muki alari -kuḷircci
poruntiya piṭāviṉatu kūriya arumpualar taṟkuriyataṉi, vaṇṭu vāy tiṟakkum
taṇṭānāṟṟam - vaṇṭu vāyiṉi nekiḻttalāl eḻumamiyāta maṇattiṉi, kūtir aṟcirattu
ūti tūṟṟa -kūtir muṉpaṉik kālaṅkaṭku uriya vāṭikkāṟṟāṉatu tūṟṟā niṟka, paṉi ali
kalaṅkiyaneñcamoṭu - paṉi alittalāl kalaṅkiyaneñcamuṭaṉ, pirinticiṉōr tiṟattu -
pirintuceṉṟārāya talivariṉ poruṭṭu, varuntuvamallamō - nām varuntutaṟ kuriyōm
allēmō.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! talivar nattuṟantu cēṇiṭiyarāyiṉumnīṭalar eṉṟi; kār kāli vantaṉṟu;
māli,piṭaviṉatu alariyiṉ nāṟṟatti ūti tūṟṟa paṉialik kalaṅkiya neñcamoṭu nām
varuntuvamallamō?
(vi - ri.) iḻi - mēkali. nam tuṟantu - nattuṟantu eṉavikāramāyiṟṟu. tuvaṉṟi -
tuvaṉṟa eṉat tirikka.kārpparuvam vanta tākaliṉ, talivar nīṭātuvantuviṭuvar eṉṟu kūṟi
āṟṟuvitta tōḻikku,avar varuntuṇiyum nām eṅṅaṉamāṟṟiyirukkaṟpālam eṉpāḷ,
'kārkālivantatu eṉiṉum māli kūtir aṟcirattu ūtitūṟṟap paṉi alik kalaṅkiya
neñcamoṭuvaruntuvam allamō' eṉṟu talivi kūṟiṉāḷ.kārkāli vantateṉiṉum māli kūtir
aṟcirattuūti tūṟṟa eṉṟatu, ap paruvaṅkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttuvirintuvarum eṉpati
uṇarttiyavāṟu.kārpparuvam kuṟittuc ceṉṟa talivar,apparuvam vantum iṉṉum
vantilāmiyiṉ,aṭuttuvarum kūtir aṟcirap paruvaṅkaḷiṟṟāṉavar varutal eṅṅaṉam
teḷiyappaṭum eṉṟāḷumāyiṟṟu.
---------
184. mulli
[talimakaṉ viṉivayiṟpirintu vantu eytiyaviṭattu tōḻi pullumakiḻvu urittatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 19. cīrmiku kuricil - ciṟappumikka talivaṉē!
5-19. veṇpiṭavuaviḻtta vī kamaḻ puṟavil - veḷḷiya piṭavamarattiṉ virinta malar maṇakkum
mullinilattē, kuṇṭik kōṭṭa kuṟumuḷ kaḷḷi - kuṟiyakiḷikaḷiyum kuṟiya
muṭkaḷiyumuṭiya kaḷḷiyiṉ, puṉtaliputitta koḻu koṭi mulli - pulliyucciyiṉi
mūṭiya vaḷaviya mullikkoṭiyiṉ, ārkaḻal putu pū uyirppiṉ nīkki - ārkkukkaḻaṉṟa
putiya pūkkaḷi mūcceṟitalāl otukki, teḷaṟal parukiya tiri maruppu eḻil kali -
teḷintanīrik kuṭitta muṟukkuṇṭa kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiyamāṉ kali, puḷḷi am piṇiyoṭu
vatiyum āṅkaṇ -puḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya piṇiyuṭaṉ taṅkumavviṭattē, kōṭu uṭik
kiyar - kaḷikkoṭṭiṉiyuṭiya kiyiṉarāy, tuḷar eṟi viṉiñar -kaḷiyiṉi veṭṭi eṟiyum
toḻiliyuṭiyavarkaḷ,ariyal ārkiyar viḷimakiḻ tūṅka - kaḷḷiṉiniṟiya uṇṭu ataṉālāya
kaḷippu mikkuṟa, celkatirmaḻukiya uruva ñāyiṟṟu - cellum katir vemmikuṟinta
cenniṟattiṉatāya ñāyiṟṟiyuṭiya,cekkar vāṉam ceṉṟa poḻutil - cevvāṉamparaviya
kālattē, kalpāl aruviyiṉ olikkumnaṟṟēr - maliyiṉpāl vīḻumaruviyippōlolikkum
nalla tēriluḷḷa,tārmaṇi pala uṭaṉ iyampa - māliyākiya maṇikaḷpalavum oruṅkē olikka,
curumpu imir malara kāṉampiṟpaṭa - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum malarkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭupiṉṉēpōka,
aruntoḻil muṭitta cemmaluḷḷamoṭu - ariya viṉiyi muṭitta talimi mēviyūkkattuṭaṉ,
nī vantu niṉṟatu - nī iṅku vantuniṉṟatu;
1-4. kaṭavuṭ kaṟpoṭu- teyvak kaṟpuṭaṉ, kuṭikku viḷakku ākiya - kuṭikkuviḷakkam
ākiya, putalvaṉ payanta pukaḻmikuciṟappiṉ naṉṉarāṭṭikku aṉṟiyum - makaṉippeṟṟa pukaḻ
mikka ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya naṉmiyiyuṭiyatalivikkē yallāmalum, eṉakkum iṉitu ākiṉṟu
-eṉakkum iṉimiyit taruvatākiṉṟatu; niṉ āyuḷciṟakka - niṉ āyuḷ ciṟantiṭuvatāka.
(muṭipu) cīrmiku kuricil! cekkar vāṉam ceṉṟapoḻutil, naṟṟēr maṇi iyampa, kāṉam
piṟpaṭacemmal uḷḷamoṭu nī vantu niṉṟatu, naṉṉarāṭṭikku aṉṟiyum, eṉakkum
iṉitākiṉṟu; niṉ āyuḷ ciṟakka.
(vi - ri.) kaṭavuṭ kaṟpu - teyvattaṉmiyiyuṭiya kaṟpu.kaṟpuṭi makaḷir vēṇṭuṅkāl
maḻiyumpeyvippārākaliṉ avaratu kaṟpu teyvattaṉmiyuṭiyatāyiṟṟu. 1'kaṟpukkaṭampūṇṭa
itteyvamallatu, poṟpuṭit teyvamyāmkaṇ ṭilamāl, vāṉam poyyātu vaḷampiḻippaṟiyātu,
nīṇila vēntar koṟṟam citiyātu,pattiṉip peṇṭi rirunta nāṭu' eṉpatuṅ kāṇka.kaṭavuṭ
kaṟpoṭu putalvaṉ payanta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiyanaṉṉarāṭṭi eṉṟu iyiyum. putalvaṉ
kuṭiyiviḷaṅkac ceypavaṉ ākaliṉ, kuṭikku viḷakkākiyaputalvaṉ eṉṟāḷ. maṉivāḻkki
makaḷirkkukkaṟpoḻukkamum, naṉmakkaṭpēṟumiṉṟiyamiyātaṉa eṉpatu, 2'maṅkalameṉpa maṉimāṭci
maṟṟataṉ-naṉkalam naṉmakkaṭpēṟu' eṉṉum poyyā moḻiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum.3'maṉikkuviḷakkam
maṭavār' eṉpatupaṟṟik kuṭikkuviḷakkākiya eṉpataṉit talivikkuēṟṟiyurittalumām.
naṉṉarāṭṭi - maṉimāṭciyāyanaṉmiyellām uṭiyavaḷ. kaṇavaṉ kūṟiyacoṟpiḻiyātu
āṟṟiyirunta arumippāṭutōṉṟat talivi iṅṅaṉam ciṟappittuk kūṟappeṟṟāḷ.
pirivāṟṟātu talivi varuntuntōṟumtāṉum uṭaṉ varunti, aritiṉ
āṟṟuvittukkoṇṭiruntavaḷ ākaliṉ, nī vantu niṉṟatueṉakkum iṉitākiṉṟatu eṉṟu tōḻi
kūṟiṉāṉ.pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭu vantatēyaṉṟi avaṉ ariyaviṉiyi muṭitta perumitattuṭaṉ
tēriṉ maṇiolikkavantu niṉṟa tōṟṟam, talivaṉatu ākkattivirumpum talivikkuk
kaḻipēriṉpampayappatākaliṉ, cemmaluḷḷamoṭu maṇi iyampa nīvantu niṉṟatu iṉitākiṉṟu
eṉṟāḷ. talivaṉ tērvanta virivu tōṉṟak kāṉam piṟpaṭa eṉṟāḷ.celkatir - mēṟṟicikkaṭ
ceṉṟu maṟiyumkatir. tuḷar - kaḷi. tār maṇi - māliyākak kōttamaṇikaḷ.
talivaṉ pōntakāṭṭiṉkaṇ mulli, kaḷḷiyiṉ puṉtaliyimaṟittiruppatu; talivi taṉ
pirivāluḷatākiyavaruttam puṟantōṉṟāmal kaṟpiṉālāṟṟiyiruttaliyum, maṟitta pūvi
uyirppiṉālnīkki nīriṉip parukiya kali piṇiyoṭu vatital,tāṉ taliviyiṉ meliviṉi
nīkki avaḷuṭaṉiṉpuṟṟu vatiyap pōvatiyum avaṉukkuppulappaṭutti maṉaveḻucci taruvaṉa
eṉka.

185. pāli
[piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭatalimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 5-14. vāṭal oli kaḻi nivanta nel uṭineṭuvetir-vāṭutaluṟṟa olikkiṉṟa
mēlnōkkieḻunta taṇṭiṉiyum neṟkaḷiyumuṭiya nīṇṭamūṅkil, kalikoḷ maḷḷar vil viciyiṉ
uṭiya- āravāram koṇṭa maṟavaratu villiṉiṉ ṟeḻumampiṉ viciyāṟ piḷantiṭa, pitu aṟa
vempiyakal poru parappiṉ - pacumi aṟak kāyntaparukkikaḷ porukiṉṟa iṭattiṉiyuṭiya,
vēṉilattattu āṅkaṇ - vēṉilāṉ uḻanta kāṭṭiṭattē,vāṉ ulantu aruvi āṉṟa uyar cimi
maruṅkiṉ -mēkam peyyā toḻitaliṉ aruvikaḷ illiyākiyuyarnta cikaraṅkaḷil, peruviḻā
viḷakkampōla -periya kārttiki viḻāviṟku iṭumviḷakkukaḷip pōla, pala uṭaṉ ili ila
malarntilavamoṭu - iliyē illaṉavāyp palavum oruṅkēmalarnta ilava maraṅkaḷik koṇṭu, nili
uyarpiṟaṅkal mali iṟantōr - uyarnta niliyiyuṭiyapakka maliyiṉiyuṭiya perumaliyiṉit
tāṇṭicceṉṟōrāya nam talivar;
1-5. āy iḻi naleḻil paṇittōḷ - āynteṭutta aṇikaḷiyumnalla aḻakiṉiyumuṭiya
mūṅkilpōlum namatu tōḷ,elvaḷi ñekiḻac cāay - oḷiporuntiya vaḷinekiḻumpaṭi
melintu, iruṅkaviṉ aḻiya -periya aḻaku keṭumāṟu, peru ki aṟṟa neñcamoṭu
namtuṟantu - periya ceyalaṟṟa neñcamōṭirukkumnammik kiviṭṭu, irumpiṉ iṉ
uyiruṭiyōrpōla - irumpiṉāl iyaṉṟa iṉiya uyiriuṭiyārpōla, valittuvalliṉar -
valittirukkavaṉmiyuṭiyōrāyiṉar.
(muṭipu) maliyiṟantōr āya nam kātalar, nam paṇittōḷcāayk kaviṉ aḻiya nattuṟantu
irumpiṉiṉṉuyir uṭiyōr pōla valittu valliṉar.
(vi - ri.) neñcamoṭu irukka eṉa orucol viritturikka.tām eytum tuṉpam karuti
iraṅkit talivarvārātiruttaliṉ irumpiṉ iṉṉuyiruṭiyōrpōla valittu valliṉar
eṉṟāḷ.nammi irumpāliyaṉṟa uyiruṭiyārpōlak karutivārātirukkiṉṟār
eṉṟurittalumām.
----------
186. marutam
[talimakaṭkuppāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpa illiṭip paratticolli neruṅkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. vāṉam vēṇṭā vaṟaṉ il vāḻkki -maḻipeytali vēṇṭāta vaṟumiyuṟuta
lillātavāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya, nōṉ ñāṇ viṉiñar - valiyatūṇṭiṟ kayiṟṟiṉiyuṭiya mīṉ
piṭippōr,kōḷ aṟintu īrkkum - (mīṉ iri kōttamuḷḷiṉip) paṟṟiyatu uṇarntu
iḻukkum, mīṉmutir ilañci - mīṉ mikka nīrniliyil,kalitta tāmari nīr mici nivanta
neṭu tāḷ akalili-taḻitta tāmariyiṉ nīrmītu uyarnta neṭiyakāmpiṉiyuṭiya akaṉṟa iliyi,
irukayam tuḷaṅka kāluṟutōṟum-periya kuḷam aliyak kāṟṟuaṭikkuntōṟum, peru
kaḷiṟṟuc ceviyiṉalikkum ūraṉoṭu - periya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉiyiṉkātupōla acivikkum
ūraṉāl, eḻunta kuviyōperitē - eṉakku uṇṭāya alarō peritākumāyiṉum;
7-9. naṭpu-avaṉnaṭpō, koḻu kōl vēḻattup puṇi tuṇi āka -ceḻitta koṟukkaṅkaḻiyālāya
puṇiyiṉittuṇiyākak koṇṭu, puṉal āṭu kēṇmi aṉittē -puṉal viḷiyāṭum naṭpiṉ
aḷaviṉatē;
9-13. avaṉē - attalivaṉṟāṉ, oḷ toṭi makaḷir paṇṭiyāḻpāṭa-oḷḷiya toṭiyiṉiyuṭiya
makaḷirpaḻiyayāḻiṉip pāṭavum, īr taṇ muḻaviṉeṟikuṇil vitirppa-mikka taṇmi
vāyntamuḻaviṉik kuṟuntaṭiyāl aṭikkavum. taṇ naṟumcāntam kamaḻum tōḷ maṇantu-taṇṇiya
naṟiyacantaṉam nāṟum tōḷik kūṭi, iṉṉum piṟaḷvayiṉāṉ - iṉṉum piṟaḷiṭattēyuḷḷāṉ
āvaṉ;aṅṅaṉamākavum ;
13-14 maṉiyōḷemmoṭu pulakkum eṉpa - avaṉ maṉivi emmuṭaṉveṟukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpar;
14-17. māri ampiṉmaḻit tōl paḻiyaṉ-veṟṟi poruntiyavēliyum maḻittuḷi pōṉṟa mikka
ampiṉiyummēkampōlum kariya kēṭakattiṉiyum uṭiya paḻiyaṉeṉpāṉatu, kāviri vippiṉ
pōor aṉṉa -kāviri nāṭṭiluḷḷa pōr eṉṉuma ūriṉi otta,eṉ ceṟi vaḷi uritiṉiṉ
uṭittalō ilaṉ-eṉatu neruṅkiya vaḷikaḷiurimiyāl uṭitta loṉṟuñ ceytilēṉ; eṉavē,
18-20. yām taṉpakiyēm allēm - yām avaḷukkup pakiyāvēmallēm, cērntōr tirunutal
pacappa nīṅkum- taṉṉic cērntōratu aḻakiya neṟṟi pacantiṭaavarkaḷi oḻittu nīṅkum,
koḻunaṉum - avaḷkaṇavaṉ, taṉ uṭaṉ uṟipaki cālum - taṉṉuṭaṉkūṭiyuṟiyum pakivaṉātaṟkup
poruntuvāṉāvaṉ.
(muṭipu) ūraṉoṭu eṉakku eḻunta kuviyō peritē: naṭpēvēḻattup puṇi tuṇiyākap
puṉalāṭu kēṇmi aṉittē;avaṉē iṉṉum piṟaḷ vayiṉāṉē; maṉiyōḷ emmoṭupulakkum eṉpa;
ceṟivaḷi uṭittalō ilēṉ; yāmtaṉ pakiyēm allēm; koḻunaṉ taṉ uṭaṉuṟi pakicālum.
(vi - ri.) mīṉ piṭikkum viṉiñar uḻaviṉāl nel mutaliyaṉaviḷittal ilarākaliṉ avaratu
vāḻkki vāṉamvēṇṭā vāḻkki eṉavum, aṉṉarāyiṉum avarpiṭikkum mīṉē avarukkup
periyatoruvaruvāyātaliṉ, vaṟaṉil vāḻkki eṉavumkūṟappaṭṭatu. nōṉñāṇ - tūṇṭiliṉ
valiya kayiṟu;atukoṇṭu viṉi ceytaliṉ avar nōṉñāṇviṉiñar eṉappaṭṭār. kōḷ -
irumpiṟkōttiriyip paṟṟuki. iṉi, valiya kayiṟṟiyuṭiyavaliyeṉak koṇṭu, aḵtu
akappaṭuttami aṟintuīrkkum eṉṟurittalumām. puṇi tuṇiyākanīrāṭuvār
nīrāṭṭumuṭintavuṭaṉ ap puṇiyiviṭuttu akaṟalpōla, talivaṉ eṉpāl vantuakaṉṟaṉaṉ eṉpāḷ,
naṭpukēṇmi aṉittueṉṟāḷ. kēṇmi - puṇiyiṉiṭattuḷatāya kēṇmi;kuṟippu moḻi. iṉi,
naṭpu oru nāḷ puṇi tuṇiyākappuṉalāṭiya avvaḷaviṟṟē eṉṟurittalumām; 1"nīveyyōḷoṭu
vēḻaveṇpuṇi taḻīi.......nerunalāṭiṉipuṉalē" eṉa muṉ vantamiyuṅkāṇka.
oṇṭoṭimakaḷir - viṟaliyar; parattiyarumām, talivaṉcēripparattiyoṭu kūṭi yoḻukutalip
parattiyarcēriyiṉiṉ ṟeḻum yāḻoliyum muḻavōciyumēyāvarumaṟiyap pulappaṭuttum:
aṅṅaṉamākavum taliviemmoṭu pulattal poruntā teṉpatu tōṉṟa, pāṭavitirppat tōḷ maṇantu
piṟaḷvayiṉāṉeṉavum, emmoṭu pulakku meṉpa eṉavum iṟparattikūṟiṉāḷeṉka. maṟṟum,
talivaṉ taliviyiyumeṉṉiyum kiyakaṉṟu cēripparattiyar māṭṭumaruvi yoḻukutalāl
eṉakkuḷatāya veṟuppiṉi eṉvaḷiyi uṭittuk kāṭṭiṟṟilēṉ; atu koṇṭēavaṉ eṉṉuṭaṉ
uḷaṉākat talivikarutiṉāḷpōlumeṉpāḷ, “ceṟivaḷi yuṭittalōilaṉē eṉṟāḷ"

kulamakaḷirallāta alavaṟ peṇṭirmutalāyiṉōr veṟuppiṉāl vaḷiyuṭittalceyvareṉpatu,
1"eṉṉōṭutiriyēṉāyiṉ....uṭikaveṉ, nēriṟi muṉkivīṅkiya vaḷiyē" eṉpataṉāla ṟikā
ilaṉ: aṉvikuti taṉmikkaṇ vantatu. koḻunaṉum; um: acinili, iṉi,
cēripparattiyariyum viṭuttuttaliviyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu uṟivāṉeṉpatu tōṉṟat 'taṉuṭaṉuṟi
paki' eṉṟāḷ; uṭaṉuṟinta koḻunaṉpakiyātal cālum eṉak kūṭṭiyurit talumām,

187. pāli
[pirivuṇarttiyatōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu. talimakaṉpiriviṉkaṇ talimakaṭkut tōḻi
colliyatūumām.]

(co - ḷ.) 10. tōḻi-,
5. kātalar - namkātalar;
1-4. tōḷ pulampuakala nammoṭu tuñci - namatu tōḷ taṉimi yakalanammuṭaṉ tuyiṉṟu, nāḷ
pala nīṭiya karantu uṟipuṇarcci - palanāḷ nīṭṭitta kaḷavuppuṇarcciyi,nāṇ uṭimiyiṉ
nīṅki - viṉiyiṉṟiyiruttaṟku nāṇutal uṭimiyāṉē nīṅki, cēynāṭṭu arum poruḷ
valitta neñcamoṭu - tūranāṭṭilē ariya pōruḷi īṭṭat tuṇintaneñcattuṭaṉ;
14-24. maṉi uṟi kōḻi- maṉiyil vatiyuṅ kōḻiyiṉ, aṇal tāḻpu aṉṉa -toṅkukiṉṟa
tāṭiyi yotta, kavi oṇ taḷirakaruṅkāl yāattu - kavartta oḷḷiya taḷiriṉiyumkariyaaṭi
yiṉiyum uṭiya yā maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya, vēṉilveṟpil kāṉam kāya - vēṉil veppam
vāyntamaliyi yaṭutta kāṭu kāyntiṭa, muṉi eḻuntu ōṭiyakeṭunāṭṭu ār
iṭi-kāṭṭaraṇiluḷḷārkuṭiyeḻuntōṭiya pāḻ nāṭṭiṉ ariya iṭattē, paṉiveḷiṟu aruntu
piṅkaṇ yāṉi - paṉaṅkuruttittiṉṉum paciya kaṇṇiṉi yuṭiya yāṉi, oḷ cuṭarmutirā iḷam
katir amiyattu - oḷḷiya cuṭarmutirāta iḷiya ñāyiṟṟiyuṭiya kāliyilē, kaṇpaṭupāyal
ki oṭuṅku aci nili - tuyilumiṭattēceyaloṭuṅki acintu koṇṭirukkum
niliyāṉatu,vāḷ vāyc cuṟaviṉ paṉi tuṟi nīnti -vāḷpōlum vāyiṉiyuṭiya cuṟā
mīṉkaḷuḷḷakuḷirnta tuṟikaḷi nīntic ceṉṟu, nāḷ vēṭṭueḻunta nayaṉil paratavar - nāṭ
kālattē mīṉvēṭṭi kuṟittu eḻunta nayamaṟṟa paratavaruṭiya, vikukaṭal ampiyil tōṉṟum -
kaṭaliṭattēporuntiya tōṇi pōlat tōṉṟāniṟkum, mipaṭu mā mali vilaṅkiya curaṉ - mēkam
poruntiyaperiya mali kuṟukkiṭṭa pāliyiṉ kaṇṇē;
10-13. cēṇ ōṅkualantali ñemiyattu - neṭuntūram uyarntakāynta ucciyiṉiyuṭiya ñemi
maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya,āḷ il āṅkaṇ - āḷ vaḻakkaṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, kalcērpu iruta cilkuṭi
pākkattu - maliyiccārntirunta cila kuṭikaḷiyuṭiya pākkattē, elviruntu ayara -
iravil viruntu ceyya, ēmattualki - pātukāvalākat taṅki;
4-5. ēki -toṭarntu ceṉṟu, nam uyarvu uḷḷiṉar - namatuuyarcciyik karutiṉar;
(ataṉāl).
5-10. kaṟuttōr -ciṉanteḻunta pakivaratu, tevmuṉi citittakaṭumparip puravi -
pakippulam tolitta kaṭiyacelaviṉiyuṭiya kutirikaḷiyuṭiya, vārkaḻalpolinta - nīṇṭa
kaḻalāṟ polivuṟṟa, vaṉkaṇmaḻavar - taṟukaṇmiyiṉi yuṭiya maḻavar ceyyum,pūntoṭi viḻaviṉ
talināḷ aṉṉa - pūntoṭiviḻā eṉṉum viḻāviṉ mutalnāḷi yottapoliviṉiyuṭiya, taru
maṇal ñemiriya tirunakarmuṟṟam - koṇarntiṭṭa maṇal paranta aḻakiyamaṉiyiṉ muṟṟam,
pulampuṟum kollō -taṉimiyuṟumō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kātalar, nammoṭu nīṭiya karantuṟi puṇarcciyi nāṇuṭimiyiṉ nīṅki,
arumporuḷ valittaneñcamoṭu, mali vilaṅkiya curattiṉkaṇṇē,pakkattē elviruntu ayara
ēmattu alki, ēki, namuyarvu uḷḷiṉar; ataṉāl tirunakar muṟṟampulampuṟuṅ kollō?
(vi - ri.) tōḷ pulampu akalat tuñci eṉṟatu, tōḷilētuyiṉṟu eṉṟapaṭi, nāṇuṭimi -
pōkam vēṇṭiāṭavarkku uyirākiya viṉiyi yoḻintiruttaṟkunāṇutaluṭimi. cēy nāṭṭu
arumporuḷ - cēynāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu tēṭum ariya poruḷ, nam uyarvu -vaṟiyōrkku
aḷittal, viruntu puṟantarutalmutaliya illaṟam purintu nām mēmpaṭutal;ataṟkup poruḷ
iṉṟiyamiyātākaliṉ,poruḷvayiṟ pirintāri nam uyarvu uḷḷiṉareṉṟāḷ. ēki uḷḷiṉar
eṉpataṉi uḷḷi ēkiṉareṉa māṟuka. pūntoṭi viḻavu, paṭikkalam payiṉṟiḷiya vīrari
araṅkēṟṟuvikkum viḻā.toṭi-ampu toṭuttal, tali nāḷaṉṉapoliviṉiyuṭiya muṟṟameṉa
viritturikka. talivarpiriviṟkuḷatām varuttatti muṟṟamtaṉimiyuṟutalāl varuntuvāḷ
pōṟ kūṟiṉāḷ.alantali - alanta tali; vikāram. ñemiyattu; attu,cāriyi. curattik
kaṭantu ēkuṅkāl, pākkattuelviruntayara ēmattu alki ēkuvār eṉṟāḷ.kāṉaṅkōḻiyiṉ
vēṟupaṭukka, maṉiyuṟi kōḻieṉappaṭṭatu. muṉi - kāṭṭaraṇ; vēṭṭuvar ūr.
keṭunāṭu-vāḻvā roḻintu pāḻpaṭṭa nāṭu.vāḷvāyc cuṟaviṉ paṉittuṟi nīnti
eṉṟatu,paratavariṉ añacāmiyip pulappaṭuttiyavāṟām. paṉiveḷiṟu aruntu eṉṟatu, vēṟu
uṇaviṉmikuṟittapaṭiyām. yāṉi acinili. kaṭal ampiyiṟṟōṉṟum curam eṉavum, mali
vilaṅkiya curameṉavum taṉittaṉi iyiyum.

188. kuṟiñci
[iravil ciṟippuṟamākattōḻi colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1.peruṅkaṭal mukanta iru kiḷi koṇmū - periya kaṭalnīri mukantukoṇṭa
periya kūṭṭamākiya mēkamēnītāṉ;
2-8. iruṇṭu -iruṭciyuṟṟu, uyar vicumpiṉ valaṉ ērpu vaḷii -uyarnta vāṉil valamāka
eḻuntu cuṟṟi, pōrppuuṟumuraciṉ iraṅki - tōṟpōrviyuṟṟa muracupōlamuḻaṅki. muṟi
purintu aṟaṉ neṟi piḻiyāt tiṟaṉaṟi maṉṉar - ceṅkōṉmi mēṟkoṇṭu aṟaneṟivaḻuvāta
ceyyuḷ kūṟu pāṭṭiṉi aṟinta maṉṉaratu,arum camattu etirnta perum cey āṭavar -
ariyapōril māṟṟāruṭaṉ etirttup pōrceyyum pērāṇmic ceykiyiyuṭiya
vīrarkaḷ,kaḻittu eṟi vāḷiṉ naḷippaṉa viḷaṅkum -uṟiyiṉiṉṟum uruvi vīcum vāḷip
pōlacceṟivaṉavāy viḷaṅkum, miṉ uṭik karuviyi ākimiṉṉiṉ tokutiyi uṭiyi āki:
nāḷum aravamkoṉṉē ceytiyō - nāṭōṟum payaṉiṉṟiāravāram ceytu oḻitiyō? aṉṟi;

8-14. poṉ eṉamalarnta vēṅki mali toṭar aṭicci - poṉpōla malarnta vēṅkip pūkkaḷālāya
niṟintamāliyit tarittu, polinta āyamoṭu kāṇtakiyali - polivuṟṟa āyattāruṭaṉ aḻaku
poruntanaṭantu, taḻali vāṅkiyum taṭṭi ōppiyum -taḻaliṉic cuṟṟiyum taṭṭiyiṉit
taṭṭiyum,aḻal ēr ceyali amtaḻi aciiyum - tīyiṉkoḻuntiṉi otta acōkiṉatu aḻakiya
taḻiyālāyuṭiyiṉi yuṭuttum, kuṟamakaḷ kākkum - kuṟamakaḷāyem talivi kākkum, ēṉal
puṟamum maḻitarutiyō-tiṉiyiṉiṭattu maḻiyum poḻiviyō!vāḻiya.
(muṭipu) koṇmūvē!nītāṉ iruṇṭu ērpu vaḷii iraṅki viḷaṅkummiṉṉuṭik karuviyi
āki, aravam koṉṉēceytiyō; kuṟamakaḷ kākkum ēṉalpuṟam maḻiyumtarutiyō vāḻiya!
(vi - ri.) muṟipurintu piḻiyā maṉṉar eṉavum, tiṟaṉ aṟimaṉṉar eṉavum taṉittaṉi
kūṭṭuka. iṟimāṭciyāvum aṭaṅkat tiṟaṉ aṟi maṉṉar eṉṟaṉaḷ.camam-am maṉṉar tam māṟṟāruṭaṉ
ceyyumpōr. peruñ cey āṭavar - vañciṉaṅkūṟippōr ceyyum pērāṇmicceykiyuṭiyavīrar.
malarnta vēṅki malitoṭaraṭicci eṉṟataṉāl tiṉi viḷiyuṅ kālampeṟappaṭṭatu. taḻal -
pirampu pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṟpiṉṉic cuṟṟi oliyuṇṭākkik kiḷiyiōṭṭuvatōr karuvi. taṭṭi -
mūṅkiṟ ṟuṇṭippiḷantu taṭṭi oliyuṇṭākkik kiḷi kaṭivatorukaruvi. ivaṟṟit “taḻalum
taṭṭiyuṅ kuḷirumpiṟavum, kiḷikaṭi maramiṉa ūḻūḻ vāṅki" eṉpataṉuriyāl aṟika. taḻi -
taḻi uṭi. ēṉal puṟam maḻiyuntarutiyō eṉa ummiyi māṟuka.
ummiyiaciyākkiyum maḻiyi viḷiyākkiyum ēṉalippuṟantarutiyō eṉṟurittalumām.
itaṟkuk koṇmūākiya maḻiyē eṉka. iraviṟ ciṟippuṟamākavuḷḷatalivaṉit tōḻi iṅṅaṉam
maḻiyākakkoṇṭukuṟippiṉālē nī iṅṅaṉam vantu oḻukutalāṟpayaṉiṉṟi alar viḷikkiṉṟāyō?
aṉṟivarintukoḷviyō? eṉak kūṟi varivu kaṭāyiṉāḷeṉka.
(mē - ḷ.) 2'uṭaṉuṟiyuvamañ cuṭṭunaki ciṟappeṉak keṭalaru marapiṉuḷḷuṟi aintē' eṉṉuñ
cūttirattuc cuṭṭueṉṉum uḷḷuṟikku icceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi,'itaṉuḷ' koṉṉē
ceytiyō aravam eṉpataṉālpayaṉiṉṟi alar viḷittiyō eṉavum kūṟi, ēṉaṟpuṟamum tarutiyō
eṉpataṉāl, varintu koḷviyōeṉavum kūṟit talimakaṉi maḻimēl vittu
kūṟaliṉcuṭṭāyiṟṟu. koṉṉē ceytiyō eṉṟataṉālvaḻuvāyiṉum varital vēṭkiyāṟ
kūṟiṉamiyiṉamintatu' eṉpar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

189. pāli
[makaṭ pōkkiya cevilicolliyatu]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. pacu paḻam palaviṉ kāṉam vempi - cevvivāynta paḻattiṉikkoṇṭa
palāmaraṅkaḷiyuṭiyakāṭu vetumpa, vicumpu kaṇ aḻiya vēṉil nīṭi -mēkam vāṉiṭattiṉiṉ
ṟoḻitaliṉ vēṉilveppam mika, kayam kaṇ aṟṟa kal ōṅku vippiṉ- kuḷaṅkaḷ tammiṭattē
nīraṟṟirukkum kaṟkaḷuyarnta iṭaṅkaḷil, nāṟu uyir maṭa piṭi taḻīi -perumūccut tōṉṟum
iḷiya piṭiyit taḻuvi, kaḷiṟu -kaḷiṟukaḷ, muḻavu eṭuttu uyari vēṟu nāṭṭu
viḻavuppaṭarmaḷḷariṉ - taṅkaḷ muḻavukaḷi eṭuttuuyarttukkoṇṭu vēṟṟunāṭṭil
nikaḻumviḻāviṉi niṉittuc cellummaḷḷarkaḷippōla, atarppaṭutta -neṟippaṭutticceṉṟa,
kal uyar - kaṟkaḷuyarnta, vevvari kavāaṉ attam cuṭṭi - koṭiyapakka maliyic cārnta
curattiṉic cellattuṇintu;
8-15. vayaliampiṇiyal vārnta kavāaṉ - vayalikkoṭiyālākiya aḻakiya taḻiyuṭi
tāḻntatuṭiyiṉiyum, titali alkul - tēmal paṭarntaalkuliṉiyum uṭiya, kuṟumakaḷ -
iḷiyaḷāya eṉ makaḷ,piyeṉa avaṉoṭu ceṉṟu - melleṉat taṉtalivaṉoṭuceṉṟu, piṟaḷ ākiya
aḷavi - eṅkaṭkup piṟaḷorutti ākiyapōtu, eṉṟum paṭarmalievvamoṭu - ekkālattum
niṉivu mikkatuṉpamoṭu, mātiram tuḻii - ticiyellāmtēṭivantu, maṉi maruṇṭu irunta
eṉṉiṉummaṉiyiṉkaṇ mayaṅkiyirunta eṉṉik kāṭṭilum,naṉi makiḻ naṉṉarāḷar
kūṭukoḷiṉṉiyam-kaḷḷiṉ kaḷippiṉiyuṭiya nallapāṇarkaḷatu oṉṟukūṭi yolikkum
vācciyaṅkaḷ,tēr ūr teruvil tatumpum-tēr ōṭum perunterukkaḷil aṟātu olikkum,
ūr-ivvūrāṉatu, taṉvīḻvu uṟu poruḷ - taṉatu viruppam mikkatoruporuḷi, iḻantaṉṟu -
iḻantatāyiṟṟu.
(muṭipu.) kavāaṉ attam cuṭṭi, kuṟumakaḷ, avaṉoṭu ceṉṟuemakkup piṟaḷākiya aḷavi,
eṉṉiṉum ūr taṉvīḻvuṟu poruḷ iḻantaṉṟu.
(vi - ri.) vempi, nīṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟi vempa, nīṭeṉattirikka. kayam-cuṉiyumām. maḷḷar
eṉṟatuīṇṭuk kūttari. muḻaveṭut tuyari vēṟunāṭṭuviḻavuppaṭar maḷḷar eṉa māṟuka.
poruḷiṉkaṇmaṭappiṭi taḻuvi eṉpataṉi uvamiyuṭaṉ kūṭṭiviṟaliyaruṭaṉ cellum kūttar
eṉavum,uvamiyiṉkaṇ muḻaveṭuttuyari eṉpataṉipporuḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭi muḻavu pōlum
palāppaḻattiṉiēntic celluṅ kaḷiṟu eṉavum urikka. piṟantanāṭṭoṭṭup pēṇi vaḷartta
emmik kiyakaṉṟuētilāṉoṭu ceṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ, 'avaṉoṭu ceṉṟupiṟaḷ ākiya aḷavi, eṉṟāḷ.
avaḷ aṉṉaḷ āyiṉumavaḷ pirivāl yāṉ eytum tuṉpam iṟappavumperitu eṉpāḷ, 'eṉṟum,
paṭarmali evvamoṭu,,mātirin tuḻii maṉi maruṇṭirunta eṉṉiṉum'eṉṟum tāṉ eytun
tuṉpattiṉum nāṟṟāyumāyattārum mutaliyōr, avaḷi iḻantu eytumtuṉpam periteṉpāḷ,
'eṉṉiṉum,ūriḻantaṉṟutaṉ vīḻvuṟu poruḷē' eṉṟumkūṟiṉāḷ. ūr eṉṟatu īṇṭu, naṟṟāy
āyattārmutaliyōrik kuṟittu niṉṟatu.

190. neytal
[tōḻi,cevilittāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 6. aṉṉi - tāyē, vāḻi-, vēṇṭu-nāṉ kūṟuvataṉivirumpik kēṭpāyāka;
6. 10. uyarcimipotumpil - uyarnta ucciyiyuṭiyacōliyilpuṉṉi ciṉi cērpu
irunta - puṉṉimarattiṉkiḷiyic cērntirunta, vampa nāri iriya - putiyanāri
oḻintiṭa, oru nāḷ - muṉpu oru nāḷ, poṅkuvaral ūtiyoṭu puṇari alippavum -
poṅkutaluṭaṉvarum vaṭakāṟṟuṭaṉ alikaḷ alittukkoṇṭirukkavum, uḻi kaṭal vaḻaṅkalum
uriyaṉ - orutalivaṉ kaṭalōrattē tēril varutaṟkum uriyaṉāyiṉaṉ;
10-17. ataṉ tali -ataṉmēlum, iru kaḻip pukāar - periyakaḻiyiṉiyuṭiya
tuṟimukattē, vayaccuṟā poruntattākki eṟinteṉa - valiya cuṟāmīṉ tam uṭampiṟporuntat
tākki eṟinta tāka, valavaṉ aḻippa -tērppākaṉ celaviṉi niṟuttalāl, eḻil
payamkuṉṟiya - eḻucciyum payaṉum kuṉṟiyaṉavum, ciṟiaḻi toḻila - pūṭṭu aḻinta
ceykiyiyuṭiyaṉavumākiya; niri maṇip puravi - nirittamaṇimāli pūṇṭa kutirikaḷ,
viri naṭi tavira -virintu cellum celavu oḻintu taṅkiṉavāka, iḻumeṉ kāṉal -
iḻumeṉṉum oliyiyuṭiya kāṉaliṭattē,viḻumaṇal acii-ciṟanta maṇalil taṅki, āyntapariyaṉ
ivaṇ vantu - vēṟu ciṟanta puraviyuṭaṉ iṅkuvantu, māṉṟa māli cērntaṉṟu ilaṉ -
mayaṅkiyamālip poḻutil taṅkiyatum ilaṉ;
5. alamaralmaḻikkaṇ amarntum nōkkāḷ - avaṉiccuḻalkiṉṟa kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya niṉ
makaḷnōkkiṉāḷum allaḷ; aṅṅaṉamākavum;
1-4. tiri uḻantuaciiya - tiriyiṉ viḷiyāṭi uḻantamiyāltaḷarcciyuṟṟa, nirivaḷi
āyamoṭu - nirittavaḷiyiṉiyuṭiya makaḷir kūṭṭattuṭaṉ, (mintarkaḷ)uppiṉ kuppi ēṟi -
uppu mēṭṭil ēṟi niṉṟu,elpaṭa - iruḷvara, varu timil eṇṇum tuṟivaṉoṭu -karikku
mīṇṭu varum paṭakukaḷi eṇṇum tuṟiyiyuṭiyaat talivaṉoṭu niṉ makaḷic cārtti
ūr-intūrāṉatu, oru taṉ koṭumiyiṉ - oppaṟṟa taṉatukoṭumiyāl, alar pāṭum - alar
kūṟā niṟkum;
6. aliyal - ātalālnī avaḷi varuttātēkoḷ.
(muṭipu) aṉṉi! vāḻi! vēṇṭu; oru talivaṉ; orunāḷ ūtiyoṭupuṇari alippavum
uḻikkaṭal vaḻaṅkalum uriyaṉ;ataṉ tali vayaccuṟā eṟinteṉa; valavaṉ aḻippapuravikaḷ viri
naṭi tavira, aciiyāyntapariyiṉaṉ, māli ivaṇ cērntaṉṟō ilaṉ,maḻikkaṇ niṉmakaḷ avaṉi
amarntunōkkiṉāḷallaḷ; aṅṅaṉamākavum niṉmakaḷiattuṟivaṉoṭu cārtti; ivvūr
taṉkoṭumiyiṉ alarpāṭum; ātaliṉ nī ivaḷialiyal.
(vi - ri.) āyamoṭu eṉṟamiyāl, mintar eṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu. eṇṇum eṉṉum
peyareccam, tuṟi eṉṉumiṭappeyar koṇṭatu. tuṟiyiyuṭiya talivaṉoṭucārtti eṉa
viritturikka. ūr eṉpatu -kuvip peṇṭiri. maḻik kaṇ: āku peyar.vaḻaṅkalum eṉṟa ummi
varutalarumiyi viḷakkiniṉṟatu. ataṉtali eṉṟatu; ūtiyoṭu puṇari atitalēaṉṟi
ataṉmēlum eṉṟapaṭi, vayac cuṟā eṟinteṉavalavaṉ aḻippa eṉṟataṉāl, kutiri
puṇpaṭṭamipeṟṟām. puṇpaṭṭamiyāl valavaṉpūṭṭaviḻttuviṭṭa kutiri naṭitavirntirukka,avaṉ
maṇalil ōriṭattē taṅkiyiruntataṉṟi vēṟukutiri pūṇṭa tēruṭaṉ yāṅkaḷ
irukkumiṭattiṟkuvantu iruṇṭa mālippoḻutil taṅkiṉāṉallaṉeṉṟum, taliviyum avaṉi
virumpinōkkiṉāḷallaḷ eṉṟum kūṟi, aṅṅaṉamākaliṉūr vāḷātu alar kūṟuvatu paṟṟi nī
taliviyivaruttātē eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷpōl, talivaṉetirppāṭṭik kuṟippiṟ
pulappaṭuttittōḻi. cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṉṟāḷ eṉka.

191. pāli
[talimakaṉ taṉneñciṟkuc collic celavaḻuṅkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 13. eṉ neñcē vāḻi-,
1-12. attam pātirituy tali putu vī - pāliyiluḷḷa tuyyiṉi ucciyiṟkoṇṭa putiya
pātirip pūvi, eri itaḻ alari yoṭuiṭi paṭa virii - neruppup pōṉṟa
itaḻiṉiyuṭiyaalarippūvuṭaṉ iṭiyiṭiyē kalantu, veṇtōṭṭuttoṭutta vaṇṭupaṭu kaṇṇi -
veṇmi poruntiyatāḻampūviṉ tōṭṭil vittuk kaṭṭiya vaṇṭuporuntum kaṇṇiyiṉit
taritta, tōl puticiraṟṟu aṭi-potinta ceruppu olikkumaṭiyiṉiyum,
kōl-kōliṉiyumuṭiya, umaṇar-uppuvāṇikaruṭiya, ūr kaṉṭaṉṉa-ūrtiraṇṭuvantālotta, āram
vāṅki-vaṇṭikaḷiiḻuttu, arum curam ivarnta-ēṟutaṟku ariyamēṭukaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭilēṟi
vanta, acivu il nōṉtāḷ tiruntu pakaṭṭu iyampumkoṭumaṇi-taḷarvillāta valiya
tāḷiṉiyuṭiyacevviya erutukaḷiṉ olikkum vaḷinta maṇikaḷiṉoli, purintu avar maṭi
viṭu vīḷiyoṭu-avarkaḷvirumpi vāyiṉi maṭittu eḻuppum cīḻkkiyoliyoṭu kaṭitu etir
ōṭi-virintu etirēceṉṟu,ōmi am peruṅkāṭṭu varuum vampalarkku-ōmimaraṅkaḷiyuṭiya
periya kāṭṭil varum putiya
( pāṭam ) 1.vaṇṭōṭṭu.
2. vavvi.
3. uriyiṉi.
4.urōṭōkakkavuṇiyaṉ cēntaṉ.
----------
rāya vaḻic celvārkku, ēmamceppum-pātukāvalik kūṟum, eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭi-veppam mikka
neṭiya kāṭṭilē, arumporuḷnacii-aritu īṭṭum poruḷi virumpi, pirintuuṟi
valli-nam taliviyip pirintu uṟiyumvaṉmi yuṭiyiyāy, ceṉṟu
viṉieṇṇutiyāyiṉ-ceṉṟu poruḷ īṭṭum viṉiyieṇṇuviyāṉāl;
13-17. uṟal iṉcāyal-uṟutaṟkiṉiya meṉmittaṉmiyiyum, nirimuki mulli
aruntu-mulliyiṉ niritta arumpukaḷiniṟiyac cūṭi,
melliyavāki-meṉmittaṉmiyuṭiyavāki, aṟal eṉa virinta oli irumkūntal-karumaṇalpōla
virinta taḻitta kariyakūntaliyumuṭiya nam talivi, tēṟumeṉa-teḷintiruppāḷ eṉṟu,
valiya kūṟavumvalliyō-pirivuṇarttum valiya coṟkaḷikkūṟavum vaṉmi yuṭiyiyō?
valliyāyiṉ;
12-13. naṉṟumuritticiṉ-avaḷiṭattē ceṉṟu peritum kūṟiavaḷit tēṟṟuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) umaṇaratu ūrkaṇṭaṉṉa āram vāṅkic curamivarnta pakaṭṭu iyampum koṭumaṇi;
avarvīḷiyoṭu etir ōṭi vampalarkku ēmam ceppumeṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭip poruḷ naciic ceṉṟu
viṉieṇṇutiyāyiṉ; iṉcāyalum kūntalumuṭiya ivaḷtēṟum eṉa valiya kūṟavum valliyō?
valliyāyiṉnaṉṟum uritticiṉ.
(vi-ri.) tōṭu-tāḻampūviṉ itaḻ. ciraṟṟu tōl eṉakkoṇṭu citaṟiya ceruppu
eṉṟalumām. kōl-pakaṭuōṭṭuṅkōl. āram-vaṇṭiyiṉ uṟuppu; vaṇṭikku-ākupeyar. mikap
palavākiya vaṇṭikaḷuṭaṉ umaṇartiraṇṭu cellun tōṟṟam ūr eḻuntu celluvatupōlumākaliṉ,
umaṇar ūrkaṇṭaṉṉa āram eṉṟār.pakaṭṭiṉ maṇi yōciyiyum umaṇar vīḷiyoliyiyum cevi yēṟṟa
vampalar ikkāṭṭilētam illi yeṉṟu uṇarvarākaliṉ, avvolikaḷvampalarkku ēmam ceppum
eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vallivaliyiyāy eṉṉum poruṭṭu ; vavvi eṉpatupāṭamāyiṉ, pirintuṟitali
maṉattiṟ koṇṭueṉka. poruḷ nacii nīḷiṭic ceṉṟu viṉieṇṇutiyāyiṉ eṉak kūṭṭuka.
aruntu - ārntueṉpataṉ vikāram. tēṟu meṉa eṉṟataṉāl, valiyeṉṟatu. poruḷīṭṭi
virintu varuvēm eṉṉuñcoṟkaḷātal vēṇṭum. kūṟavum valliyō eṉṟatukūṟutaṟku arumiyi
viḷakki niṉṟata. maṟṟu,valliyalli eṉṉum poruḷ tarutaliṉ viṉimāṟṟu; aciyumām.

192. kuṟiñci
[tōḻi, talimakaṉic 1ceṟippuaṟivuṟīi iravukkuṟi maṟuttatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 13-15. uravup peyal urum iṟikoṇṭa-valiya maḻiyuṭaṉiṭi taṅkutal koṇṭa,
uyarcimi perumali nāṭa -uyarnta ucciyiyuṭiya periya malināṭṭiyuṭiyatalivaṉē;
3-8. vicumpileyyā varivil aṉṉa pintār cevvāy ciṟukiḷi - vāṉiluḷḷa eyyap
peṟāta aḻakiya villiotta paciya māliyiṉiyum civanta vāyiṉiyumuṭiya ciṟiya kiḷi,
citiya vāṅki tiṉicitiyumpaṭi koytu, poṟi melintiṭṭapulpuṟap peru kural -
cumakkalāṟṟātu pōkaṭṭapulliya puṟattiṉiyuṭiya periya katiriṉi, vaḷi ciṟivāraṇam
kiḷiyoṭu kavara-vaḷinta ciṟakukaḷi yuṭiyakāṉaṅkōḻi taṉ iṉattuṭaṉ kavarntuṇṇumāṟu,ēṉalum
iṟaṅku poṟi uyirttaṉa - tiṉiyumvaḷinta katirkaḷi īṉṟaṉa (ākaliṉ em talivi
iṉiiṟceṟikkappaṭuvaḷ);
8-12. pālnāḷ nīvantatu aḷikkuvi eṉiṉē. pāti iravil nī vantuaḷippāy eṉṟu kūṟiṉ,
māl vari mi paṭu viṭarakamtuḻii - periya maliyiṉ iruḷ poruntiya kukiyiṉiṭaṅkaḷit
tuḻāvi, oy eṉa aruvitanta aravu umiḻtirumaṇi - viriya aruvikoṇṭuvanta
pāmpuumiḻnta aḻakiya maṇi, peruvari ciṟu kuṭi maṟukuviḷakkuṟuttaliṉ - periya
maliyiluḷḷa ematucīṟūriṉ teruviṉi iruḷakaṟṟi viḷaṅkacceytalālē,
15. niṉ malarntamārpu - niṉ akaṉṟa mārpiṉ kūṭṭattiṉi;
13. iravum iḻantaṉaḷ- iraviṉum iḻantāḷ āvaḷ;
1-3. mati arumpu aṉṉa- iḷiya matiyiṉi yotta, mācu aṟu cuṭar nutal ivaḷatukuṟṟamaṟṟa
oḷḷiya neṟṟi, poṉ nēr vaṇṇamkoṇṭaṉṟu-poṉṉi yotta niṟattiṉikkoṇṭatu; aṉṉō -
antō, yāṅku ākuvaḷ kol -eṅṅaṉam ākuvaḷō;
11. aḷiyaḷ -iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) perumali nāṭa! ēṉal poṟi uyirttaṉa; (ākaliṉem talivi iṉi
iṟceṟikkappaṭuvāḷ;) nīpālnāḷ vantu aḷikkuvi eṉiṉē, aravumiḻtirumaṇi maṟuku
viḷakkuṟuttaliṉ, niṉmār piṉiiravum iḻantaṉaḷ; cuṭarnutal poṉ nēr vaṇṇamkoṇṭaṉṟu;
aṉṉō yāṅkākuvaḷ kol; aḷiyaḷ.
(vi - ri.) nutal poṉnēr vaṇṇam koṇṭaṉṟu eṉṟatu pacalipūttatu eṉṟapaṭi. vicumpiṉ
vil eṉka. eyyāvil eṉṟatu vāṉa villiṟku veḷippaṭi. vari -aḻaku. ēṉal poṟi
uyirttaṉa eṉṟatu,tiṉiyaṟukkuṅ kālamākaliṉ taliviiṟceṟikkappaṭuvaḷ eṉpatu kūṟi,
ataṉālpakaṟkuṟi ariteṉṟu uṇarttiyavāṟām. tirumaṇimaṟuku viḷakkuṟuttaliṉ eṉpataṉāl
iravukkuṟiyiṭiyīṭu uṇartti, ataṉāl aḵtum ariteṉuṇarttuvāḷ niṉ mārpiṉi iravu
iḻantaṉaḷeṉṟāḷ. iravum eṉṉum ummi eccap poruṭṭu.tāṉē eṉpatu kaṭṭuric cuvipaṭa
niṉṟatu.
193. pāli
[poruḷa valittaneñciṟkuc collit talimakaṉ celavuaḻuṅkiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-11. neñcē, kāṉ uyar maruṅkil kavali allatu -uyarnta kāṭṭiṉiṭattēyuḷḷa
kavartta neṟikaḷiyaṉṟi, vāṉam vēṇṭā vil ēr uḻavar-maḻiyivēṇṭutalillāta villākiya
ērāl uḻutalicceyyumāṟalippōr, kiḷi eḻa vāytta-tamkiḷiñaruṭaṉ eḻāniṟkak kiṭitta,
perunāḷvēṭṭam - naṉṉāḷ vēṭṭattil, poru kaḷattuoḻinta-avarkaḷ poruta kaḷattil āṟu
celvāruṭampiṉiṉṟum ukka, kuruti cevvāy -kurutiyiṉi uṇṭamiyāl civanta
vāyiṉiyum,poṟitta pōlum vāl niṟa eruttiṉ -poṟittu vittāṟpōlum veṇṇiṟam
vāyntakaḻuttiṉiyum, aṇinta pōlum ceñcevi eruviceytu cūṭṭiyatu pōlum civanta
ceviyiṉiyumuṭiyakaḻuku, kuṟumpoṟi eḻunta neṭuntāḷ yāattu - kuṟiyamaliyiṭattē
vaḷarnta neṭiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya yāmarattiṉ, aruṅ kavaṭṭu uyarciṉip piḷḷi ūṭṭa- ariya
kavaṭṭiṉniṉ ṟeḻunta uyarntakiḷikaḷiluḷḷa taṉ kuñciṉi uṇpikka, virintuvāy vaḻukkiya
koḻu kaṇ ūṉ taṭi - viritaliṉālēak kuñciṉ vāyiṉiṉṟum tavaṟi vīḻntakoḻumiyuṟṟa
kaṇṇākiya ūṉiṉ tuṇṭu, tolpacimutunari valci ākum curaṉ - paḻiya
paciyiṉiyuṭiyamutiya narikku uṇavākum kāṭukaḷ, namakku eḷiya maṉ -nām cellutaṟku
mikavum eḷiyaṉavē āyiṉum;
11-4. nam maṉi-namatunalla maṉiyiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa, pal māṇ taṅkiya- palamāṇpum uṟṟa, cāyal -
meṉmittaṉmiyiṉiyum,iṉmoḻi - iṉiya moḻiyiṉiyum, muruntu ērmuṟuval -
mayiliṟakiṉ aṭiyiṉi otta paṟkaḷiyumuṭiya,iḷiyōḷ - iḷiyōḷāya nam taliviyiṉ,perutōḷ
iṉ tuyil - periya tōḷiṉpāleytum iṉiya tuyiliṉi, kiviṭukalaṉ -
kiviṭutaliāṟṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! curaṉ namakku eḷiyamaṉ; āyiṉum, iḷiyōḷ peruntōḷ iṉtuyil
kiviṭukalaṉ.
(vi - ri.) uḻutoḻil ceyvārkku maḻi tuṇik kāraṇamāmeṉpatu, 1'ēriṉuḻāar uḻavar
puyaleṉṉum, vāri vaḷaṅkuṉṟikkāl eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām; āyiṉum,āṟalippārākiya villēr
uḻavarkkuk kāṭṭiṉkavarneṟi yallatu maḻi tuṇiyātal illiyākaliṉ,'kavali yallatu vāṉam
vēṇṭā villēr uḻavar'eṉṟār. avarkku vēṭṭi peritum vāyttanāḷ ciṟanta nāḷ ākaliṉ,
atu perunāḷeṉappaṭṭatu. kiḷi yeḻa eṉpataṟku āṟu celvārkiḷiyuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu vara
eṉṟurittalumām.kurutic cevvāy - kurutiyi yuṇṭamiyāṟ civantavāy eṉa
viritturikka. aṇintapōlumceñcevi eṉṟa karuttu, 2'ūṉpatittaṉṉa veruvaru ceñcevi
eruvic cēval' eṉamuṉṉarp pōntamiyuṅ kāṇka.
yāmarattiṉuyarcci mikuti kūṟuvār neṭuntāḷ yāattueṉṟum, aruṅkavaṭṭu uyar ciṉi
eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.virintu ūṭṭa eṉṟu kūṭṭi yurittalumām.
eruvi kuñciṟkukkaṇṇiṉik koṇarntu uṇavākak koṭuppatu, 3'kaṇṇumiḻkaḻukiṉ kāṉam'
eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum, tolpaci - palnāḷ uṇaviṉmiyāl eytiya paci. kolpaci eṉṉum
pāṭattiṟku varuttum paciyeṉṟurikka. talivaṉ curattiṟ cellutaṟkuañcukiṉṟilēṉ
eṉpāṉ, 'curaṉ namakku eḷiyamaṉ' eṉṟum, eṉiṉum taliviyiṉ tōḷiṟpeṟum iṉiya
tuyilik kiviṭṭup pōtal iyalāteṉpāṉ 'iḷiyōḷ peruntōḷ iṉṟuyilkiviṭukalaṉē'
eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. avaḷkiviṭaṟkuariyāḷ eṉpataṉip 'paṉ māṇ taṅkiya cāyaliṉmoḻi
muruntēr muṟuval iḷiyōḷ'eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ.

194. mulli
[paruvaṅ kaṇṭu āṟṟāmimītūrat talimakaḷ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 16-9. tōḻi-, pōrmika-pōrmikku mūṇṭatāka,koṭuñci neṭutēr
pūṇṭa-koṭuñci yeṉṉumuṟuppiṉi yuṭiya neṭiya tēriṟ pūṭṭappeṟṟa,kaṭumpari viri uḷi
nalmāṉ kaṭiiya-virintacelaviṉiyum virinta piṭari mayiriṉiyum uṭiyanalla kutiriyic
celutti, varutum eṉṟu avarteḷitta pōḻtu - iṉṉa pōḻtil varuvēm eṉṟunam talivar
terivitta kālam;
1-16. pēr uṟitaliiya periya maḻipeyta, perum pular vikaṟi-iruḷperitum pularnta
kālip poḻutilē, ēr iṭampaṭutta irumaṟu-ērkaḷāl iṭamuṇṭāka uḻappeṟṟaperiya
vaṭuviṉiyuṭiya, pūḻi puṟam māṟupeṟṟa - kīḻmēlākap puraṇṭa puḻutiyiyuṭiya, pūval
īrattu -cemmaṇnilattiṉ īrampaṭṭa cevviyil, ūṉkiḻittaṉṉa cem cuval neṭucāl -
ūṉikkiḻittālotta cevviya mēṭṭunilattippiḷantu ceṉṟa neṭiya paṭiccāliṉiṭattu,
vittiyamaruṅkil - vititta iṭaṅkaḷil, viti palanāṟiya-vitikaḷ palavum muḷittu vaḷara,
iṟalinalmāṉ iṉam parantavipōl - nalla kalimāṉiṉkūṭṭam parantaṉapōla, kōṭu uṭi talikkuṭi
cūṭiyaviṉiñar - kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya ōlik kuṭiyittaliyiṟ cūṭiya kolli uḻavar, kaṟaṅku
paṟiccīriṉ - olikkum paṟiyiṉ oliyoṭu, iṟaṅkavāṅki kaḷi kāl kaḻīiya - payirkaḷ
vaḷiyumpaṭiotukkik kaḷiyip paṟittut tūymi ceyta,perum puṉa varakiṉ - periya
kolliyil viḷintavarakiṉ, kavi katir irupuṟam katūu uṇṭa - kavittakatirkaḷiṉ kariya
puṟattiṉip paṟṟi uṇṭa, kuṭumi neṟṟi neṭu mā tōki- kuṭumi poruntiya
taliyiṉiyuṭiya nīṇṭa kariyamayil, kāmar kalavam parappi - aḻakiya tōkiyiṉivirittu, ēm
uṟa - iṉpam uṟa, kolli uḻavarkūḻ niḻal oḻitta - kolliyi uḻumviṉiñarkaḷ kūḻ uṇṭaṟku
niḻalāka viṭṭuvitta,val ilik kuruntiṉ vāṅkuciṉi iruntu - valiyiliyiṉiyuṭiya
kuruntamarattiṉ vaḷintakiḷiyiliruntu, kiḷikaṭi makaḷiriṉ viḷipaṭapayirum - kiḷikaḷi
ōṭṭum makaḷirpōloliyuṇṭāka akavum, kār itu - kār kālam ākiyituvēyākum. maṉ -
aṅṅaṉamākavum avar iṉṉumvantilarē.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talivar varutum eṉṟu teḷittapōḻtukār itumaṉ; varakiṉ katir uṇṭa
tōki kalavamparappik kurun tiṉ ciṉiyiruntu kiḷikaṭimakaḷiriṉ viḷipap payirum kār
eṉka.
(vi - ri.) ēriṭam paṭutta irumaṟup pūḻip puṟamāṟu peṟṟa pūvaleṉṟatu, puḻuti
kīḻmēlākap puraṇṭu naṉku pularumāṟupaṉ muṟi uḻuta cemmaṇ nilam eṉṟapaṭi;
1'toṭippuḻutikaḵcāuṇak kiṟ piṭitteruvum-vēṇṭātu cālappaṭum' eṉa vaḷḷuvar kūṟu
vatuṅ kāṇka. aṅṅaṉamuṇakkiya puḻutiyil maḻipeytu īram paṭṭa cevvipārttu mīṭṭum
uḻutu vititeḷippār eṉpatu,'pūval īrattu, ūṉkiḻit taṉṉa ceñcuvalneṭuñcāl,
vittiya' eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.īramuṭiya cemmaṇnilattē uḻupaṭi kiḻitta vaṭuviṉtōṟṟam,
ūṉiṉik kiḻittu vittārpōlumeṉṟār. nāṟi - nāṟa eṉat tirikka; nāṟa-muḷittavaḷara
eṉka. maḻikkut taṭiyāka viṉiñar taliyiṟcūṭiya ōlikkuṭiyiṉ iru muṉiyum
nīṇṭuuyarntirukku mākaliṉ ataṉiyuṭiya viṉiñarkaḷiraliyiṉam pōlvārāyiṉar eṉka. kaṟaṅku
paṟiccīriṉ eṉṟa iraliyiṉam pōlvārāyiṉar eṉka.kaṟaṅku paṟic cīriṉ eṉṟataṉāl,
avarkaḷkaḷipaṟikkuṅkāl, paṟiyi olippippar eṉpatupeṟṟām. kaḷikāl kaḻīiya-kaḷiyi
aṭiyuṭaṉ kaḻitteṉṟumām. tōki-mayil kāriṉik kaṇṭa mayilkaḷippuṟṟu akavutal iyal
pākaliṉ, tōki kalavamparappi ēmuṟap payirum eṉṟār. mayil kuruntiṉciṉiyiliruntu
akavuvatu kiḷikaṭiyum makaḷir,marattiṉ kiḷimītiruntu olippatu pōlum eṉka.avar
teḷitta pōḻtu ituvākavum iṉṉumvantilarē eṉpatupaṭa niṉṟamiyiṉ, maṉoḻiyiciyākum.

195. pāli
[makaṭpōkkiya naṟṟāycolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5.arum curam iṟanta eṉ perum tōḷ kuṟumakaḷ-ariyacuraneṟiyik kaṭantu
ceṉṟa eṉ periyatōḷiṉiyuṭiya iḷiyaḷāya makaḷ, tiruntu vēlviṭaliyoṭu varum eṉa-
tiruntiya vēliṉiyuṭiyatalivaṉuṭaṉ varum eṉṟu, tāy - avaḷ tāy, puṉimāṇiñci pūval
ūṭṭi - puṉital māṭcimippaṭṭamāṇpuṟṟa puṟaccuvaril cemmaṇ pūci, maṉi maṇalaṭuttu -
maṉiyiṉ muṟṟattē maṇalip peytu, mālināṟṟi - mālikaḷit toṅka viṭṭu, uvantu
iṉituayarum eṉpa - makiḻntu iṉitē (maṉiyiṉ kaṇkōlam ceyyum eṉpa ;
5-10. yāṉum-, māṉpiṇi nōkkiṉ maṭa nallāḷi-peṇ māṉ pōlumpārviyiyuṭiya maṭappam
vāynta avaḷi, īṉṟa naṭpiṟku aruḷāṉāyiṉum-īṉṟāḷ eṉṉum aṉpupaṟṟi eṉpālaruḷ
ceyyāṉāyiṉum, iṉ naki muṟuval ēḻiyi -iṉiya nakiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya paṟkaḷiyuṭiya avaḷi,
pal nāḷkūntal vāri nucuppu ivarntu ōmpiya - palanāḷumkūntali vārimuṭittu
maruṅkilē ēṟṟippātukātta, nalampuṉi utaviyum maṉ uṭiyaṉ - naṉmiporuntiya utaviyum
peritum uṭiyaḷ āvēṉ;
11. aḵtuaṟikiṟpiṉō naṉṟu - avaṉ ataṉi aṟiyiṉnaṉṟām;
12-14. aṟuvi tōyumoru peruṅkuṭumi - āṭi cūḻnta oru periyucciyiṉiyum, ciṟu
pi nāṟṟiya pal tali koṭukōl-ciṟiya pi toṅkaviṭappeṟṟa
palataliyiyuṭiyavaḷintakōliṉiyum uṭiyaṉāy, ākuvatuaṟiyum mutuvāy vēla - mēl nikaḻ
vataṉi aṟintukūṟum aṟivu vāynta vēlaṉē!
15-19. kaṅkuliṉāṉātu tuyarum - iravil amiyātu tuyaruṟum,em-emmuṭiya, māṟā
varupaṉi kaluḻum kaṇ - iṭiyaṟātuvarum nīruṭaṉ kalaṅki aḻum kaṇkaḷ, iṉitupaṭīiyar-iṉitu
tuyilum poruṭṭu, emmaṉi muntuṟattarumō-ematu maṉiyiṉkaṇ muṟpaṭak koṇarntutarumō,
(aṉṟi) taṉ maṉi uykkumō - taṉmaṉiyiṉkaṇ muṟpaṭak koṇṭu celvāṉō, yātuavaṉ kuṟippu
kūṟuvāyāka - at talimakaṉ kuṟiyi, kūṟuka.
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷ viṭaliyoru varumeṉat tāy, ūṭṭi, aṭuttunāṟṟi ayarum eṉpa:
yāṉum ōmpiya utaviyumuṭiyaṉ; aḵtaṟiciṟpiṉō naṉṟu; vēla! emmaṉimuntuṟat tarumō taṉ
maṉi muntuṟa uykkumō, avaṉkuṟippu yātu? niṉ kaḻaṅkiṉ tiṭpam kūṟuka.
(vi-ri) iñci - maṉiyi aṭuttup puṟattē cūḻnta matilpōlum cuvar. ayartal -
koṇṭāṭutalumām. yāṉumeṉṟa ummi, tāy eṉṟataṉik karuti niṟṟaliṉeccavummiyākum.
utaviyum eṉṟa ummiyum, eccavummiyē. aḵtu aṟikiṟpiṉ - utavi yākiya ataṉiaṟiyiṉ
īṉṟa naṭpiṟku aruḷāṉāyiṉumaḵtaṟikiṟ piṉō naṉṟu eṉka. aṟikiṟpiṉōnaṉṟu eṉṟatu,
aṟintu eṉ maṉikkaṭ koṇṭuvariṉnaṉṟa eṉṟapaṭi. aṟuvi tōyum oru peruṅkuṭumi
eṉṟatu,taliyilē mayirkkaṭṭākac cuṟṟi oru puṟattētoṅkaviṭṭa āṭi tōynta kuṭumi
eṉṟavāṟu,kuṭumiyiṉiyum kōliṉiyum uṭiya vēleṉka.piḻiyāta kuṟi eṉṉum poruṭṭu
maṉ, tilla,mātu, ō: acikaḷ.
(mē - ḷ.) 1'taṉṉumavaṉum avaḷuñ cuṭṭi' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu,ippāṭṭiṉik kūṟi, itu
teyvattoṭu paṭuttuppulampiyatu eṉṟum,

1'kaḷavalarāyiṉum kāmameyp paṭuppiṉum' eṉṉuñcūttirattu, 'pōkkuṭaṉ
aṟintapiṉtōḻiyoṭu keḻīik, kaṟpiṉ ākkattuniṟṟaṟkaṇṇum' eṉṉum tuṟikku 'emmaṉi
muntuṟat tarumō, taṉmaṉi yuykkumō yātavaṉ kuṟippē'eṉṟa pakutiyi utāraṇamākak kāṭṭi,
(itu) 'kaṟpiṉākkattukkaruttu nikaḻtal' eṉṟum 2'veḷippaṭavaritalpaṭāmi varital' eṉṉuñ
cūttirattu, 'emmaṉi.. . . . .yātavaṉ kuṟippē' (itu) 'pōlvaṉaveḷippaṭuvataṉ muḷṉark
koṇṭu talikkaḻintuḻik koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇamnikaḻntamiyiṉ atuvum veḷippaṭāmal
varintatām' eṉṟum, 3'koṭuppōriṉṟiyum karaṇamuṇṭē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu,icceyyuḷ
koṭuppō riṉṟik karaṇamnikaḻntatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
4'maṟiveḷippaṭutalum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu 'emmaṉi. . . . . .yātavaṉ kuṟippē' eṉpatu
maṟi veḷippāṭueṉṟaṉar, pērā.

196. marutam
[parattiyiṟpirintuvanta talimakaṟkuk kiḻatti colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. neṭu koṭi nuṭaṅkumnaṟavumalipākkattu-neṭiyakoṭikaḷ aciyum kaḷ
malinta pākkattilē, nāḷtuṟip paṭṭa mōṭṭu iru varāal-viṭiyaṟkālivēṭṭattut tuṟikkaṇ
akappaṭṭa periyaakaṭṭiṉiyuṭiya varāl mīṉiṉ, tuṭikaṇkoḻukuṟinoṭuttu - tuṭiyiṉ
kaṇpōṉṟu akaṉṟa vaḷaviyatuṇṭat tiṉi viṟṟu, uṇṭu āṭi-kaḷḷuṇṭu
āṭi,vēṭṭammaṟantu-mīṇṭum vēṭṭam pōtali maṟantu,tuñcum koḻunarkku-uṟaṅkik
kiṭakkumkaṇavaṉmārkku, pāṭṭi - (avaravar maṉiviyarāya)pāṇ makaḷir, āmpal akalili -
āmpalatu akaṉṟiliyil, amali vemcōṟu tiraṇṭa viruppantarumcōṟṟi, tīmpuḷi pirampiṉ
tiraḷ kaṉi peytu -pirampiṉ iṉippuṭaṉ kūṭiya puḷippiṉiyuṭiyatiraṇṭa paḻattiṉip
peytu, viṭiyal vikaṟi iṭūumūra - iruḷ pularum viṭiyaṟ kālattē iṭum ūraṉē!
8-13. tantikaṇkaviṉ aḻittataṉ tappal-taṉ tantiyiṉkaṇṇiṉ eḻilik keṭuttatākiya
tavaṟṟiṟkāka,oṉṟu moḻik kōcar - neṭumoḻiyiṉiyuṭiyakōcarkaḷi, kaṭutēr titiyaṉ -
virintatēriṉiyuṭiya titiyaṉatu, aḻunti - aḻuntūreṉṉumiṭattu, teṟuvarakkoṉṟu
muraṇ pōkiya -accam uṇṭākak kolvittu māṟupāṭu tīrnta,koṭuṅkuḻi aṉṉi
miñiliyiṉ - vaḷinta kuḻiyiṉiaṇinta aṉṉi miñili eṉpāḷippōla, iyalum -kaḷippuṟṟu
naṭakkum, niṉnala takuviyi-niṉnalattiṟkut takkāḷāya parattiyi, muyaṅkiyamārpu -
taḻuviya mārpiṉi, toṭu kalam-tīṇṭēm,kuṟuka vāral - emmi aṇuka vāraṟka.
(muṭipu) ūra! aṉṉi miñiliyiṉ iyalum niṉnalat takuviyimuyaṅkiya mārpiṉit
toṭukalam; kuṟukavāral:
vēṭṭam maṟantutuñcum koḻunarkkup pāṭṭi amali veñcōṟupirampiṉ kaṉipeytu vikaṟi
iṭūum ūra eṉka.
(vi - ri.) noṭuttu - viṟṟu. pāṭṭi pāṇmakaḷ ; pāṇaṉmaṉivi. pāṭṭiyar eṉap
paṉmiyākakkoḷka.viṭiyal vikaṟi-viṭiyaṟku muṉṉarttākiya vikaṟieṉpar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.
1tappal-tappaṟkueṉa nāṉkaṉurupu virikka. kōcar eṉpār. taṉtantiyi aruḷiṉṟik kaṇ
kaḷintamiyiṉ, aṉṉimiñili eṉpāḷ ciṉam koṇṭu, kuṟumpiyaṉtitiyaṉ eṉpavarkaḷāl ak
kōcarikkolvittu māṟupāṭutīrntu kaḷippuṟṟāḷeṉpatu, 2'vāymoḻittantiyik kaṇkaḷintu
aruḷātu, ūrmutu kōcarnavitta ciṟumiyil, kalattum uṇṇāḷ vālitumuṭāaḷ, ciṉattiṉ
koṇṭa paṭivam māṟāḷ,maṟaṅkeḻu tāṉik koṟṟakkuṟumpiyaṉ, ceruviyalnaṉmāṉ titiyaṟ
kurittavar, iṉṉuyirceruppak kaṇṭuciṉa māṟiya, aṉṉi miñili'eṉpataṉāṟ pōtarum.
koṉṟu-kolvittu eṉappiṟaviṉiyākak koḷka.

iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntatu aḻuntūrileṉpatu, titiyaṉ aḻunti eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.niṉ pirivāl
yāṉumiṟparatti mutalāyiṉārumvaruntumpaṭi ceytu, cērip parattiyāṉavaḷperumitaṅ
koṇṭirukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ, aṉṉimiñiliyiṉ iyalum niṉṉalat takuviyi eṉattalimakaḷ
kūṟiṉāḷ. niṉ nalatti nukarvataṟkuttakkavaḷākiya ap parattiyi muyaṅkiya mārpiyām
tīṇṭutalum takāteṉpāḷ toṭu kalameṉṟum, kuṟukavāral eṉṟum kūṟiṉaḷ.
(u -ṟi) 'varāaṟ koḻuṅkuṟi noṭuttu uṇṭu āṭi vēṭṭammaṟantu
tuñcumkoḻunarkkuppāṭṭiyaramaliveñcōṟu vikaṟiiṭūum ūra' eṉṟatu, talivaṉ
iravellāmparattik kut taṉ mārpiṉi nalki avaḷ tarumiṉpatti nukarntu, taliviyi
maṟantoḻuki,viṭiyalil taliviyi niṉintu varukiṉṟāṉeṉṟapaṭi.
197. pāli
[piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭatalimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, māmalar vaṇṇam iḻanta kaṇṇum -kariya kuvaḷi malariṉ
aḻakiṉi iḻanta kaṇkaḷum, pūnekiḻ aṇiyil cāaya tōḷum - aḻaku nekiḻntutirinta aṇi
pōṉṟa melinta tōḷkaḷum(koṇṭu), naṉṉar mākkaḷ viḻivaṉar āyntatol nalam iḻanta
tuyaramoṭu - naṉmiyuṭiyaayamakkaḷ virumpi ārāyum paḻiya nalattiṉiiḻantataṉālāya
tuyarattuṭaṉ, eṉṉatūum iṉiyal -ciṟitum varuntātē ;
10-7. tōḷtāḻpuiruḷiya kuvi iru kūntal - tōḷkaḷil tāḻntuiruṇṭa tiraṇṭa
periyakūntaliyuṭiya, maṭavōḷtaḻīiya viṟalōṉ mārpil-maṭappattiyuṭiyamaṉiviyit taḻuviyuḷḷa
mēmpaṭṭa talivaṉmārpil, puṉtalip putalvaṉūrpuiḻintāṅku-pulliya taliyiṉiyuṭiya
putalvaṉēṟi iṟaṅkuvatu pōṉṟu, kaṭucūl maṭappiṭi taḻīiyaveṇkōṭṭu iṉam cāl
vēḻam-niṟintacūliṉiyuṭiya iḷiya piṭiyiṉit taḻuvikkiṭantaveḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya
iṉattuṭaṉ kūṭiya kaḷiṟu,kaṉṟu ūrpu iḻitara-kaṉṟu tammītuēṟi iṟaṅka,paḷḷikoḷḷum
paṉiccuramnīnti-paṭuttirukkum naṭukkattiṉittarumcuraneṟiyik kaṭantu, oḷ iṇark koṉṟi
ōṅkumali attam - oḷḷiya pūṅkottukkaḷiyuṭiyakoṉṟi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya ōṅkiya
maliyiyaṭuttaneṟiyil, viṉivali yuṟūm neñcamoṭu -poruḷīṭṭum viṉiyi vaṟpuṟukkum
neñcattāl;
9. niṉ niri vaḷinekiḻa-niṉatu nirittu vaḷikaḷ nekiḻum paṭi;
18. iṉiyar ākinampirinticiṉōr - iṅṅaṉam aruḷilarākinammip pirintu ceṉṟa talivar;
5 - 9. muṉi eḻamuṉṉuvar ōṭṭiya-pōrceyya muṟpaṭṭōriōṭṭiya, muraṇmiku
tiruviṉ-valimikka vīraccelvattiṉiyum, māmiku tāṉi-vīramikkapaṭiyiṉiyumuṭiya, kaṇṇaṉ
eḻiṉi-kaṇṇaṉ eḻiṉieṉpāṉatu, tēmmutu kuṉṟamiṟantaṉarāyiṉum-tēṉmikka
mutukaṉṟattiṉikkaṭantuḷarāyiṉum, nīṭalar-nīṭṭit tirātu vallēvantuṟuvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! toṉṉalamiḻanta tuyaramoṭu iṉiyal;paṉiccuram nīnti. ōṅkumali
attamviṉivaliyuṟūum neñcamoṭu, niṉ nirivaḷi nekiḻiṉiyarāki. nap pirintaciṉōr,
kaṇṇaṉ eḻiṉimutukuṉṟam iṟantaṉarāyiṉum nīṭalar.
(vi - ri.) pūnekiḻ aṇi - nekiḻcciyuṟṟa malaraṇi eṉṟumām. aṇi - taliyaṇi, kaṇṇum
tōḷum koṇṭu eṉorucol varuvitturikka. viḻivaṉar āynteṉṟatu, taliviyiṉ nalatti
ārāytal avarkkuōr viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṟapaṭi. yāḻa, aci. maṭap piṭikkumaṭavōḷum ataṉit
taḻuviya vēḻattiṟku viṟalōṉumataṉmītu ēṟi iṟaṅkum kaṉṟukkup putalvaṉamuvamam. kaṭuñcūl
maṭappiṭi eṉṟamiyāl cūluṟṟamaṭavōḷ eṉṟa koḷka. kaṭuñcūl mutaṟ cūlumām.
----------
198. kuṟiñci
[puṇarntu nīṅkiyatalimakaḷatu pōkku nōkkiya talimakaṉ taṉneñciṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 18. eṉ neñcē-,
1-11. karanta kāmam - naṅmamuḷmaṟintuṟiyum kāmattiṉi, kūṟuvam kollōkūṟulam kol
eṉa - ivaṭkuk kūṟuvēmō aṉṟikkūṟātu viṭuvēmō eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam eṇṇi eṇṇi,
kiniṟukkallātu-(muṭivil) aṭakka iyalātu, nayantu nāmviṭṭa naṉ moḻa nampi - virumpi
nāmveḷiyiṭṭiranta iṉiya moḻayi virumpi, ari nāḷyāmattu - pātiyiravil, viḻumaḻi
karantu-peyyum mikka maḻiyil maṟintu, kārviri kamaḻum kūntal-kār kālattu maṇam
kamaḻumvantaluṭaṉ, tū viṉi naṇ nūl ākam poruntiṉaḷ -tūya toḻiṟpāṭaminta nuṇṇiya
nūlālākiya āṭi taṉuṭampiṉkaṇṇē poruntap peṟṟavaḷāy,veṟpiṉ iḷamaḻi cūḻnta maṭamayilpōla
-maliyiṭattuḷḷa iḷamukil cūḻnta maṭappattiyuṭiyamayilpōla, vaṇṭu vaḻip paṭara taṇ malar
vēyntu -vaṇṭukaḷ piṉṉē toṭarak kuḷirnta malarkaḷiccūṭi, vil vakuppu uṟṟa nalvaṅku
kuṭaccūl -villiṉippōlum vaki yamintanaṉmiporuntiya kuṭaccūl ākiya, am
cilampuoṭukki - aḻakiya cilampiṉi oliyātu aṭakki,añciṉaḷ vantu - accattuṭaṉ vantu,
ūr tuñcuyāmattu - ūrmuḻutum tuyilum yāmat tilē,muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ -
nammittaḻuimīḷvōḷ;
12 - 3. -ṉṟa kaṟpilcāṉṟa periya am mā ariviyō allaḷ - niṟintakaṟpiṉāl uyarnta
perumiyuṟṟa aḻakiya māminiṟamuṭiya peṇṇō allaḷ;
13 - 7. teṉāatuāayanalnāṭṭu aṇaṅka uṭi cilampil -teṟkiṉkaṇ uḷḷatākiya āy eṉpāṉatu
nallanāṭṭil teyvattiyuṭiya maliyil, kavirampeyariya urukeḻu kavāṉ - kaviram eṉum
peyariyuṭiyaaccam poruntiya pakkamaliyil, nērmalarniṟi cuṉi uṟiyum - melliya
malarkaḷiyuṭiyaniṟinta cuṉiyil uṟiyum, cūrmakaḷ eṉṉum -cūraramakaḷē yāvaḷ eṉpēm.
(muṭipu) eṉ neñcē! nām viṭṭa naṉmoḻi nampi, taṇmalar vēyntu añcilam
poṭukki,maṭamayilpōla añciṉaḷ vantu yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷpeyarvōḷ ariviyō allaḷ;
caṉiyuṟiyumcūrmakaḷ eṉṉum.
(vi - ri.) kūntalum nuṇnūlāṭiyum, uṭampiṉ piṉṉammuṉṉum poruntiyuḷḷa taliviyiṉ
tōṟṟam,iḷamēkam cūḻnta mayil pōlvatāyiṟ ṟeṉka.kuṭaccūl-cūluṟṟatupōl
puṭipaṭaṭirukkum cilampu.kuṭaccūl ākiya cilampu eṉa irupeyaroṭṭākakkoḷka. kuṭiccūl
eṉap pāṭaṅ koḷḷalumām; 1'pacumpoṉkuṭiccūl cittirac cilampu' eṉpatu kāṇka.
'veḷippāṭṭiṟkuañcik karantu cilampoṭukki ūrtuñcu yāmattuvantu muyaṅkip peyarvāḷ
āyiṉaḷ; aṅṅaṉamvantu celvōḷi ōr arivi yeṉṟu kūṟutaṟkueḷiyaḷ allaḷ; ākaliṉ avaḷic
cuṉiyuṟiyumteyvam eṉpēm;' eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉneñciṟku viyantu kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. tuñcu
ūryāmam eṉpataṉi ūr tuñcu yāmam eṉa māṟuka. ērmalar eṉakkeṇṭu aḻakiya malar
eṉṟalumām.eṉṉum-eṉpēm eṉṉum poruṭṭu.

(mē - ḷ.) 1'paṇpilpeyarppiṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu icceyyuḷikāṭṭi, itu iravuk
kuṟikkaṇ avaṭpeṟṟumalintatueṉṟum, 2'iravukkuṟiyē illakat tuḷḷum, maṉiyōr
kiḷavikēṭkum vaḻiyatuvē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, 'añcilampoṭukki yaṅciṉaḷ vantu,
tuñcūr yāmattumuyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ' eṉa varuvatu 'maṉiyōrkiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatu'
eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

199. pāli
[poruḷ kaṭikkūṭṭiyaneñciṟkut talimakaṉ colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 16. neñcē-,
1-13. kari pāy veṇtirikaṭuppa - karikkaṇṇē pāyum veḷḷiya aliyioppa, niri kāl
oṟṟaliṉ - variciyāka varumkāṟṟu mōtutaliṉ, pala uṭaṉ cērpu kal utirum -palavum
oruṅkē cērntu pāṟiyil utirkiṉṟa,vari cēr marāattu ūḻ malar - maliyiṉkaṇporuntiya
veṇkaṭampiṉ muṟṟiya malari, peyalcettu - maḻiyeṉṟu karuti, uyaṅkal yāṉi nīrnacikku
alamara-nīr vēṭkiyāl varuntutaliyuṭiṭayayaṉi atiti taṇit taṟkuc ceṉṟa cuḻala,
cilampivalanta vaṟuciṉi vaṟṟal - cilampi nūlpiṉṉappeṟṟa vaṟiya ciṉiyiṉiyuṭiya
vaṟṟalākiya, alaṅkal ulavi ari niḻal acii-acikiṉṟamaraṅkaḷiṉ aṟalpaṭṭa niḻalil taṅki,
tiraṅkumaral kavviya kiyaṟu tokunili - vāṭiya marikikavviya ceyalaṟṟa kūṭṭattil
(pukuntu), aram tiṉūci tiraḷ nuti aṉṉa - arattāl arāvappaṭṭūciyiṉ tiraṇṭa muṉiyi
yotta, tiṇ nilieyiṟṟa cennāy eṭuttaliṉ-uṟuti poruntiyapaṟkaḷiyuṭiya
cennāytākkutaliṉ, vaḷimuṉippūḷiyiṉ oy eṉṟu alaṟiya - kāṟṟiṉmuṉ pūḷippūveṉa
virintu alaṟi yōṭiya, keṭumāṉ iṉaniritarīiya - kāṇātoḻinta taṉ iṉa
mākiyamāṉkūṭṭattik kūṭṭaṟku, kali - kalimāṉ,katirmāy māli - ñāyiṟu
maṟintamālippōḻtil, āṇ kural viḷikkum - taṉatuāṇ kural tōṉṟa aḻikkum, kaṭal ēāl
kāṉampiṟpaṭa-kaṭalpōl akaṉṟa kāṭu piṉṉē pōka;
13-6. piṟarpōl -ētilarippōla, celvēm āyiṉ em celavu naṉṟueṉṉum - nam taliviyip
pirintu pōvēmāyiṉematu pōkku naṉṟākum eṉṟeṇṇum, āci uḷḷamacivu iṉṟu turappa -
poruḷāciyuṟṟa niṉ uḷḷamtaḷartaliṉṟic celutta, nī celaṟku uriyi nīcellutaṟku uriyi
āvāy;
16-24. yāṉē - yāṉō,vēypōl taṭiyiṉa maṉṉum-mūṅkilippōlavaḷintaṉavākip poruntum,
taṇṇiya tiraṇṭaperutōḷ arivi oḻiya - kuḷirnta tiraṇṭa periyatōḷiṉiyuṭiya nam talivi
(ivaṇ taṉittu) oḻiya,valampaṭu koṟṟam tanta vāy vāḷ - veṉṟaveṟṟiyiṉit tanta vāytata
vāḷiṉiyuṭiya,kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭiccēral-kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēral eṉṉum
cēra maṉṉaṉ,kuṭāatu irumpoṉ vāki perantuṟic ceruvil - mēṟkiṉkaṇṇatākiya
periyapoṉṉiṉiyuṭiya vāki maramniṟkam peruntuṟi eṉṉumiṭattu nikaḻntapōril,
polampūṇ naṉṉaṉ - poṟpūṇ aṇintanaṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉ, porutu kaḷattu oḻiya-pōrceytu kaḷattil
maṭiya, iḻanta nāṭu tantaṉṉa - tāṉmuṉpu iḻanta nāṭṭip peṟṟālotta,vaḷamperitu
peṟiṉum - periya celvattippeṟuvatāyiṉum, vāraleṉ - varuvēṉ allēṉ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! piṟarpōl (taliviyip pirintu)celvē māyiṉ em celavu
naṉṟeṉṉumāciyuḷḷam turappa; kaṭalpōl kāṉam piṟpaṭa nīcelaṟkariyi; yāṉ kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi
nārmuṭiccēral vākip peruntuṟic ceruvil naṉṉaṉāṉavaṉporutu kaḷattu oḻintamiyāl,
tāṉ iḻanta nāṭutantaṉṉa vaḷam peritu peṟiṉum peruntōḷarivi oḻiya vāraleṉ.
(vi - ri.) niri niriyāka marāmarattiṉmēṟ pāyntukalakkum kāṟṟiṟku muṟi muṟiyākak
kariyiṟpāyayum tiri uvamiyākum. marāattu utiruma malareṉak vaṭṭuka. nīr nacikku-nīr
naciyāl.vaṟuñciṉi - taḻiyaṟṟa kiḷi. vaṟṟal - vaṟṟiyamaraṅkaḷ. ulavi-maracceṟivu. kāṭṭiṉ
virivutōṉṟak kaṭalpōl kāṉam eṉṟār.aṉpuṭiyār piriyak karutārākaliṉ
piṟarpōlcelvōmāyiṉ eṉṟāṉ. piṟarpōl nīcelaṟku uriyi eṉṟa iyit turit
talumām.vākimaram niṟṟaliṉ, peruntuṟiyāṉatu vākippaṟantali eṉavum paṭum;
ivvākiyāṉatu naṉṉaṉkāvalmaram eṉpatum, ataṉi nārmuṭiccēraltaṭintāṉ eṉpatum,
1'poṉṉaṅkaṇṇip polantēr naṉṉar, cuṭarvī vākikkaṭimutal taṭinta, tārmika mintiṉ
nārmuṭiccēral' eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. 2'uruḷpūṅkaṭampiṉ peruvāyil naṉṉaṉi, nilic
ceruviṉāṟṟali yaṟattavaṉ, poṉpaṭuvāki muḻumutal taṭintu'eṉpataṉāl kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil
eṉpatumvākipperuntuṟi eṉpatum ōriṭattiyēkuṟippaṉapōlum. kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil
naṉṉaṉūr eṉpar patiṟṟappattu uriyāciriyar.kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēral
eṉṉumpeyarkkuk kāraṇam kūṟuvārāyp patiṟṟuppattuuriyāciriyar 3'kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi
nārmuṭi eṉṟatu, kaḷaṅkāyāṟ ceytakaṇṇiyum nārāṟ ceyta muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu'eṉavum,
'tāṉ muṭi cūṭukiṉṟa kālattu orukāraṇattāl muṭittaṟkut takka kaṇṇiyum
muṭiyumutavāmiyiṟ kaḷaṅkāyāl kaṇṇiyum nārālmuṭiyum ceytu koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟavāṟu'
eṉavumurittār. iḻantanāṭu tantaṉṉa eṉṟataṉāl, muṉ naṉṉaṉpāl iḻanta nāṭṭi mīḷappeṟṟaṉaṉ
eṉpata pōtarum. nāṭu tantaṉṉa vaḷameṉṟiruppiṉum tanta nāṭu pōlum vaḷam eṉpatukaruttākak
koḷka.

----------
(mē - ḷ.) 1'celaviṭiaḻuṅkal cellāmi yaṉṟē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu,'kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi. .
. . . . vāraleṉ yāṉē'eṉṉum aṭikaḷik kāṭṭi, ivi vaṉpuṟi
kuṟittuccelavaḻuṅkutaliṉ pāliyāyiṟṟu eṉṟār. nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

200. neytal
[talimakaḷ kuṟippaṟintatōḻi talimakaṟkuk kuṟinayappak kūṟiyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 7. vaḷimēy parappa - caṅkukaḷ mēyum kaṭaṟparappiṉiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
1-7. nilāviṉilaṅku maṇal mali maṟukiṉ - nilāvip pōlaviḷaṅkum maṇal mikka
teruviluḷḷa, pulvēykurampi - pullāl vēyappeṟṟa kuṭicikaḷiyuṭiya,pulāl am
cēri-pulāl nāṟum cēriyākiya empati, ūr eṉa uṇarāc ciṟumiyoṭu-ōr ūr
eṉṟuuṇaralākāta ciṟumiyiṉatāy, nīr uṭuttu - nīrkuḻappeṟṟu, iṉṉā uṟiyuṭṭu
āyiṉum- pelivaṟṟa uṟiyuḷiyuṭiya tāyiṉum, iṉpam-iṉpamtarumāṟṟāl,
orunāḷuṟinticiṉōrkkum-orunāḷtaṅkiyiruntārkkum, vaḻi nāḷ-piṟṟi nāḷ,tampati
maṟakkum paṇpiṉ - tammuṭiya patiyiṉiyēmaṟantiṭac ceyyum paṇpiṉiyuṭimiyāl, empati -
ematu appatikku, vantaṉi ceṉmō - vantucelvāyāka;
8-14. pommalpaṭutiri kam eṉa uṭitarum-polivuṟṟa olikkumali virintu citarntiṭum,
maraṉ ōṅku oruciṟi -maram ōṅkiya oru pakkattē, palapārāṭṭi - palavāyaniṉ kuṇaṅkaḷi
yāṅkaḷ pārāṭṭa, elli emmoṭukaḻippi - pakaṟpoḻutiṉi emmuṭaṉiruntu kaḻittu,el
uṟa - iravu uṟaṅkāl, naltēr pūṭṭalumuriyīr - numatu nalla tēriṉip
pūṭṭiccellutaṟkum uriyīrāvir;aṟṟaṉṟu-atuvēyumaṉṟi, cērntaṉir celkuvirāyiṉ -
iravil em patiyil taṅkiccelluvīrāyiṉ, yāmum emvari aḷaviyilpeṭkuvam - yāṅkaḷum
eṅkaḷukku iyaṉṟa aḷavilpēṇutal ceyvēm; num oppatuvō - (itu)
numkaruttiṟkicivatāmō? emakku uritticiṉ -emakkuk kūṟuvīrāka.
(muṭipu) em cēri iṉṉā uṟiyuṭṭu āyiṉum, iṉpam orunāḷ uṟinticiṉōrkkum
vaḻināḷ tampatimaṟakkum paṇpiṉiyuṭiya miyāl, empati vantaṉiceṉmō; elli emmoṭu
kaḻappi elluṟa naltērpūṭṭalum uriyīr; aṟṟaṉṟu, cērntaṉircelkuvir āyiṉ, yāmum em
vari aḷaviyiṟpeṭkavam; num oppatuvō emakku uritticiṉ.
(vi - ri.) pulvēy kurampi: pul - paṉimaṭṭi mutaliyaṉa. pulālnāṟutalāṉum, pulvēy
kurampi yuṭimiyāṉum,nīruṭutta lāṉum, cēri iṉṉā uṟiyuṭṭu āyiṟṟu.aṉṉatāyiṉum
aṉpuṭiyāruṭaṉ kūṭi nukarum iṉpamperituṭiyatu eṉpāḷ. 'iṉpam, oru
nāḷuṟinticiṉōrkkum vaḻināḷ, tam patimaṟakkam paṇpiṉ em pati' eṉṟāḷ. oru
nāḷuṟinticiṉōrkkum eṉpataṉāl, palanāḷuṟivār peṟum iṉpam
kūṟavēṇṭātāyiṟṟu.pārāṭṭi-pārāṭṭa eṉat tirikka. naltērpūṭṭalum eṉṟatu, ataṉ kāriya
mākiya celkiyiuṇarttiṟṟu. aṟṟaṉṟu-aḵtaṉṟi, vantucellutaleṉṉum potumi karuti
iraṇṭiṉiyum oppatuvō eṉorumiyāṟ kūṟiṉāḷ.

201. pāli
[piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭatalimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-12. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, amma - nāṉ kūṟuvataṉikkēṭpāyāka; poṉṉiṉ avir
eḻilnuṭaṅkum-poṉṉiṉālākiya viṭṭu viḷaṅkumaḻakiyoḷi aciyum, aṇikiḷar ōṭi -
aḻukumikkaneṟṟip paṭṭattiṉiyuṭiya, viṉi navil yāṉi -ēārc ceyaliṟ payiṉṟa
yāṉikaḷiyuṭiya,viṟalpōrp pāṇṭiyaṉ - pōrilveṟṟikoḷḷum pāṇṭiyaṉatu, pukaḻmali
ciṟappiṉkoṟki muṉtuṟi-pukaḻmikka ciṟappiṉiyuṭiyakoṟkip patiyiṉ kaṭaṟṟuṟiyilē,
avir katirmutamoṭu valampuri corintu-viḷaṅkumoḷiyiṉiyuṭiya muttukkaḷuṭaṉ
valampuriccaṅkiṉiyum corintu, taḻi aṇip polintakōṭu ēntu alkul paḻiyar makaḷir -
taḻiyuṭiaṇitalāṟ polivuṟṟa pakkam uyarntaalkuliṉiyuṭiya paḻiyaratu peṇṭir, paṉittuṟi
parava -kuḷirntatuṟikkaṇ teyvattiṉip paravāniṟka,pakalōṉ maṟinta anti āriṭi-ñāyiṟu
maṟintaantiyākiya ariya pōḻtilē, urukeḻu peruṅ kaṭaluvavuk kiḷarntāṅku-accam
poruntiya periyakaṭalāṉatu niṟimati nāḷil āravāritteḻuntāṟpōla, alarum maṉṟu
paṭṭaṉṟu-
(ūrārkūṟum) alarum maṉṟiṉkaṇ paraviyatu, aṉṉiyumporuntāk kaṇṇaḷ veyya
uyirkkum-tāyumkaṇtuyilāḷāy vemmiyuṭiyaṉavāka mūcceṟiyum,eṉṟu-eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam vaṟi,
evaṉ kiyaṟṟaṉi-ēṉceyalaṟṟaṉi?
---------
12. ikuḷi - tōḻiyē!
14-9. muṉāatu - paḻiyatākiya, vāṉ puku taliya kuṉṟat tuk kavāaṉ-vāṉil ōṅkiya
ucciyiṉiyuṭiya maliyiṉ cāraṟ kaṇṇē, peruṅki eṇkiṉ pēḻvāy ēṟṟi - periya
kiyiṉiyum piḷanta vāyiṉiyumuṭiya āṇ karaṭi, iruḷ tuṇintaṉṉa kuvavu mayirkkuruḷi -
iruḷit tuṇittu vittāṟ pōṉṟa tiraṇṭa mayiriṉiyuṭiya kuṭṭiyuṭaṉum, tōlmulip
piṇavoṭu - tiraṅkiya muliyiṉiyuṭiya peṇ karaṭiyuṭaṉum, tiḷikkum -
makiḻntirukkum, vēṉil nīṭiya curaṉ iṟantōr-vemmimikka pāli nilattik kaṭantu ceṉṟa
nam talivar;
12-4. cōḻar veṇnel vippiṉ nalnāṭu peṟiṉum - cōḻa maṉṉaratu veṇṇel viḷiyum
ūrkaḷiyuṭaya nalla nāṭṭiṉiyē peṟuvatāyiṉum, āṇṭu amintuuṟinar allar-aṅkē
maṉamuvantu taṅkiviṭuvārallar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! amma: ‘peruṅkaṭal uvavuk kiḷarn tāṅku alarum maṉṟu paṭṭaṉṟu;
aṉṉiyum veyya uyirkkum’ eṉṟu evaṉ kiyaṟṟaṉi; ikuḷi! curaṉiṟantōr, cōḻar nalnāṭu
peṟiṉum, āṇṭu amintu uṟinar allar.
(vi - ri.) muttamoṭu valampuri corintu eṉṟamiyāl, avaṟṟip peṟutaṟkiṭaṉākiya
koṟkit tuṟiyiṉciṟappukkūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu. paḻiyar-neytal nilamākkaḷ: kaḷ viṟpavar.
paḻiyar makaḷir uvānāḷilē muttiyum valampuriyiyuñ corintu kaṭaṟṟeyvattip
paravuvāreṉka. uvā nāḷiṟ kaṭal poṅki eḻumā kaliṉ peruṅkaṭal uvavuk kiḷirntāṅku
eṉṟār. uvavu:kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉi keṭṭu ukaram peṟṟu muṭintatu. alarum,
aṉṉiyum eccavummikaḷ. cōḻaratu nalnāṭu pōlum poruḷi āṇṭup peṟuvatāyiṉum eṉpatu
karuttākakkoḷka. eṇkiṉ ēṟṟi kuruḷiyoṭum piṇavoṭum tiḷikkum eṉṟatu, aṉpuṟu
takuna vākiya avaṟṟik kaṇṭu, talivar virivil mīḷvar eṉṟu kuṟittu vāṟākum.
202. kuṟiñci
[iravuk kuṟikkaṇ vantu nīṅkum talimakaṟkut tōḻi colli varivu kaṭāyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-8. vayaṅku veḷ aruviya kuṉṟattu kavāṉ-viḷaṅkumveḷḷiya
aruviyiṉiyuṭiya maliyiṉ cāraṟkaṇṇē,kayam tali maṭappiṭi iṉaṉ ēm ārppa -
melliyataliyiṉiyuṭiya iḷiya piṭi iṉattuṭaṉ iṉpamaṭiya, pulipaki veṉṟa puṇkūr
yāṉi-puliyākiya pakiyiveṉṟapuṇ mikka āṇ yāṉi, kal akac cilampil kieṭuttu
uyirppiṉ - kaṟkaḷi iṭattē koṇṭapakka maliyil kiyi yuyarttipperumūcceṟitalāl,
naliṇar vēṅki naṟuvī - vēṅkimarattiṉatu nalla kottukkaḷiluḷḷa naṟumaṇamuṭiyapūkkaḷ,
kollaṉ kuruku ūtu mitiulippitirvil poṅki - kollaṉ kuruku ūtumitiulip
pitirvil poṅki - kollaṉturuttiyi mitittu ūtum uliyiṟ pitirntueḻum tīppoṟi
pōlap poṅki eḻuntu, ciṟupalmiṉmiṉi pōla-ciṟiya palavāya miṉmiṉippūccikaḷip pōla,
palavuṭaṉ maṇi niṟa irumputaltāvum-palavum oruṅkē nīlamaṇiyiṉ niṟattiṉiotta periya
putaril paravi viḻum,nāṭa-nāṭṭiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
9-15. pāṉāḷ - naṭuiravil, araviṉ utti pi tali tumiya - pāmpiṉatupuḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya
paṭam poruntiya tali tuṇi paṭa, uravuurumu uraṟam uṭkuvaru naṉantali - valiya
iṭiyāṉatumuḻaṅkum accam varum akaṉṟa iṭattē, tavirvuil uḷḷamoṭu eḵku tuṇiyāka -
ūkkamkuṉṟutalillāta uḷḷattuṭaṉ vēlē tuṇiyāka, kaṉiiruḷ paranta kalatar ciṟuneṟi -
ceṟinta iruḷparaviya
kaṟkaḷiyuṭiya vaḻiyākiyoṭuṅkiya neṟiyil,tērātuvarūum niṉvayiṉ - ētatti ārāyātu
varumniṉṉiṭattu, ār añar arum
-----------
(pāṭam) 1. āvūrkiḻār maḷḷaṉākaṉār.
---------
paṭar nīntuvōr -poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpamākiya ariya niṉiviṉinīntuvōr, yāmē aṉṟiyum
uḷarkol -emmiyaṉṟip piṟarum uḷarō?
(muṭipu) nāṭa! ciṟuneṟi tōratu varum niṉvayiṉ, ārañararumpaṭar nīntuvōr yāmē
aṉṟiyum uḷarō?yāṉiuyirppiṉ vēṅki naṟuvī poṅki miṉmiṉipōlap putal tāvum nāṭa
eṉka.
(vi - ri.) uyirppiṉ - uyirttalāl. pitirvu -pitirkiṉṟa tīppoṟi. paṭar-tuyar
niṉivu.nīntuvōr
eṉṟataṉāl niṉivākiya veḷḷameṉpatu peṟṟām. nī iravuk kuṟikkaṇ varutalālyām
tuṉpuṟutalpōlat tuṉpuṟuvār piṟar ilareṉpāḷ, nīntuvōr yāmē yaṉṟiyumuḷarkol
eṉṟāḷ; ataṉāl, ematu tuṉpam nīṅkavirivil varintu koḷka eṉa varivu kaṭāyiṉāḷām.
(u - ṟi.) pulippaki veṉṟa yāṉi yuyirppiṉ vēṅkivīmiṉmiṉipōlap putal tāvum
eṉṟatu, talivaṉiṭiyīṭṭiṉik kaṭantu iravuk kuṟikkaṇ varutalālūreṅkum alar eḻukiṉṟata
eṉṟapaṭi.
203. pāli
[makaṭ pōkkiya 1tāycolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-5. uvakkunaḷ āyiṉum uṭalukaḷ āyiṉum-uvappuaṭivāḷ āyiṉum
māṟupaṭuvāḷ āyiṉum, yāyaṟintu uṇara eṉṉār-tāy āyntuuṇarka eṉṟu karutārāy, alar
viṉi mēval ampalpeṇṭir-koṭiya vāyāl alar kūṟuntoḻiliyēviruputaluṭiya puṟaṅkūṟum
peṇṭirkaḷ, niṉmakaḷiṉṉaḷiṉiyaḷ eṉa-niṉ makaḷ iṉṉa taṉmiyaḷiṉiya taṉmiyaḷ eṉṟu, pal nāḷ
eṉakku vantuurippavum - pala nāḷum eṉpāl vantu kūṟavum;
5-7. nāṇuvaḷ ivaḷ eṉa- ivaḷ nāṇuvāḷ eṉṟu eṇṇi; taṉakku urippuaṟiyēṉ - avaḷukku
urittal ceyyēṉāy,naṉikarantu uṟiyum-mikavum maṟittu oḻukum,yāṉiv vaṟumaṉi oḻiya -
yāṉ inta vaṟiya maṉiyiloḻintiṭa;
7-11. tāṉ - eṉmakaḷ, aṉṉi aṟiyiṉ ivaṇ uṟi vāḻkki - eṉaṉṉi aṟiyiṉ iṅkat
talivaṉuṭaṉ kaḷaviṟkūṭiyuṟiyum vāḻkki, eṉakku eḷitākil eṉa -eṉakku eḷiya toṉṟātal
illi eṉṟueṇṇiṉamiyāl, kaḻaṟkāl miṉ oḷir neṭuvēliḷiyōṉ muṉuṟa-kaḻaliṉit taritta
kāliyummiṉ pēla oḷiviṭum nīṇṭavēliyumuṭiya iḷamipparuvamuṭiya taṉ talivaṉ muṉṉē ēka,
pal mali aru curampōkiya taṉakku-pala mali kaḷiyuṭiya ariya kāṭṭiṉikkaṭantu ceṉṟa
avaḷukku;
11-4. yāṉ aṉṉēṉaṉmi naṉ vāyāka-yāṉ attaṉ miyiṉēṉallāmi mikka uṇmiyātal tōṉṟa,
māṉ atarmayaṅkiya malimutal ciṟu neṟi-vilaṅkukaḷcellumneṟikaḷ piṉṉik
kiṭakkummaliyaṭiyiṟciṟiya neṟikaḷil, veytuiṭiyuṟātumuṉṉar eyti - iṭiyē tīṅku
uṇṭākātavāṟuavarkaṭku muṉṉarē ceṉṟu cērntu, pul eṉ māmali pulampu koḷ cīṟūr-
polivaṟ ṟirukkumperiya maliyiccārnta taṉimikoṇṭa ciṟiyūril, cel viruntu āṟṟi -
varum viruntākēṟṟu avarkaḷi uṇpittu, tuccil irutta -taṅkumaṭattil irukkac ceyya;
17-8. nuṉi kuḻittualamarum nocci - muṉikaḷ taḷirkkap peṟṟuacintiṭum nocci
cūḻnta, maṉikeḻu peṇṭayāṉ ākuka - maṉikkuriya peṇṭāka yāṉ āvēṉāka.
(muṭipu) ampal peṇṭir iṉṉaṉ niṉ makaḷeṉap paṉṉāḷeṉakku vantu urippavum (avaḷ)
taṉakkuurippaṟiyēṉ karantuṟiyum yāṉ vaṟumaṉi oḻiya,iḷiyōṉ muṉṉuṟa aruñcuram pōkiya
taṉakku, yāṉaṉṉēṉ aṉmi vāyāka, ciṟuneṟi eyticcīṟūrc cel viruntāṟṟit
tuccilirutta,maṉikeḻu peṇṭu yāṉākuka.
(vi - ri.) iṉṉaḷ iṉiyaḷ: oru poruḷ kuṟitta coṉṉaṭi.ampaṟ peṇṭiratu koṭumi
kūṟuvāḷ, avar puṟattēurittalaṉṟip piṉṉāḷ eṉpāl vantu,niṉmakaḷ iṉiyaḷeṉa urittār
eṉṟum,ataṉikkēṭṭum taṉ makaḷ nāṇāl varuntāta paṭi,avaḷukku uriyāmiyē yaṉṟi
evvāṟṟālumatupulappaṭā mal tāṉ maṟittalum ceyta arumitōṉṟat, ‘taṉak kurippaṟiyēṉ
nāṇuvaḷ ivaḷeṉanaṉikaran tuṟiyum yāṉ’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taṉmakaḷ nīṅkiya maṉi ciṟitum
poliviṉṟi vaṟuviyamaṉiyākat tōṉṟaliṉ, yāṉ iv vaṟumaṉi yoḻiyeṉṟāḷ. aruñcuram
pōyiṉāḷ; aṅṅaṉam pōkiyataṉakku eṉa vēṟu aṟuttu urikka. avaḷ talivaṉoṭunukarum
iṉpatti uṇarnta nāṉ veṟāmiyē yaṉṟiuvattalum ceytal muḻu uṇmiyātali
ivvāṟaṉṟiveḷippaṭuttal ariteṉpāḷ, ‘yāṉaṉṉēṉaṉmi naṉvāyākac, cīṟūrc
celviruntāṟṟit tuccilirutta maṉikeḻu peṇṭi yāṉākuka’eṉṟāḷ. maṉ: acinili;
oḻiyiciyumām.
204. mulli
[viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉtērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 9.pāka - pākaṉē!
1-4. ulaku uṭaṉniḻaṟṟiya toliyā veṇkuṭi - ulaka muḻutum niḻalceyta keṭāta
veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyiṉiyum,kaṭalpōl tāṉi-kaṭalpōṉṟa cēṉiyiṉiyum, kalimā-cerukkiya
kutiriyiṉiyumuṭiya, vaḻuti - pāṇṭiyaḷ,veṉṟu amar uḻanta viyaṉ peru pācaṟic ceṉṟu
-pōril muyaṉṟu veṉṟa akaṉṟa periya pācaṟikkaṇṇē ceṉṟu, viṉi muṭittaṉam āyiṉ -
viṉiyiveṟṟipeṟa muṭittōm ātalāṉum;
5-8. kār peyaṟkuetiriya kāṇtaku puṟavil-kārkālattiṉ maḻiyiēṟṟukkoṇṭu (taḻitta)
kāṇṭaṟku iṉiyakāṭṭil, kaṇam koḷ vaṇṭiṉ am ciṟittoḻuti - kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāka varum
aḻakiyaciṟiyiṉiyuṭiya vaṇṭiṉ tokuti, maṇam kamaḻa mullimāli ārppa-maṇam vīcum mulli
malaril mālikkālattē āravārikka, poḻutu utukkāṇvantaṉṟu-utō, kār kālam vantu
viṭṭatu(ātalāṉum);
10-4. veṇnelarinar-veṇṇelli arivōr (aṭikkum),maṭivāy taṇṇumi-tōl
maṭaṅkiyavāyiṉiyuṭiyakiṇiyiṉ oli, pal malarp poykipaṭupuḷ oppum-pala malarkaḷiyuṭiya
poykiyilporuntiya paṟavikaḷi ōṭṭum, kāy nelpaṭappi-viḷinta neṟkaḷiyuṭiya
vayalkaḷiyuṭiya,vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭit taṇṭali kamaḻum kūntal-vāṇaṉatuciṟukuṭiyiluḷḷa cōli
eṉa maṇakkum kūntaliyum, oḷtoṭi-oḷi poruntiya vaḷiyaliyum uṭiya, maṭantitōḷ
iṇipeṟa - nam taliviyiṉ tōḷiṇiyimuyaṅkutaṟku;
4. iṉṟē -ippoḻutē;
8-9. niṉ nal viṉineṭu tēr - niṉata nalla toḻiṟ ṟiṟam amintanīṇṭa tēr, val virintu
celka - mika virintucelluvatāka;
(muṭipu) pāka! vaḻuti pācaṟi viṉi muṭittaṉam ātalāṉum, utōpoḻutu vantaṉṟu
(ātalāṉum), maṭanti tōḷiṇi peṟa,neṭuntēr virintu celka.
(vi - ri.) aracaṉatu veṟṟiyik kuṭikku ēṟṟi, koṟṟak kuṭieṉpavākaliṉ toliyā
veṇkaṭi eṉṟār. veṉṟuamar uḻanta-amar uḻantu veṉṟa eṉa māṟuka.muṭittaṉam
āyiṉ-muṭittaṉam ātalāl eṉka. ātalāleṉpataṉip piṉṉuṅkūṭṭi, muṭittaṉam
ātalāṉum,poḻutu vantaṉṟu ātalāṉam eṉa urittuk koḷka.vaṇṭākiya toḻuti eṉka.
utukkāṇ - utō tērval virintu celka eṉṟatu, cellumāṟu celuttukeṉṟapaṭi.
205. pāli
[talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇvēṟupaṭṭa talivi vaṟpuṟukkum tōḻikkuccolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. (tōḻi!), tām - nam talivar, uyir kalantuoṉṟiya toṉṟupaṭu
naṭpil-uyiruṭaṉ kalantuporuntiya pala piṟavikaḷiṉum toṭarntu varumnaṭpiṉāl, ceyirtīr
neñca moṭuceṟintōrpōla-kuṟṟamaṟṟaneñcattālkalantōrpōla, tiyal - peṇṇē,niṉvayiṉ
piriyalam yām eṉa -niṉṉiṭattiṉiṉ ṟum yām eṉṟum piriyēmeṉṟu, poy val uḷḷamoṭu
purivuṇakkūṟi-poymikka uḷḷattāl yām virumpumāṟukūṟi, tuṇivuil koḷkiyarāki -
uṟutiyillātakoḷkiyiṉarāki, iṉi-ippoḻutu, nōymalivaruttamoṭu nutal pacappu
ūra-tuṉpam mikkavaruttattāl namatu neṟṟiyiṟ pacali parakkavum,nāmaḻa-nām aḻavum,
tuṟantaṉarāyiṉum-nammippirintu ceṉṟārāyiṉum;
15-24. maḻi kālaṟcirattu māl iruḷ nīṅki - maḻiyeṉṉappeyyum muṉpaṉip paruvattu
mayakkattiṉit tarumiruḷ nīṅka, nīṭu ami nivanta niḻalpaṭucilampil-nīṇṭa mūṅkil
uyarntaniḻalporuntiya pakka maliyil, kaṭāa yāṉikkavuḷ maruṅku uṟaḻa-matakkaḷiṟṟiṉatu
kavuḷiṉpakkattiṉi yoppa, -m ūrpu iḻitarukāmarceṉṉi-nīr ūrntu iṟaṅkum
aḻakiyuccimaliyil, puli uri vari ataḷ kaṭuppa -puliyiṉ uriyākiya varikaḷiyuṭiya
tōli yoppa,nāḷpū vēṅki naṟumalar utira-maṇanāḷik kāṭṭumpūkkaḷāya vēṅkiyiṉ naṟiya
pūkkaḷ utirntiṭa,kaliciṟantu mēkku eḻu peru ciṉi ēṟi - cerukkumikku
alvēṅkiyiṉ mēṉōkki yeḻunta periyakiḷiyil ēṟi, kaṇa kali - kūṭṭamāya āṇ
kuraṅkukaḷ,kūppiṭūu ukaḷum-tam iṉaṅkaḷik kūppiṭṭuttāvum, kuṉṟakac
ciṟuneṟi-kuṉṟiṭiyēcellumciṟiya neṟiyākiya, kal piṟaṅku -riṭivilaṅkiya-kaṟkaḷ
viḷaṅkiya ariya iṭaṅkaḷkuṟukkiṭṭa, colpeyar tēetta curaṉiṟantōr-moḻi vēṟupaṭaṭa
tēyaṅkaḷiluḷḷapāliyik kaṭantu ceṉṟa avar;
8-14. vāymoḻiniliiya cēṇ viḷaṅku nal ici-uṇmi nilipeṟṟaneṭuntūrattu
viḷaṅkumnalla pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya,vaḷaṅkeḻu kōcar-celvam mikka kōcarkaḷatu,viḷaṅku paṭi
nūṟi - viḷakkamuṟṟa paṭiyiṉi aḻittu,nilamkoḷa veḵkiya polampūṇ kiḷḷi -
avarkaḷnilattik kikkoḷḷa virumpiyapoṟpūṇiyuṭiya cōḻaṉatu, pūviri neṭuṅkaḻināppaṇ -
pūkkaḷ virinta nīṇṭa kaḻiyiṉ naṭuvē,paṭappi - tōṭṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya, perum peyark
kāvirippaṭṭiṉattu aṉṉa - periya pukaḻiyuṭiyakāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉatti yotta, ceḻunakar
-vaḷaviya nam illil, nalviruntu ayarmār - nallaviruntāṟṟataṟku, ēm uṟa - iṉpam
uṇṭāka,viḻuniti eḷitiṉiṉ eytuka-ciṟanta poruḷieḷitiṉil aṭivārāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talivar, ‘tiyal! niṉ vayiṟpiriyalam yām’ eṉakkūṟi, nutal
pacappūra nāmaḻat tuṟantaṉarāyiṉum, coṟpeyar tēetta curaṉiṟantōrāya avar, kāvirip
paṭṭiṉattaṉṉa ceḻunakar nalvirun tayarmār ēmuṟa viḻunitieḷitiṉiṉ
eytuka;cilampilāmūrpu iḻitaru ceṉṉiyil vēṅki malar utira,peruñciṉi ēṟik kaṇakkali
ukaḷum kuṉṟakacciṟuneṟiyāya āriṭi vilaṅkiya curaṉ eṉka.
(vi - ri.) toṉṟupaṭu naṭpu-paḻami poruntiya naṭpu; palapiṟavikaḷiṉum toṭarntu varum
naṭpu eṉṟapaṭi.kaḻināppaṇ paṭap piyiyuṭiya kāvirippaṭṭiṉameṉka. tilla:
viḻivupporuṭṭu. aṟcirattumāliruḷ nīṅki eṉavē piṉpaṉiyum vēṉilumpōntami
peṟṟām. nīṅki-nīṅka eṉattirikka. aruvi nīroḻukum maliyiṉ ceṉṉikkumatañ cintum
yāṉiyiṉ kavuḷ uvamiyākakkūṟappaṭṭatu; 1'malipaṭukaṭāam mātirattu iyampa’ eṉṉum
toṭarum,ataṉ ciṟappāl uttarāṟṟuppaṭi malipaṭukaṭāmeṉap peyar eytiyatum īṇṭu
aṟiyaṟpālaṉa. nāṭpū- putiyapū eṉṟumām.

206. marutam
[vāyil vēṇṭic ceṉṟaviṟalikkut talimakaḷ vāyil maṟuttu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-6. tōḻi-, nal makiḻ - nalla kaḷippuṭaṉ,pēṭippeṇ kāṇṭu-pēṭiyākiya
peṇaṇiṉ uruvampūṇṭu, āṭu ki kaṭuppa - āṭuvāṉatu piṉceṉṟumēlvaḷinta kiyi yoppa,
narakuvap paṇitta tirimaruppu erumi - viḷaṅkutaluṟap perutta muṟukkuṇṭakompiṉiyuṭiya
erumiyiṉ, mayirkkaviṉ koṇṭa mā tōl iru puṟam-mayirāl aḻakupeṟṟakariya tōliṉiyuṭiya
periya mutukil, ciṟutoḻilmakāar ēṟi- kuṟuntoḻilkaḷic ceyyumciṟuvarkaḷ ēṟi,
cēṇōrkku tuṟa kal mantiyiltōṉṟum ūraṉ – cēymiyiluḷḷārkku uruṇṭik kalliṉ mītuḷḷa
mantipōlakkāṇappaṭum ūraṉākiya nam talivaṉ;
7-12. māri īṅki mātaḷir aṉṉa - mārik kālattu īṅkic ceṭiyiṟṟōṉṟum ciṟanta
taḷiriṉi otta, am mā mēṉiāy iḻi makaḷir - aḻakiya māmi
niṟattiṉiyuṭiyamēṉiyiṉiyum āynta aṇiyiṉiyumuṭiya parattiyaratu,āram tāṅkiya alar
muli ākattu - muttumāliyiēntiya paranta muliyiṉiyuṭiya mārpa kattē, ārākkātaloṭu
tār iṭi kuḻiya-amiyāta kātaluṭaṉ pūmāli iṭippaṭṭuk kuḻiya, muḻavu
mukampularāviḻavu uṭi viyaṉ nakar - muḻaviṉ oli ōytalillātaviḻaviṉiyuṭiya periya
maṉiyil, vatuvi mēvalaṉākaliṉ - maṇattiṉip poruntutaluṭiyaṉātaliṉ;
12-16. atu pulantu acceliṉi veṟutta, aṭupōr vēḷir vīri muṉtuṟi -vellum
pōriṉi
valla vēḷirkaṭku uriya vīrieṉṉum iṭattiluḷḷa tuṟiyiṉ muṉṉiṭattē, neṭuveḷ uppiṉ
nirampāk kuppi-nīṇṭukiṭakkum veḷḷi yuppiṉ aḷavilaṭaṅakāta kuviyal, perum peyaṟku
urukiyāṅku-mikka maḻiyāl urukiṉāṟpōla, taṭa mel tōḷ - eṉatu periyamelliya tōḷkaḷ
melintu, tiruntu iaiānekiḻntaṉa - tiruntiya vaḷikaḷ nekiḻappeṟṟaṉa;
1. eṉ eṉappaṭumkol-ivvāṟākavum iv viṟaliyiṉ kūṟṟuyāteṉappaṭum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! ūraṉ vatuvi mēvalaṉ ākaliṉ, meṉ tōḷiḻi nekiḻntaṉa; (ivvāṟākavum
ivviṟaliyiṉkūṟṟu) eṉ eṉappaṭum!
(vi - ri.) pēṭippeṇ - pēṭiyākiyapeṇṇeṉa iru peyaroṭṭu. peṇkoṇṭu -
peṇṇiṉatuuruvu koṇṭu. āṭu ki kaṭuppa eṉṟamiyāṟpiṉceṉṟu mēl vaḷinta maruppu eṉka.
erumiyiṉpuṟattiṟkut tuṟukallum mākaarukku mantiyumuvamam. tār iṭi kuḻiya mēvalaṉ
eṉavum,
atu pulantutōḷ iḻi nekiḻntaṉa eṉavum kūṭṭuka. muḻuvu mukampulāra viḻavuṭi viyaṉakar
vatuvi mēvalaṉ eṉṟamiyāl,avaṉ parattiyar illil vatuvi ayartali avvāravāramē
pulappaṭuttum
eṉṟāḷāyiṟṟu. uppiṉkuppi peyaṟku urukiyāṅku eṉṟa karuttu, 1'kaṭalviḷiyamutam
peyaṟkēṟṟāaṅ kuruki yukutal’ 2'uppiyalpāvi yuṟiyuṟṟatapōla, ukku viṭumeṉṉuyir’
3'uppoy cakaṭam, perum peyaṟaliya vīntāṅku’eṉap palaviṭattum varutal kāṇka.

207. pāli
[makaṭ pōkkiya 4tāycolliyatu.]

(co - ḷ.) 1-7. aṇaṅka uṭi munnīr paranta ceṟuviṉtayvattiyuṭiya kaṭaliṉnīr paraviya uppu
viḷiyumvayalil, uṇaṅku tiṟam peyarnta veṇkalamiḻtam - nīr kāynta taṉmiyāl
vēṟākiyaveḷḷiya uppākiya amiḻtiṉi, kuṭavula maruṅkiṉuymmār - mēṟkut ticiyiluḷḷa
iṭaṅkaḷukkucceluttum poruṭṭu, puḷ ōrttu - nimittampārttu, paṭi amittu eḻunta
peruñcey āṭavar -paṭikkalaṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭepanta periyavīrac ceyalkaḷip puriyum
āṭavar, niriparappoṟiya nari puṟak kaḻuti-aṭukkiya uppuppotiyāya cumiyiṉiyuṭiya
veḷḷiya puṟattiṉiyuṭiyakaḻutikaḷiṉ, kuṟik kuḷampu utitta kalpiṟaḻiyaviṉ-tēynta
kuḷampu utittalālparaṟkaṟkaḷ piṟaḻntu kiṭakkum vaḻiyāya, vemcuram pōḻnta añcuvaru
kavali - koṭiya
pāliyiṉiūṭaṟuttuc cellum accantōṉṟum kavarttaneṟikaḷil;
8-12. miñiṟārkaṭāam karantuviṭu kavuḷa - vaṇṭukaḷ poruntummatam karantoḻinta
kaṉṉattiṉiyuṭiya, veyiltiṉa varuntiya nīṭu maruppu oruttal - veyilnalitalāl varuntiya
nīṇṭa kompiṉiyuṭiyakaḷiṟṟiṉ, piṇar aḻi peruṅki puraṇṭa kūval -caruccari aḻinta
periya ki nīrvēṇṭittuḻāvip pārtta kiṇaṟṟiṉi, aṟaṉ ilāḷaṉtōṇṭa - aṟam illāṉākiya
taṉ talivaṉtōṇṭutalāl ūṟiya, teḷ uvari kuṟikkuṭa mukavi -teḷinta uvariyuṭiyatākiya
kuṟikkuṭamāka mukakkappaṭṭa nīri;
14-17. tēm kalantuaḷiiya tīmpāl ēnti-tēṉip peytu naṉkukalanta iṉiya pāli
ēntikkoṇṭu, kūḻi uḷarntumōḻimi kūṟapum - kūntalik kōti nayamoḻikaḷikkūṟavum,
maṟutta collaḷ āki - maṟutturippāḷāki, veṟutta uḷḷa moṭu uṇṇātōḷ -
veṟuppuṟṟuḷḷattuṭaṉ ataṉi uṇṇātōḷākiya eṉmakaḷ;
12-13. veytuuyirttu - veppattuṭaṉ mūcceṟintu, piṟinutal viyarppa uṇṭaṉaḷ
kol-piṟimati pōṉṟaneṟṟi viyarttiṭap parukiṉaḷō!
(muṭipu) añcuvaru kavaliyil, kūvali aṟaṉilāḷaṉ tōṇṭa,kuṟikkuṭa mukavi nīri,
tēmkalantu aḷiiya tīmpāliēnti uḷarntu mōḻimi kūṟavum uṇṇātōḷ,veytuyirttu nutal
viyarppa uṇṭaṉaḷ kol.
(vi - ri.) kuṭapula maruṅkiṉ uymmār eṉṟamiyāl, uppuviḷintatu kuṇakaṭaṟ ceṟuvil
eṉpatu pōtarum.paṭi-cēṉiyu mām. cey-vīracceyki; mutaṉilittoḻiṟpeyar. kavuḷavākiya
oruttal eṉavum,uvariyākiya mukavi eṉavum iyiyum. mukavi-mukantanīr. taṇkaṇ: kaṇ aci.
1'mīṉkaṇ aṟṟataṉcuṉiyē’ eṉpuḻippōla. talivipāl mikkaaṉpuṭiyaṉāyiṉum tamariṟ
pirittukkoṇṭuceṉṟamiyiṉ aṟaṉilāḷaṉ eṉṟāḷ mōḻimi -tāḻnta moḻi.

----------
